# Awesome Jev [![Awesome](https://awesome.re/badge.svg)](https://awesome.re)

> Discover Jev-powered apps, developer resources, and runnable decision examples.

[Jev](https://docs.typesafe.ai/introduction) is TypeSafe AI's System One model: give it state and typed questions, then use the returned choices, scores, and probabilities in your code. This independent community directory collects applications that use Jev, resources for developers, and small, inspectable workflows to learn from.

**[Explore Jev-powered apps](community/projects/apps/README.md)** · **[Share your app](CONTRIBUTING.md#list-a-jev-powered-app)**

**Try the beta web UI at [JevList](https://jevlist.ai/).** Explore the projects in this directory through a searchable web interface. We're continually improving the experience—take a look and let us know what you think!

## Explore Jev with our skill

Use **Awesome Jev Guide** to understand Jev and explore solutions that fit your needs. Describe your workflow in plain language, and your coding agent will help you choose an example, adapt a project, or build a suitable starter—with guided setup along the way.

**1. Install the skill.** With Node.js 22+ installed, run this in your project directory:

```sh
npx skills add AppitStudio/awesome-jev --skill awesome-jev-guide
```

Select your coding agent when prompted. For Codex, you can add `--agent codex`. No TypeSafe account or API key is needed to explore ideas or run the offline examples.

**2. Open your coding agent in that project and try a prompt.**

New to Jev? Start here:

```text
Use awesome-jev-guide. Explain Jev simply, help me understand what I can
build with it, and walk me through the easiest example. I don't have
an API key yet.
```

Have a workflow in mind? Fill in the brackets:

```text
Use awesome-jev-guide. Today I [describe the work I do manually].
I want to [describe the result]. I use [language, framework, or tools].
Help me find the best starting point in this repo and explain what
needs adapting. Ask me anything essential that's missing.
```

Ready to build?

```text
Use awesome-jev-guide to build a small starter for [my workflow] using
[my stack]. Start with a working offline demo, explain what I should
customize, then guide me through API-key setup and a first live check
when I'm ready.
```

**3. Follow the next step together.** The guide uses current TypeSafe documentation and explains what already works, what needs building, and how to handle uncertain results. When you want live access, it walks you through private key setup—never paste a key into chat.

See the [full onboarding guide](docs/using-the-guide.md) for installation options, demos, and setup details.

## Start here

**[Explore what you can build](docs/explore-use-cases.md)** — choose a real implementation by the result you want, then follow its guide, worked example, and setup path.

- [Introduction](https://docs.typesafe.ai/introduction) - Understand Jev's state-and-questions interface and its three decision primitives.
- [Official quick start](https://docs.typesafe.ai/introduction/quickstart) - Make a first request using Python or HTTP.
- [Run an example locally](docs/getting-started.md) - Try a complete workflow with synthetic mock responses, without an account, an API key, or package installation.
- [Choose a decision pattern](docs/decision-patterns.md) - Match a task to Choice, Score, or Noul and define what happens when the result is uncertain.
- [HTTP API reference](https://docs.typesafe.ai/api) - Check the wire format, authentication, answer fields, and error responses.

## Official SDKs and tools

These resources are maintained by TypeSafe.

- [Agent skill](https://docs.typesafe.ai/agent-skill) - Official docs for giving a coding agent API context and guidance when designing narrow System One / Jev decision questions (Claude Code plugin and skills.sh install paths).
- [JavaScript SDK](https://github.com/typesafe-ai/typesafe-sdk-js) - JavaScript and TypeScript client that infers answer types from the supplied questions.
- [Python SDK](https://github.com/typesafe-ai/typesafe-sdk-python) - Synchronous and asynchronous clients with typed answers and configurable retries.
- [System One Adapter](https://github.com/typesafe-ai/system-one-adapter-python) - Run a similar typed-question interface against other LLM providers for comparisons; those responses do not come from Jev.
- [TypeSafe Agent Skills](https://github.com/typesafe-ai/skills) - Official Claude Code / skills.sh agent skill for designing typed System One / Jev decisions; [docs](https://docs.typesafe.ai/agent-skill).

## Community projects

The [community knowledge base](community/knowledge-base/README.md) turns credited articles into original implementation guides with source links, project connections and stated validation limits.

**[Browse the project directory](community/projects/README.md)** for separate app and tool directories, each with full project pages covering use cases, setup, examples, limitations, and review evidence. The links below still take you directly to the upstream projects.

Checks are tied to reviewed versions; see each page and the [validation scope](docs/validation.md#community-project-checks). Mocked tests and reported live smoke checks do not establish model quality on your workload.

### Apps powered by Jev

- [1 Million Emojis](https://github.com/cwdx/1-million-emojis) - `Open source` · `Free` · `BYOK`. Shared million-emoji canvas where TypeSafe Jev paints alongside humans from stroke context. [Try app](https://chriswijnia.com/lab/emoji) · [Project guide](community/projects/apps/1-million-emojis.md).
- [AI Model Picker](https://github.com/Gavinluo19/ai-model-picker) - `Open source` · `Free`. macOS task-to-model picker that opens the task in your existing AI app; optional TypeSafe Jev classification. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/ai-model-picker.md).
- [AI Plays Pokémon (kotlinds)](https://github.com/kotlinds/ai-plays-pokemon) - `Source available` · `Free source build` · `BYOK`. Kotlin desktop app that puts an AI in front of Pokémon HeartGold on a Nintendo DS emulator: the game RAM is described as JSON, and a Jev backend picks each action from a closed list with per-option probabilities, optionally with an LLM planner in hybrid mode. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/ai-plays-pokemon.md).
- [AI, SI, or Whatever](https://github.com/SjJ1017/ai-si-or-whatever) - `Open source` · `Free source build` · `BYOK`. Chrome extension where TypeSafe Jev can choose AI/SI/alternating/custom webpage terminology per page. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/ai-si-or-whatever.md).
- [AI;DR](https://github.com/duyet/aidr) - `Source available` · `Free`. Ranked daily AI-news site and digest whose importance scoring can use Jev through AnyRouter, with a chat-model rubric as backup. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/aidr.md).
- [Albatross](https://github.com/morganlinton/Albatross) - `Open source` · `Free source build` · `BYOK`. Terminal-first coding harness (bring your own model, key or MCP server) with an experimental Jev Mode that puts TypeSafe's decision model before the main LLM. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/albatross.md).
- [AlgorithVideo (open-source desktop video clipper with optional Jev clip review)](https://github.com/algorithco/algorith-video) - `Open source` · `Free` · `BYOK`. Open-source Electron desktop app that turns podcasts, interviews and streams into captioned short clips rendered on your own computer; an opt-in Jev review step, through OpenRouter's Decisions API, scores candidate moments from transcript excerpts. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/algorithvideo.md).
- [Anki Review-Finder](https://github.com/elias170105/anki-review-finder) - `Open source` · `Free source build` · `BYOK`. Alpha CLI that audits Anki flashcards through AnkiConnect with TypeSafe Jev, flagging structural defects such as answer leaks, missing context, scope problems and binary questions, then writes priority and defect tags back to Anki (never card text) and produces an HTML report. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/anki-review-finder.md).
- [Anti Slop for X (Chrome extension that folds AI-bait and bot replies on X, scored by Jev)](https://github.com/Caph-dev/anti-slop-for-x) - `Open source` · `Free source build` · `BYOK`. Chrome extension for x.com that pre-screens replies with local keywords, then optionally sends a post and its parent to a decision model through your own proxy worker. Jev answers whether it reads like AI engagement bait, which kind of bait it is and how strongly; replies over the threshold are folded with the score and reason shown, and mute or block only happens when you click. Inspired by slop-filter (MIT) but written independently. README in Chinese. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/anti-slop-for-x.md).
- [Aperture (ranked reading feed)](https://github.com/richfairhurst/aperture) - `Open source` · `Free`. Single-user, self-hosted intelligence feed that imports Readwise Reader documents and optional public sources (Reddit, GitHub Trending, YouTube, reports, CVEs), has Jev rate each item for quality, personal utility and actionability, and shows a ranked, explainable reading queue with summaries. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/aperture-reading-feed.md).
- [Aplausômetro](https://github.com/rf-camillo/jev-aplausometro) - `Open source` · `Free source build` · `BYOK`. Paste a social post and watch a simulated audience react in real time with TypeSafe Jev calibrated probabilities. [Try app](https://aplausometro.rafaelcamillo.com.br) · [Project guide](community/projects/apps/jev-aplausometro.md).
- [Apparite (jev2ui)](https://github.com/dglazkov/jev2ui) - `Open source` · `Free source build` · `BYOK`. Local design-mock lab: TypeSafe Jev chooses IA/anatomy; Gemini writes copy; code assembles A2UI-inspired mocks. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/jev2ui.md).
- [arJev](https://github.com/harmoniqs/arjev) - `Open source` · `Free source build` · `BYOK`. Daily arXiv digest engine: lexical front-line plus TypeSafe Jev confidence-gated rerank against an Obsidian vault. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/arjev.md).
- [AskJev (openconstruct)](https://github.com/openconstruct/askjev) - `Open source` · `Free` · `BYOK`. Static single-page yes/no UI: OpenRouter Decisions + TypeSafe Jev Noul; key stays in browser localStorage. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/openconstruct-askjev.md).
- [ASO Workbench (App Store Optimization on your Mac, with Jev keyword clues)](https://github.com/mustafakendiguzel/aso-workbench) - `Source available` · `Free`. Mac app for App Store Optimization that tracks keyword ranks per country, estimates popularity and difficulty with labelled sources, writes and publishes store listings in every language, and measures what each change did; Jev answers narrow keyword questions that feed its popularity estimate. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/aso-workbench.md).
- [Atmosphere (animate a rigged 3D character from a plain-language action, with Jev judging each pose)](https://github.com/JackMerma/atmosphere) - `Open source` · `Free source build`. Web app built for the Devpost Build With AI: Basics hackathon: upload a rigged 3D character, describe an action, and an OpenAI model plans the timing, beats and poses while Jev judges and polishes each pose before the result plays back. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/atmosphere-3d-animation.md).
- [Attention Vaccine (Chrome extension that flags manipulation techniques with Jev)](https://github.com/nevrs/attention-vaccine) - `Open source` · `Free`. Chrome extension that tags posts and articles using manipulation techniques such as rage bait, easy-money pitches, impersonation notices, dehumanizing language and unsupported assertions, without judging viewpoints or fact-checking. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/attention-vaccine.md).
- [Audience Moderator (Android counter for people and raised hands, with an optional Jev round insight)](https://github.com/irvan-putra/jev-moderator) - `Open source` · `Free source build`. Android helper for event moderators: counts people and raised hands on-device (MediaPipe or YOLOv8n-pose), runs timed question rounds, picks a random audience member, and can optionally send round results, or one photo you confirm, to Jev for a short insight and a second count; camera pictures are never saved and there is no face recognition. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/audience-moderator-jev.md).
- [Auto-Oui](https://github.com/jasondupontbizz/auto-oui) - `Open source` · `Free` · `BYOK`. Chrome extension (French UI) that watches a tab you enable and answers "Oui" — or clicks the approve button — when an AI agent on supported sites stops to ask for permission; detection is local rules by default with no network calls, and an optional Jev mode with your TypeSafe key judges ambiguous wording against a probability threshold (0.90 default). The README warns to keep it off for payments, messages, deletions and production actions. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/auto-oui.md).
- [axo-launcher](https://github.com/axolutions/axo-launcher) - `Open source` · `Free source build` · `BYOK`. Minimalist Android launcher where TypeSafe Jev settles ambiguous quick-capture phrases (Portuguese docs). [Project guide](community/projects/apps/axo-launcher.md).
- [Baton (notch app)](https://github.com/jeanlucandre-afk/baton) - `Open source` · `Free source build` · `BYOK`. macOS notch app that alerts you when AI chats finish or need you; TypeSafe Jev decides which. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/baton-notch.md).
- [Beam (Omarchy launcher with optional Jev intent search)](https://github.com/RohitKaushal7/omarchy-beam) - `Open source` · `Free`. One-box launcher plugin for Omarchy that launches apps, runs Omarchy menu actions, calculates and converts inline, and opens web searches and URLs; with an optional Jev key it also finds actions by meaning, so typing screen warmer finds Nightlight. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/omarchy-beam.md).
- [Beat Jev](https://github.com/ojusave/beat-jev) - `Open source` · `Free source build` · `BYOK`. Penalty shootout against TypeSafe Jev on Render Workflows with Python and TypeScript examples. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/beat-jev.md).
- [beebots](https://github.com/imikerussell/beebots) - `Open source` · `Free source build` · `BYOK`. Three OKX paper-trading bees; every trade decision via TypeSafe Jev (OpenAI only for bee design/portraits). Not financial advice. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/beebots.md).
- [Better Email Filter (local Gmail triage with Jev that learns from your corrections)](https://github.com/miguelgarcia/better-email-filter) - `Source available` · `Free source build` · `BYOK`. Local command-line Gmail triage: Jev sorts messages into Spam, Ignore or Important, sends uncertain ones to Review, and the labels you correct in Gmail become examples for future classifications. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/better-email-filter.md).
- [betterx](https://github.com/kalowery/betterx) - `Source available` · `Free source build` · `BYOK`. Chrome/Brave extension that classifies posts and replies on x.com with TypeSafe Jev as you browse — seven questions per post in one ~150–250 ms request (tone, post type, argument quality, racist, contempt for a nationality or immigrants, antisemitic, sexually explicit) — and labels, dims, blurs or hides them by your rules. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/betterx.md).
- [Bilibili noise filter for Firefox (JEV)](https://github.com/Ray4AI/jev-bili-filter) - `Open source` · `Free source build` · `BYOK`. Firefox (and Chrome) port of littlewindy123/jev-bili-filter: toggles for spoilers, trolling, ads and partisan chants, plus your own one-sentence rules, filter Bilibili comments and danmaku with TypeSafe Jev; adds local keywords that work without a key, per-item Canvas danmaku filtering, a block log and usage tracking. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/ray4ai-jev-bili-filter.md).
- [Booksmith (Calibre ebooks to Plex audiobooks, with Jev-planned pauses)](https://github.com/neuralcataccel/booksmith) - `Source available` · `Free source build` · `BYOK`. Early Windows-first app that turns DRM-free Calibre ebooks into chaptered audiobooks narrated locally with Kokoro and imports them into Plex; optional Jev annotation proposes sentence pauses, reviews them in paragraph context and suggests pronunciations for ambiguous words. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/booksmith.md).
- [BOOLS (Breakout played live by Jev in the browser)](https://github.com/neovibrant/BOOLS) - `Open source` · `Free` · `BYOK`. Browser Breakout game where a System One decision model steers the paddle: while the ball falls the page sends its state and one Choice question over eight zones, and the paddle moves to the pick; on-screen stats show latency, accuracy by distance and per-zone probabilities. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/bools.md).
- [Bot Derby (Jev and OpenAI Decisions compete in a paper-trading experiment)](https://github.com/Day-in-the-Country-LLC/bot-derby) - `Open source` · `Free`. Public paper-trading experiment: Ferdi (TypeSafe Jev) and Bodi (OpenAI Decisions) each get $50,000 of simulated cash, the same stock evidence and the same legal action menu for five large US stocks, and a public scoreboard tracks how their portfolios diverge. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/bot-derby.md).
- [buildornot (BUILD, MAYBE or DON'T BUILD verdicts on side-project ideas, judged by Jev)](https://github.com/blakestone-x/buildornot) - `Open source` · `Free source build` · `BYOK`. Command-line tool that judges a side-project idea: it searches GitHub and Hacker News for prior art, has Jev rate pain, reach, feasibility and deal-breakers and how likely each match does the same job, and prints BUILD, MAYBE or DON'T BUILD with the reasons. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/buildornot.md).
- [BUTLR.ai (Windows desktop assistant that operates the focused app, choosing each UI action with Jev)](https://github.com/notyourgiantcoder/BUTLR.ai) - `Open source` · `Free source build` · `BYOK`. Focus any Windows app, press Ctrl+Win and type what you want done. BUTLR.ai reads the app's accessible controls through Windows UI Automation, asks Jev (through Vercel AI Gateway) to pick the next action, then types, clicks and checks the result. Steps such as Submit, Send, Pay, Delete or Install always wait for your approval, password fields and card numbers are never captured, and password managers are blocked. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/butlr-ai.md).
- [B站降噪 · JEV (Bilibili noise filter)](https://github.com/littlewindy123/jev-bili-filter) - `Open source` · `Free` · `BYOK`. Original Chrome/Edge extension (Chinese, with English README) that filters Bilibili comments and plain-text danmaku on the page with TypeSafe Jev: four toggles — spoilers, trolling, ads and fandom flame wars — plus one-sentence custom rules, no blocklists or prompts to maintain. Upstream of the listed Firefox port. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/jev-bili-filter.md).
- [Call Coach](https://github.com/ZeroGold/call-coach-ai) - `Open source` · `Free source build` · `BYOK`. Local live sales-call coach: TypeSafe Jev picks next actions and buying stage from transcript turns (mic or sample call). [Project guide](community/projects/apps/call-coach-ai.md).
- [Call Coach by Darlison (live interview checklist on macOS, ticked by Jev)](https://github.com/ByronDarlison/call-coach) - `Open source` · `Free source build`. Live checklist for interviews and discovery calls: whisper.cpp transcribes both sides on your Mac, Jev ticks off each question once it is answered, tracks which listed question you just asked and flags answers worth a follow-up, and an MCP server lets your AI add follow-ups. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/byrondarlison-call-coach.md).
- [Capture (sgaabdu4)](https://github.com/sgaabdu4/capture) - `Open source` · `Free source build` · `BYOK`. Private Mac voice diary: local Parakeet transcription, TypeSafe Jev sorting, Notion after approval. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/capture.md).
- [CareerSurge (free desktop app that finds job postings and applies for you locally, with Jev only picking among values it already found)](https://github.com/Ben19001/applypulse-public) - `Open source` · `Free source build` · `BYOK`. Local desktop app that searches Greenhouse, Lever and Ashby listings through your own Apify account, shows a batch to approve, and fills and submits each form in a headless Chromium. It does no captcha solving or made-up answers; Jev, if you add a key, only picks among values the app already found (resume parsing, label mapping, question classification), and anything unresolved waits in 'Needs you'. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/careersurge.md).
- [ChatJev by JamesANZ (ask Jev to judge a document or web lookup in plain language)](https://github.com/JamesANZ/ChatJev) - `Open source` · `Free source build`. Web app where you upload a document or ask about the web: a fast LLM turns your question into Jev yes/no, choice or score judgments, can attach web excerpts as evidence, and then restates only Jev's numbers, with a verdict card showing each judgment. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/chatjev-jamesanz.md).
- [ChatJevPT](https://github.com/samkoosh/ChatJevPT) - `Open source` · `Free source build` · `BYOK`. Chat app that makes TypeSafe Jev 'generate' an answer one character at a time by asking the same choice question over and over (next character or END), shows each letter's top-five probabilities, lets Jev grade its own answer, and meters cost; several 'model levels' compare naive and fuller pipelines. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/chatjevpt.md).
- [ChatJump](https://github.com/saydus/chatjump) - `Open source` · `Free source build` · `BYOK`. Menu-bar launcher for your AI chats: press Control-Option-Space, type what you remember, and Jev ranks open Claude, ChatGPT, Codex and Chrome chat tabs by meaning, then opens the right one. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/chatjump.md).
- [CheckMyBrandName (free brand-name checker where rules gather evidence and Jev judges what rules can't)](https://www.checkmybrandname.com/) - `Closed source` · `Free`. Free brand-name screen for spelling, pronunciation, distinctiveness, meanings in six languages and domain availability, with Jev judging what rules can't (site claim); no sign-up, also an MCP server. [Try app](https://www.checkmybrandname.com/) · [Project guide](community/projects/apps/checkmybrandname.md).
- [Cheese Toast](https://github.com/jbum/cheesetoast) - `Open source` · `Free`. Classic teapot-in-space arcade game in the browser; TypeSafe Jev moderates score names and can play as a bot. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/cheese-toast.md).
- [Chess with Jev](https://github.com/vlaier/chessWithJev) - `Source available` · `Free source build` · `BYOK`. Browser chess where your opponent is TypeSafe Jev: every move is a short chain of choice questions (focus, piece, destination) whose options are the legal moves, so illegal moves cannot be expressed; you can read each answer chain and rewrite the questions in an in-app workflow editor. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/chess-with-jev.md).
- [Clasificador de noticias regionales con Jev](https://github.com/EdgarTapara/clasificador-noticias-jev) - `Open source` · `Free source build` · `BYOK`. Spanish-language Windows workflow that turns a monthly multi-portal news Excel into a reviewed list of Arequipa economic news, using Jev to classify headlines before scraping. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/clasificador-noticias-jev.md).
- [Clean Code Review](https://github.com/frostney/clean-code-review) - `Open source` · `Free source build` · `BYOK`. Hosted/source PR reviewer: TypeSafe Jev judges files on Clean Code questions; Luna writes evidence-first prose (MCP included). [Try app](https://clean-code-review.vercel.app) · [Project guide](community/projects/apps/clean-code-review.md).
- [Clef homelab decision engine](https://github.com/NikhileshThiru/clef) - `Open source` · `Free source build`. Self-hosted homelab dashboard where open Clef-flash (GGUF on a 6 GB laptop GPU) answers System One questions over llama.cpp's `/v1/systemone` endpoint — the same request shape as Jev — to triage Gmail, filter news, watch ~2,350 company job boards for internships and push matches to your phone through ntfy. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/clef-homelab.md).
- [Computer Use (lite) by ajokhai (Mac computer use for any AI, with an optional Jev mode)](https://github.com/ajokhai/computer-use-lite) - `Source available` · `Free` · `BYOK`. Menu bar app and native MCP server that let an AI operate the whole Mac, not just a browser: Claude, ChatGPT, Gemini, OpenRouter or a local model plans the steps, and in its Jev modes TypeSafe Jev picks the next action and element from the app's accessibility tree. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/computer-use-lite.md).
- [Conclave (local multi-model council app with Jev routing and answer checks)](https://github.com/btrischetta/conclave) - `Open source` · `Free source build` · `BYOK`. Local Windows app that sends one question to a council of up to 13 AI providers, has a chosen model synthesise their answers blind and an optional model from another company audit the result; optional Jev checks route questions, flag refusals or off-topic answers and test draft claims, in shadow (record-only) mode by default. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/conclave.md).
- [Covan (local-first personal agent desktop app with Jev reply decisions)](https://github.com/Lichas/covan) - `Open source` · `Free source build` · `BYOK`. Local-first personal agent desktop app with memory, tools, workspaces and scheduled tasks, where several bots can chat with you or in groups; an optional Jev strategy decides whether each bot should reply in a group or follow up, with low-confidence answers falling back to the LLM strategy. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/covan.md).
- [CrawlHive](https://github.com/h100envy/crawl-hive) - `Open source` · `Pricing unverified`. Memecoin signal crawler swarm: TypeSafe Jev rates every crawler find in ~0.5 s before safety scans and optional Grok review. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/crawl-hive.md).
- [Crush Monitor](https://github.com/FerryCorleone/crush-monitor) - `Open source` · `Free source build` · `BYOK`. Local WeChat-style chat analyzer: TypeSafe Jev labels emotion/intent, scores affinity, and rates replies (BYOK). [Project guide](community/projects/apps/crush-monitor.md).
- [Crush Monitor with Jev](https://github.com/zhengge6/crush-monitor-with-jev) - `Open source` · `Free source build` · `BYOK`. WeChat affinity analyzer (MIT fork of Crush Monitor) powered by TypeSafe Jev judgments. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/crush-monitor-with-jev.md).
- [CryptoCadence](https://github.com/KarelTestSpecial/crypto-trading-lab) - `Open source` · `Free source build` · `BYOK`. Paper-trading/backtest crypto stack with calibrated TypeSafe Jev decisions, hard risk vetoes, and a tamper-evident journal. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/crypto-cadence.md).
- [Danmaku spoiler filter (JEV)](https://github.com/meetchen/jev-danmaku-filter) - `Open source` · `Free source build` · `BYOK`. Zero-dependency Chrome extension (plus CLI) that has TypeSafe Jev judge whether Bilibili danmaku comments are spoilers and filters the hits, batching comments per request with a local cache; built to add other danmaku sites later. Docs in Chinese and English. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/jev-danmaku-filter.md).
- [DeciFlow](https://github.com/call518/deciflow) - `Open source` · `Free source build` · `BYOK`. Live-stream overlay for old-school programming channels built with React95: eight ready OBS scenes, chat that passes local rules and then Jev before it appears on screen, an LLM fallback when Jev is unsure, a Clippy that answers chat questions and optional text-to-speech. Portuguese-language docs. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/stream95.md).
- [Surge Desk](https://github.com/valensangui8/surge-desk) - `Source available` · `Free` · `BYOK`. Hackathon demo for 911 surges: each call transcript (multilingual, with location) gets six typed TypeSafe Jev questions in one request — same emergency as an open incident?, what the call adds, severity, life threat — so code can merge duplicates, keep new life-safety facts, split hidden emergencies and rank P1–P4, asking the supervisor when Jev is under 70% sure; nothing is dispatched without human approval. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/surge-desk.md).
- [Switchyard](https://github.com/kevinebaugh/switchyard) - `Open source` · `Free source build` · `BYOK`. macOS menu-bar Dia/Chrome profile router: TypeSafe Jev chooses the track, then learns local rules. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/switchyard.md).
- [Synax](https://github.com/coldmint9/Synax) - `Open source` · `Free` · `BYOK`. Open-source local workspace for coding agents and codebase docs (agent chats, files, diffs, terminal) whose Electron app offers optional Jev-assisted Computer Use: with a TypeSafe key (or OpenRouter), Jev picks bounded semantic actions for the app-hosted Cua Driver instead of the default Direct Cua strategy. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/synax.md).
- [Tab (Mac next-action autocomplete)](https://github.com/alaramartin/paradigmhacks) - `Source available` · `Free source build` · `BYOK`. Hackathon proof of concept that predicts your next action on a Mac and shows it as a gray ghost (a highlighted button or gray text); press Tab to do it. Jev picks among code-proposed candidate actions, and x402 paywalls become testnet payments with an amount check. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/tab-mac-next-action.md).
- [Tab Bouncer](https://github.com/MANISH007700/tab-bouncer) - `Open source` · `Free source build` · `BYOK`. Chrome extension that rates open tabs for a typed task with TypeSafe Jev (keep noul + kind choice; batches of 120), then closes mismatches. Distinct from Jev for Chrome. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/tab-bouncer.md).
- [Tab Land](https://github.com/robin-anil/tab-land) - `Open source` · `Free source build` · `BYOK`. Chrome extension that finds any tab fast, groups tabs by the job you are doing, and closes stale ones; with a Jev key, Jev double-checks each group, removes tabs that do not fit, and names groups. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/tab-land.md).
- [TabTidy](https://github.com/123456342g-lang/tabtidy) - `Open source` · `Free source build` · `BYOK`. Chrome MV3 extension that filters tabs locally (active, pinned, audible, downloading stay), then sends one Jev request with a KEEP / CLOSE / UNCERTAIN choice question per remaining tab; it closes only what you confirm (one-click undo through Chrome sessions) and turns your corrections into domain rules that override Jev next time. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/tabtidy.md).
- [Tabwerk](https://github.com/DigitalIQ/tabwerk) - `Open source` · `Free`. German-language Chrome extension for people with many tabs: quick search, groups, sorting, duplicates, page watchers and full history, where exact work is done in code and optional Jev calls answer the semantic questions (find a tab by description, suggest groups, rank priority, judge watcher conditions). [Project guide](community/projects/apps/tabwerk.md).
- [talktojev (Jev Prime)](https://github.com/xucian/talktojev) - `Open source` · `Free` · `BYOK`. Generation-as-classification web chat: TypeSafe Jev chooses every word via OpenRouter Decisions (no LLM). [Try app](https://talktojev.com) · [Project guide](community/projects/apps/talktojev.md).
- [Tally (automatic time, income and evidence tracking for hourly contract work)](https://github.com/0Calories/tally) - `Open source` · `Free source build`. Menu bar app for hourly contractors that records which app, window and tab you are in every few seconds, assigns time to projects by your rules, seals intervals into a hash-chained ledger and bills only explicit client sessions; optionally Jev assigns unmatched windows to projects (at 80% confidence or more) and to activity categories. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/tally-contract-time.md).
- [Tavli](https://github.com/DimisCodes/tavli) - `Open source` · `Free source build` · `BYOK`. Greek backgammon (Portes) played against TypeSafe Jev via OpenRouter, with a typed-decision tutorial. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/tavli.md).
- [Tessmora (MMA-RAG)](https://github.com/Champ-X/MMA-RAG) - `Source available` · `Free source build` · `BYOK`. Tessmora (Chinese docs): self-hosted omni-modal agentic retrieval platform for documents, images, audio and video, with opt-in TypeSafe Jev semantic judgment for a simple-question intent fast path, choice reranking and citation-support diagnostics. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/tessmora.md).
- [Text-Only YouTube (userscript with Jev genre tags)](https://github.com/JohnVonDrashek/youtube-text-only) - `Open source` · `Free`. Violentmonkey userscript that turns YouTube into a plain list of titles without thumbnails, avatars or Shorts; one click sends the visible titles to Jev to tag each video with a genre icon you can filter by. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/text-only-youtube.md).
- [Text-To-VRMA](https://github.com/Kirakun0328/text-to-vrma) - `Open source` · `Free` · `BYOK`. Text-to-motion VRM avatar app (VRMA/GIF/video export) where optional TypeSafe Jev analysis of emotion, gaze and tempo conditions ARDY/LLM motion generation. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/text-to-vrma.md).
- [tg-crush](https://github.com/BrickerP/tg-crush) - `Open source` · `Free source build` · `BYOK`. Local real-time Telegram coach: TypeSafe Jev scores drafts and partner messages before you send (BYOK). [Project guide](community/projects/apps/tg-crush.md).
- [TG-Spam](https://github.com/umputun/tg-spam) - `Open source` · `Free source build` · `BYOK`. Self-hosted Telegram anti-spam bot and Go library (maintained since 2023); its optional Jev provider asks one typed spam question (plus an optional gibberish question) and thresholds the returned probability. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/tg-spam.md).
- [THE SWARM](https://github.com/machine1986/the-swarm) - `Open source` · `Free source build` · `BYOK`. WebGL swarm of 1,000 light particles whose strategy (flee, attack, protect, split, regroup) is chosen live by TypeSafe Jev every 1.5 seconds or on events, while local rules move each particle. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/the-swarm.md).
- [Tidyward (private file housekeeper for macOS where Jev answers what each file is)](https://github.com/chatpoc-ai/tidyward) - `Open source` · `Free source build` · `BYOK`. Early macOS command-line housekeeper: it identifies files locally first, masks real names before anything leaves the machine, asks Jev only semantic yes/no questions such as "is this an invoice?", and your plain-text rules turn the answers into a keep, archive or Trash plan you confirm; every move can be undone. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/tidyward.md).
- [TipTour](https://github.com/milind-soni/tiptour-macos) - `Open source` · `Free source build` · `BYOK`. macOS menu bar app that uses Jev to select desktop click targets from typed requests, alongside a separate Gemini voice mode. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/tiptour.md).
- [Toru (recording infrastructure for coding agents, with optional Jev browser navigation)](https://github.com/alifoo/toru) - `Open source` · `Commercial` · `Freemium` · `BYOK`. MCP recorder that lets Claude Code, Cursor, Codex and other agents record product demos, changelog GIFs and bug repros in your signed-in Chrome or on your Mac, renders them with your brand and checks each take; optional server-side Jev navigation picks browser operations and targets for a goal. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/toru.md).
- [ToS Decipher (terms-of-service and privacy-policy reviewer whose claims are checked by Jev)](https://github.com/javierBrenesAI/ToS-Defender) - `Open source` · `Free source build` · `BYOK`. English and Spanish web app, Chrome/Edge MV3 extension and Python API that summarise terms of service and privacy policies, highlight important clauses next to their source text and find policy links on a page; Jev classifies links and verifies each finding against the source passages. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/tos-decipher.md).
- [ToS Watch](https://github.com/watthem/tos-watch) - `Open source` · `Free source build` · `BYOK`. Email alerts on material ToS/privacy changes via Open Terms Archive + TypeSafe Jev ([tos.watch](https://tos.watch)). [Project guide](community/projects/apps/tos-watch.md).
- [Transcript Lens](https://github.com/sensahin/transcript-lens) - `Open source` · `Free source build` · `BYOK`. Next.js (Türkçe UI) YouTube transcript explorer: TypeSafe Jev classifies blocks for kind/value/signals without rewriting text. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/transcript-lens.md).
- [Treg ("OpenRouter for agent tools": pay-per-call tool catalog whose tool search is judged by Jev)](https://github.com/superdesigndev/treg) - `Source available` · `Paid` · `Commercial`. Hosted tool registry from Superdesign: agents use one base URL and token to call thousands of priced endpoints (SEO, social, people and company enrichment, ads, scraping, media generation) without provider accounts, plus team keys and skills; tool search sends the query and candidates to Jev in one judge request, and Jev is also offered as a catalog endpoint. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/treg.md).
- [Tube Focus Mode](https://github.com/OriolSegarra/tube-focus-mode) - `Open source` · `Free source build` · `BYOK`. Chrome extension that filters YouTube down to your channels, playlists and chosen topics: Jev scores each other video against your focus topics, and off-topic videos are blurred or blocked. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/tube-focus-mode.md).
- [TypeSafe Fun AdBlocker](https://github.com/realZachi/typesafe-adblock) - `Open source` · `Free source build` · `BYOK`. Experimental Chrome extension that asks Jev whether heuristically selected DOM elements are ads, then removes or highlights matches. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/typesafe-adblock.md).
- [uCable (your YouTube subscriptions as an early-2000s satellite TV guide, with Jev tagging every video)](https://github.com/hiitsmax/ucable) - `Open source` · `Free source build` · `BYOK`. Web app that takes the last 14 days of videos from your YouTube subscriptions and schedules them into numbered themed channels with a two-screen TV guide; type 1-0-5 to change channel. Jev labels each video with its channel theme, while a plain-code scheduler decides order and remembers where you stopped. Italian UI. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/ucable.md).
- [Unclutter](https://github.com/kitze/unclutter) - `Open source` · `Free source build` · `BYOK`. Classifies page clutter with Jev and saves reversible hiding rules for similar pages, with manual analysis by default. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/unclutter.md).
- [undertone](https://github.com/Nuu-maan/undertone) - `Source available` · `Free source build` · `BYOK`. Text box that reads draft tone with TypeSafe Jev before you send (hosted demo + source; no LICENSE file). [Project guide](community/projects/apps/undertone.md).
- [Usher (macOS menu bar app that sizes the focused window by the kind of work, classified by Jev)](https://github.com/jabreeflor/usher) - `Open source` · `Free source build` · `BYOK`. When you switch windows, Usher sends the app name and window title to Jev on OpenRouter, sorts the window into Code, Reference, Media, Writing, Chat or Ambient, gives it that category's share of the screen (two-thirds for code, full for media) and arranges the other windows around it. Shares are adjustable from the menu bar, answers are cached per app and title, and with no network or key it guesses from the app. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/usher.md).
- [UtilityPot](https://github.com/tugrulguner/utilitypot) - `Open source` · `Free`. Free web utilities for messy data, shared text, diagrams and planning: browser-local CSV/JSON conversion, date cleanup and meeting-time finder, plus three bounded Jev tools for custom entity extraction, text-to-Mermaid flowcharts and finding the exact paragraphs on a webpage that answer a question. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/utilitypot.md).
- [Varina](https://github.com/deillusion/Aha-Engine) - `Source available` · `Free source build` · `BYOK`. Chinese-language multi-seat AI design-exploration workbench (local web UI + CLI) where an optional TypeSafe Jev post-gate decides START or ASK — whether a finished answer merits deeper multi-seat exploration — before any extra model spend; Jev never writes answers. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/varina.md).
- [VerdictKit](https://verdictkit.com) - `Closed source` · `Freemium` · `Commercial`. Independent hosted Jev playground: build Choice, Score, or Noul questions over your text or JSON and inspect typed responses. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/verdictkit.md).
- [Vertika Command](https://command.vertika.io) - `Closed source` · `Free` · `BYOK`. Chrome extension that runs your browser by voice or typed commands; free in-browser Laya or TypeSafe Jev online with your own key. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/vertika-command.md).
- [Vibe Check for X](https://github.com/RafalWilinski/vibecheck) - `Source unverified` · `Pricing unverified` · `BYOK`. Chrome extension that uses Jev to score draft X posts and display a verdict, with optional OpenAI media descriptions. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/vibecheck.md).
- [Vicaura](https://vicaura.com) - `Closed source` · `Pricing unverified`. Hosted product-to-markdown tool: extract features, ICP, messaging, and pricing strategy into md for coding agents (vendor: powered by Jev). [Project guide](community/projects/apps/vicaura.md).
- [voice-browser](https://github.com/ken-code-wq/voice-browser) - `Open source` · `Free source build` · `BYOK`. macOS command bar that drives Chromium/Dia/Arc/Chrome from plain English with TypeSafe Jev (or SubQ Snap) picking clicks and targets. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/voice-browser.md).
- [Vonage Video API x Jev support demo](https://github.com/Vonage-Community/demo-video-javascript-jev-starter) - `Open source` · `Free source build` · `BYOK`. Vonage Community demo where Jev reads a customer's chat messages for sentiment and frustration and escalates to a live Vonage video call with a human agent. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/vonage-video-jev-starter.md).
- [Warren Duffer](https://github.com/arimanyus/warrenduffer) - `Open source` · `Free source build` · `BYOK`. Intraday Nifty-50 trading bot where TypeSafe Jev ranks names and code sizes/stops live broker orders (experimental; real-money risk). [Project guide](community/projects/apps/warrenduffer.md).
- [Watermelon](https://github.com/shashwatc12/watermelon) - `Open source` · `Free source build` · `BYOK`. Status-update honesty auditor: TypeSafe Jev judges language while code parses slip signals; live demo available. [Try app](https://watermelon.shashwatchavan.com) · [Project guide](community/projects/apps/watermelon.md).
- [X Semantic Filter (Chrome extension that folds low-value X posts by your plain-language rules, with Jev)](https://github.com/Nixz0824/x-semantic-filter) - `Open source` · `Free source build` · `BYOK`. Chrome extension that folds posts on X in real time: describe what you don't want in plain language, every post gets a probability per rule, and anything above your threshold collapses into a one-line bar you can expand. Backends are Jev, Cloudflare's Clef-omni (which also reads images) or an OpenAI-compatible model; bilingual Chinese and English UI, no backend of its own. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/x-semantic-filter.md).
- [Xift](https://github.com/asaidaoui-netria/xift) - `Open source` · `Free source build` · `BYOK`. Firefox add-on that collapses noise on your X timeline: TypeSafe Jev (via OpenRouter) scores each post for engagement bait, rage bait, low substance, and self-promotion, and posts over your thresholds fold to a one-line reason. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/xift.md).
- [XPostFilterPlus (Chrome extension that hides X posts by keyword or by Jev classification)](https://github.com/doraTeX/XPostFilterPlus) - `Open source` · `Free source build`. Unofficial Chrome extension that hides X posts using keyword rules with case, accent and symbol normalisation, plus optional semantic exclusion conditions judged by Jev, OpenAI Decisions or Cloudflare Clef with your own key; settings open over the X page and a chip counts hidden posts; Japanese and English documentation. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/xpostfilterplus.md).
- [Xtags](https://github.com/manifoldor/xtags) - `Open source` · `Free source build` · `BYOK`. Chrome extension/userscript that tags X posts with TypeSafe Jev intent and risk signals for personal local browsing. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/xtags.md).
- [YapSketch](https://github.com/farouqaldori/yapsketch) - `Open source` · `Free source build` · `BYOK`. macOS app for explaining bugs by talking and pointing: record your screen and voice, and Jev splits what you said into points, matches each to what you showed, and picks the clearest screenshot, then puts numbered text plus images on your clipboard for a coding agent. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/yapsketch.md).
- [YouTube Jev Filter (Chrome extension that hides clickbait, hype and Shorts on YouTube using Jev)](https://github.com/Vlad0n1m/youtube-jev-filter) - `Open source` · `Free source build` · `BYOK`. Manifest V3 Chrome extension: for each video card it sends the title, channel, length and description to Jev with three questions (clickbait yes/no, hype yes/no, substance score 0-3) and hides or dims the video when a threshold is crossed, with soft, normal and strict modes. Shorts are removed. Answers are cached per video ID; the README estimates about 3 cents per 1,000 videos. README in Russian. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/youtube-jev-filter.md).
- [YouTube Sponsor Skip (Jev)](https://github.com/AnatoliyLikarchuk/youtube-sponsor-skip) - `Open source` · `Free` · `BYOK`. Chrome/Safari extension that finds spoken sponsor segments in Russian YouTube videos via TypeSafe Jev on subtitles and skips them. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/youtube-sponsor-skip.md).
- [Zearch](https://github.com/zearchcomputer/zearch) - `Open source` · `Free source build` · `BYOK`. Perplexity-style open-source UI for TypeSafe Jev: search to evidence state to typed decisions. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/zearch.md).
- [Zéro Ping (local inbox for lead developers ranked with Jev)](https://github.com/Alexandredc/zero-ping) - `Open source` · `Free source build`. Local inbox that replaces GitHub notification emails with a ranked list of reviews, replies, PRs and sprint items; Jev ranks lists and gives each PR KPI cards such as production risk, review effort and merge readiness. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/zero-ping.md).
Applications with a user-facing workflow powered in part or entirely by Jev. The [full app directory](community/projects/apps/README.md) shows platforms, access requirements, and Jev's specific role. [Tags](community/APP_TAGS.md) distinguish source access from pricing; commercial and closed-source apps can qualify. Makers are welcome to [submit their own apps](CONTRIBUTING.md#list-a-jev-powered-app).

### Developer projects and integrations

- [2048 with Jev](https://github.com/abarslan04/2048-with-jev) - 2048 experiment where a bot chooses moves via the TypeSafe SDK (`typesafe-sdk`, pinned) using a fixed Jev model id so runs do not silently follow `jev-latest` alias moves. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/2048-with-jev.md).
- [2048: Jev vs Laya](https://github.com/Devikrishna545/2048GameDecision) - Experiment where two System One decision models — hosted TypeSafe Jev and open-weight Laya (plus a LoRA fine-tune) — play 2048 on the same seeds against random and greedy baselines, each move asking a `move` choice and a `danger` yes/no from a board description, with every move logged and reports comparing win likelihood, choices and latency. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/2048-jev-vs-laya.md).
- [8-K Filings and Decision Models](https://github.com/magilliam27/sec-8k-filings-and-decision-models) - Jev rated 26,962 S&P 500 8-K filings (2013 to 2026) from very bad to very good for $2.32; within item group and year the rating's rank correlation with the next two days' market-adjusted return is +0.025, positive in 13 of 14 years, and the move is mostly gone by the next open. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/sec-8k-decision-models.md).
- [@amatelic/jev](https://github.com/amatelic/jev) - Node library and local server for one-line choice/noul/score decisions from small open models, with no API key or cloud call. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/amatelic-jev.md).
- [@contexera DSH jev plugin](https://github.com/Contexera/dsh-plugins) - DeepSeek Harness plugin monorepo whose `jev` package adds a TypeSafe Jev service for typed decisions alongside channel-gateway and context-continuity plugins. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/contexera-dsh-jev.md).
- [@rbxts/jev (Jev for roblox-ts)](https://github.com/Lypt1x/rbxts-jev) - Typed TypeSafe Jev client for roblox-ts games: declare choice, score and noul questions about one state, and the option names become TypeScript types your server code branches on, with a shared rate limiter, retries and request deduplication over Roblox `HttpService`. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/rbxts-jev.md).
- [@sideform/browser-agent](https://github.com/dan-rozhkov/browser-agent) - TypeScript goal-driven browser agent library whose step policy uses TypeSafe Jev via a System One client. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/sideform-browser-agent.md).
- [A Decision Model as a World Model](https://github.com/thddydgnl/jev-world-model) - Code, data, run logs and manuscript for a KIIS 2026 Fall Conference paper testing whether a frozen TypeSafe Jev decision model can serve as an LLM agent's world model in TextWorld, against zero-shot and LoRA-tuned Qwen3-4B in decision-style and generative set-ups; the author reports Jev near the oracle on task success without task training. English and Korean docs. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-world-model.md).
- [Abide](https://github.com/coldteadotai/abide) - Enforce AGENTS.md/CLAUDE.md rules on every coding-agent edit/turn with TypeSafe Jev (or Vercel AI Gateway) per-rule probabilities on diffs. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/abide.md).
- [Abstract Sift (screen research abstracts against your interests with Jev, OpenAI Decisions or both)](https://github.com/ValiusSciences/abstract-sift) - Local CLI for screening conference or paper abstracts against a research-interest text with Jev, OpenAI Decisions or both side by side, with agreement and disagreement reports. Concurrent, resumable scoring, cost estimates and a `--max-cost-usd` cap, CSV/JSONL exports and an offline HTML report; an ESMO 2026 run guide is included. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/abstract-sift.md).
- [Accurate and Useless (article and anonymized data from 5,300 Jev calls in a multi-agent coding pipeline)](https://github.com/sash-ua/jev-experiments) - Article, figures and anonymized data from a 16-day trial of Jev inside a multi-agent build pipeline for Claude Code, testing advisory checks, an on-demand tool for agents and binding gates. Jev answered 4,222 calls across five real builds plus 1,092 offline; the authors report that most label sets had a single class, so no check showed reliable judging and the hoped-for token savings were never measured. `recompute.mjs` rebuilds all 103 published numbers from the data. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-experiments-coding-pipeline.md).
- [ACP Bridge](https://github.com/xiwan/acp-bridge) - Bridge that exposes local coding agents over the Agent Client Protocol, with a virtual `auto` agent where TypeSafe Jev picks the best-fit agent per task with a confidence gate. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/acp-bridge.md).
- [adecider](https://github.com/Agents365-ai/adecider) - Typed System One decisions for coding agents: one call, many Noul/Choice/Score questions, pluggable local Laya / TypeSafe Jev / OpenAI-compatible backends. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/adecider.md).
- [adk-go-typesafe](https://github.com/craigh33/adk-go-typesafe) - TypeSafe System One Go client and Google ADK-Go function tool (OpenAPI-generated types; Apache-2.0). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/adk-go-typesafe.md).
- [Advocaat](https://github.com/pithings/advocaat) - TypeScript `ask` client that batches typed Jev choice, score, and yes/no questions about structured data, with optional Vercel AI Gateway support. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/advocaat.md).
- [Agastya (training-free retrieval where Jev walks a document's table of contents and local embeddings rank the leaves)](https://github.com/cars3/agastya) - Retrieval research code: Jev walks a document outline level by level, giving one noul probability per candidate section, and EmbeddingGemma ranks the leaves it keeps. No training or labels; the README reports the maintainer's benchmark against flat Jev ranking and embedding baselines. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/agastya.md).
- [agemem (project memory for coding agents, with Jev screening notes)](https://github.com/illescasDaniel/agemem) - Experimental memory for coding projects: a readable Markdown memory bank, screened SQLite notes and cited source excerpts, exposed to Codex, Cursor or Claude Code over MCP; decision models screen, classify and select memories, with Jev as the default and local QSIV or ollaya models as options. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/agemem.md).
- [Agent Browser (caglarkc)](https://github.com/caglarkc/agent-browser) - Research browser runtime for AI agents: observes pages through Lightpanda DOM controls, lets Jev choose the next action, and verifies outcomes independently, with reproducible benchmarks. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/caglarkc-agent-browser.md).
- [Agent checks (routing, brief lint, plan and review gates)](https://github.com/jacob-mcclelland/agent-checks) - Claude Code hooks and scripts that put Jev judgements around AI coding agents: route each turn on purpose, lint briefs before dispatch, block inline edits to files assigned to a worker, check cited sources in research reports, and ask whether returned work shows a check actually ran. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/agent-checks-jev.md).
- [Agent Chess (Claude vs Jev)](https://github.com/bakirgdev/agent-chess) - Local chess board styled after chess.com where Claude models and Jev play each other, with per-side engine settings, Jev move probabilities in the live view, a per-run spend cap and server-side legality checks. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/agent-chess.md).
- [Agent Router](https://github.com/nidhi-singh02/agent-router) - Quota-aware Herdr launcher that uses TypeSafe System One (Jev) to pick Cursor/Claude Code/Codex/OpenCode model and effort after local eligibility rules. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/agent-router.md).
- [Agent Seek](https://github.com/Gitmaxd/agent-seek) - Cheap web recall for agents: You.com discover plus TypeSafe Jev cascade ranking (REST/MCP/UI); does not write answers. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/agent-seek.md).
- [Agent Stack](https://github.com/korallis/agent-stack) - Local multi-agent software team stack where TypeSafe Jev makes small typed merge/assignment decisions (no root LICENSE at tip). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/agent-stack.md).
- [agent-chaperone](https://github.com/agent-chaperone/agent-chaperone) - Calibrated firewall for agent tool calls: MCP proxy plus hooks adapter; TypeSafe Jev screening with shadow mode and a local judgment log. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/agent-chaperone.md).
- [agent-desktop](https://github.com/lahfir/agent-desktop) - Rust macOS accessibility CLI for desktop computer use; optional jev-desktop skill/scripts use TypeSafe Jev for target/command choice without putting the a11y tree in agent context (BYOK). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/agent-desktop.md).
- [agent-doc-search](https://github.com/beqaux/agent-doc-search) - Docs routing for coding agents: BM25 and a TypeSafe Jev search propose, a Jev yes/no check confirms relevance. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/agent-doc-search.md).
- [agent-evals](https://github.com/marianoberton/agent-evals) - Deterministic LLM-agent eval harness with optional calibrated TypeSafe Jev judge for CI deploy gates. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/agent-evals.md).
- [agent-factory (self-hosted company of AI agent seats that review and land code under scripted gates, with Jev routing)](https://github.com/rodaddy/agent-factory) - Bun/TypeScript service with PostgreSQL that keeps your git repositories moving: agent seats review pull requests, triage issues, dispatch fixed jobs to disposable runner boxes and publish bounded results back to the forge, under hard scripted gates and a daily token budget kill switch. Roles name a capability tier rather than a model, and Jev picks the route. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/agent-factory.md).
- [agent-fastpath](https://github.com/abhishekswe/agent-fastpath) - MCP decision layer for coding agents: deterministic rules then TypeSafe Jev ship/risk/triage/browser tools. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/agent-fastpath.md).
- [agent-on-agent-ids](https://github.com/stanibaj/agent-on-agent-ids) - Intrusion detection for AI agents over Opik traces, with TypeSafe Jev as a supported low-cost judge. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/agent-on-agent-ids.md).
- [agent-reflex](https://github.com/psygreg/agent-reflex) - Coding-agent plugin that uses a System One decision model (Jev, or a local model served by llama.cpp, Ollama or LM Studio) for a shell guardrail, post-edit checks, task complexity classification and a confidence check across OpenCode, Claude Code, Codex, Hermes, Qwen Code, Antigravity and Pi. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/agent-reflex.md).
- [agent-test-pruning](https://github.com/olegpustovit/agent-test-pruning) - Scripts and data for the paper "Which Tests to Keep When a Coding Agent Writes the Test Suite": replays of Claude Code writing tests, planted and real bugs, and pruning signals including four facet questions asked of Jev per test. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/agent-test-pruning.md).
- [agent-tree](https://github.com/s112076649/agent-tree) - Claude Code plugin that only watches: optional bottom HUD lines merged into your status line plus an on-demand `/agent-tree` pane showing the main agent and subagents, and — when you opt in — TypeSafe Jev reading a small session summary to draw four advisory flags (looping, stuck, fan-out wide, near done) and a health score. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/agent-tree.md).
- [Agentic Failure Attribution (what agent traces do and don't tell you, with Jev step judgments)](https://github.com/Sudarsh3301/trace-analysis) - Study on τ-bench banking (20 models, 7,760 conversations, 97 tasks) testing the signals trace-QA tools rely on against real outcomes; Jev judged each step and every user message (17,160 calls), and the finding is that task difficulty predicts failure better than anything in the trace, retrieval matters within a task, and user pushback is not an early warning. Includes a trace dashboard. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/agent-failure-attribution.md).
- [AgentRun](https://github.com/Parcha-ai/agentrun) - Workflow language for the agents you already run: define repeatable steps, let TypeSafe Jev make focused typed checks (`@parcha/agentrun-jev`), and call an agent only when work needs investigation. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/agentrun.md).
- [AgentScope (multi-agent framework with a built-in Jev classifier model)](https://github.com/agentscope-ai/agentscope) - Popular Apache-2.0 Python framework for building multi-agent applications. Since 2026-09 it ships a classifier interface with `JevClassifierModel`, which turns AgentScope questions into TypeSafe typed questions through the TypeSafe SDK, so agents can hand narrow judgments to Jev instead of a chat model. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/agentscope.md).
- [AGIW MCP (careful coding workflow on local models, with consent-gated Jev checks)](https://github.com/louiscalata/agiw-mcp) - Local MCP server for Claude Code, Factory or OpenCode: a readiness check, a Nisi workflow where one local model drafts, tests run and a second reviews, and optional advisory Jev questions before and after a task (risk, missing detail, unbacked claims) that send nothing until you consent. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/agiw-mcp.md).
- [AGNES Arena (Pokémon battles with Jev-assisted decisions)](https://github.com/ZRDONG/AGNES-Arena) - Pokémon Showdown battle arena where two persistent agent players evaluate legal moves through Jev, commit an action and play whole matches, with session management, a scheduler and a browser battle viewer. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/agnes-arena.md).
- [agrouter](https://github.com/SvetlovA/agrouter) - Go CLI that asks Jev which coding-agent CLI, model and reasoning effort (Claude Code, Codex or any configured CLI) a prompt needs, then prints the choice as JSON or launches that CLI with it. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/agrouter.md).
- [agy-cua (background computer use for Antigravity)](https://github.com/saariuslystoned/agy-cua) - Unofficial MCP plugin that gives Google Antigravity (app and `agy` CLI) background computer use on macOS through the open-source Cua Driver, with an optional Jev lane that picks each guarded step in about 0.2 to 0.3 s and refuses secure fields and send or commit actions. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/agy-cua.md).
- [agy-jevgate](https://github.com/catpotd/agy-jevgate) - Fail-closed Antigravity `PreToolUse` hook for shell commands using TypeSafe Jev risk scores. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/agy-jevgate.md).
- [AI PM Job Radar](https://github.com/aakashg/ai-pm-job-radar) - Every open PM role at 36 AI and tech companies, sorted by Jev (via Vercel AI Gateway) into AI PM without ML, AI PM with ML, non-AI PM and not-a-PM-role. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/ai-pm-job-radar.md).
- [aidiff-gate](https://github.com/JohnRiceML/aidiff-gate) - Claude Code plugin: optional TypeSafe Jev judge flags flaky AI-written tests and trims walls of text. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/aidiff-gate.md).
- [Ailuros](https://github.com/0x4272616e646f6e/ailuros) - Ingress decision service for proxies: Envoy (`ext_proc`, `ext_authz`), NGINX `auth_request`, Traefik `ForwardAuth` and Envoy Gateway on Kubernetes send each request to Ailuros, which asks TypeSafe Jev or an OpenAI-compatible model, including local models, for an allow or block decision. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/ailuros.md).
- [aiscript](https://github.com/illescasDaniel/aiscript) - CLI that turns a plain-English intent into a cached Bash or PowerShell script written by an LLM, then checks every generated script with a static scan and optionally Jev (or a local Ollaya model) before it is saved or run; risky scripts need an explicit approval. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/aiscript.md).
- [aisdk/typesafe (native Jev decisions provider for the framework-agnostic PHP AI SDK)](https://github.com/phpaisdk/typesafe) - Provider package for the PHP AI SDK (`aisdk/core`) that runs Jev decisions natively from PHP 8.3+: portable predicates map to TypeSafe `noul`, choices and scores to native criteria, and values, probabilities, confidence, usage and the raw response are kept. Works with any PSR-18 HTTP client. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/php-aisdk-typesafe.md).
- [aitomaton (error-to-fix automation with a Jev decision tier)](https://github.com/sirmmo/aitomaton) - Service that listens to Sentry, urgentry and Alertmanager, analyses each error alone, in bursts and as long-term patterns across a Docker Swarm, and decides what to do through a chain of rules, the open Laya model, Jev and an LLM; code defects can go to a coding agent that opens a pull request. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/aitomaton.md).
- [AIWG](https://github.com/jmagly/aiwg) - Multi-agent workflow framework for existing AI tools whose opt-in decision runtime can bind the same validated decision definitions to Jev or to an LLM subagent, with pins, projection, bounded retries, fallback and receipts. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/aiwg.md).
- [ajevt-browser](https://github.com/XYenon/ajevt-browser) - Bounded TypeSafe Jev browser tool for Pi, OpenCode V2, Amp, and MCP powered by agent-browser (AGPL-3.0). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/ajevt-browser.md).
- [Albanian sentiment analysis with Jev](https://github.com/uran-lajci/albanian-sentiment-analysis-jev) - Reproducible study classifying Albanian COVID-19 Facebook comments (AlbAna, three-annotator labels) with TypeSafe Jev and no training, re-running the 20 classifiers of Kastrati et al. (2021) on the paper's own test split; maintainer reports Jev at 75.71 weighted F1 vs 72.08 for mBERT, with paired-bootstrap intervals. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/albanian-sentiment-jev.md).
- [AlphaOptimizer](https://github.com/alpha-tales/alphaoptimizer) - Compact large Codex/tool outputs locally; optional TypeSafe Jev ranks relevant chunks (BYOK). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/alphaoptimizer.md).
- [Amplifier Jev provider (Microsoft)](https://github.com/microsoft/amplifier-module-provider-jev) - Optional, experimental provider module for Microsoft's Amplifier agent framework that exposes Jev decisions (predicate, choice and score) to consumers such as browser and computer UI use, without ever joining the chat fallback chain. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/amplifier-provider-jev.md).
- [AnchorLint](https://github.com/prantikmedhi/anchorlint) - Audits internal links in built HTML sites with deterministic checks plus optional TypeSafe Jev promise/relevance judgments. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/anchorlint.md).
- [android-jev (FZ2000)](https://github.com/FZ2000/android-jev) - MCP server/skill: drive an Android phone over adb with TypeSafe Jev choosing each next action (distinct from listed jev-android / jevdevice). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/fz2000-android-jev.md).
- [Ankan Core (self-hosted anything tracker with an MCP proxy and optional Jev routing for Telegram messages)](https://github.com/bharatkashyap/ankan-core) - Self-hosted tracker for anything you want to log, with a Vue frontend, a local Convex backend and an MCP proxy, started with Docker Compose. A Telegram bot can add entries from chat; with a Jev key set, incoming Telegram text is classified by Jev before falling back to Google AI. Email one-time-code sign-in through ZeptoMail. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/ankan-core.md).
- [anofox-decide](https://github.com/DataZooDE/anofox-decide) - DuckDB extension: NL predicates and answer sets via TypeSafe Jev or local open System One–compatible models (remote opt-in). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/anofox-decide.md).
- [Antigravity System One (agy-smart: Jev picks which skills the Google Antigravity CLI loads per prompt)](https://github.com/sarayutbit58/antigravity-systemone) - Wrapper for the Google Antigravity CLI (`agy`) that classifies each task with Jev and mounts only the relevant skills before handing off to the main reasoning model, instead of loading 50+ installed skills (3,000-8,000 tokens) into every turn. Includes a local dashboard. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/antigravity-systemone.md).
- [antigravity-approval (fail-closed tool-call guardrail for Antigravity: local rules first, Jev for the ambiguous calls)](https://github.com/zjply4000/antigravity-approval) - `PreToolUse` permission hook for Antigravity: a fast local tier (whitelist, blocklist, network gate, path guard) settles clear commands with no network traffic, ambiguous ones go to Jev, and anything uncertain falls back to asking you. Also hosts the shared `jev_eval` policy core reused by the author's ZCode hook. English and Chinese docs. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/antigravity-approval.md).
- [AnyJev (Nokia Applied Research)](https://github.com/nokia-applied-research/AnyJev) - Turn open LLMs into Jev-style typed decisions with probabilities (L0–L2); independent of official Jev / TypeSafe hosted API. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/nokia-anyjev.md).
- [ApexBench (benchmark for forecasting Apex Legends team placements from replay data, Jev by default)](https://github.com/cyanxiao/apex-bench) - Open benchmark that loads a historical ALGS Apex Legends replay, rebuilds the match state at a chosen time and asks a model to forecast one team's final placement, then compares forecasts with the result. Jev is the default model through OpenRouter's Decisions API; GPT, Claude, Gemini, DeepSeek and other OpenRouter models can be swapped in. Includes terminal playback, caching and export. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/apexbench.md).
- [App Store Indexor (describe an app in plain words and get matching US top-chart apps scored by Jev)](https://github.com/soloiaros/archies-appstore-lookup) - Natural-language lookup over Apple's public App Store charts: describe an app or its look ("habit tracker with streaks", "yellow bird icon"), local text and icon embeddings shortlist up to 120 apps, and one Jev request scores them; apps under 30% are dropped. Every field is marked verified, estimated or unavailable. Runs as a local Next.js site. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/archies-appstore-lookup.md).
- [Apple RAG MCP](https://github.com/BingoWon/apple-rag-mcp) - MCP server for Apple developer documentation and WWDC transcripts: hybrid retrieval, then TypeSafe Jev ranks the merged candidates against the query's API, platform and version needs, falling back to a Qwen3 reranker; hosted with a free tier and paid Pro plan, or self-host the MIT source. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/apple-rag-mcp.md).
- [argmax](https://github.com/WalterGropius/argmax) - Unity swarm-programming RTS where four commanders, including TypeSafe Jev answering typed battlefield questions, emit the same decision type. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/argmax.md).
- [article-quality-gate (build-blocking quality gate for articles with deterministic checks and a Jev judge)](https://github.com/s0yabean/article-quality-gate) - Quality gate for a site's articles: deterministic structural checks plus a 12-question judge, a hash ledger that forces re-scoring after edits, calibration against golden samples, and a build step that blocks publishing below the bar. The judge can be a mock or Jev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/article-quality-gate.md).
- [arXiv Jev Digest (daily arXiv screening with Jev, summaries by email)](https://github.com/Monkeypox966/arxiv-jev-digest) - GitHub Actions job that has Jev judge each new arXiv paper in your field against your research interests, filters and ranks by relevance and verified author institutions, then has DeepSeek write structured summaries and emails the top picks. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/arxiv-jev-digest.md).
- [arxiv-relevance-watch](https://github.com/johnlam1968/arxiv-relevance-watch) - Zero-dependency Node CLI that screens arXiv for one research topic and ranks papers with five typed questions to a System One decision model (Jev via OpenRouter, TypeSafe or a local `/v1/systemone`), with no generative model anywhere and a second stage for reading survivors against your open items. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/arxiv-relevance-watch.md).
- [ask-jev (Aether-254)](https://github.com/Aether-254/ask-jev) - MCP + Codex/Claude plugin for TypeSafe Jev evaluate/batch/ping (Choice/Score/Noul). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/aether-254-ask-jev.md).
- [ask-jev (agent skill and zero-dependency runner for typed Jev decisions in Claude Code, Codex and other agents)](https://github.com/justinramos101/ask-jev) - Agent skill that tells a coding agent when and how to ask Jev for typed selections, labels, yes/no checks and scores instead of prompting and parsing text, with a Node.js runner that has no package dependencies. It can rank files and check claims, and works with TypeSafe, Jev on Cloudflare Workers AI or the Vercel AI Gateway. Includes a trigger-evaluation harness with saved results. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/ask-jev.md).
- [ask-jev (Go CLI and MCP server)](https://github.com/hrubymar10/ask-jev) - Standard-library Go CLI and MCP stdio server for TypeSafe's System One API: ask choice, yes/no (noul) and score questions or a batch, get JSON back, and keep a local outcome log. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/hrubymar10-ask-jev.md).
- [ask-jev-skill](https://github.com/shantanugoel/ask-jev-skill) - Hermes skill that asks TypeSafe Jev for typed Choice/Score/Noul tiebreaks when paths remain plausible. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/ask-jev-skill.md).
- [ask-jev-skill (DiegoSalazar)](https://github.com/DiegoSalazar/ask-jev-skill) - Claude Code skill plus a dependency-free Bun `jev` CLI (`yes`, `pick`, `rate`, `rank`, `triage`) that lets Claude offload cheap, repetitive judgments to TypeSafe Jev — which files to read, whether command output means pass or fail, which skill fits, filtering and ranking — so raw content stays out of Claude's context. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/diegosalazar-ask-jev-skill.md).
- [AskGLP1 (paper code: GLP-1 patient-narrative knowledge graph with Jev post screening)](https://github.com/ZhaoqianXue/AskGLP1) - Code, prompts and evaluation questions for a paper that turns Reddit, WebMD and FAERS data about GLP-1 medications into a source-linked knowledge graph and a KG-grounded question-answering agent; Jev is the screening classifier that keeps posts reporting a drug, a personal experience and clinical detail before GPT extraction. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/askglp1.md).
- [askif](https://github.com/asakaxgit/askif) - TypeScript toolkit for typed branching on Jev answers — `ask.if(…).elseif()`, `ask.switch`, `ask.score` and `.unsure()` bands — with a provider-neutral `Backend` contract, a mock backend for tests, and `@askif/jev` as the TypeSafe Jev bundle. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/askif.md).
- [askjev](https://github.com/pZacca/askjev) - Unofficial MCP server for TypeSafe Jev (local stdio or hosted Worker): agents get calibrated Noul/Choice/Score answers over held context. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/askjev.md).
- [assay](https://github.com/patkusch/assay) - Local open take on System One/Jev: typed answers with calibrated confidence from a small laptop model (stdlib Python). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/assay.md).
- [Astra-Ares](https://github.com/miuuyy/Astra-Ares) - Adaptive GPT-6 Astra reasoning-effort selection during Codex tasks via TypeSafe Jev (experimental patched Codex CLI). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/astra-ares.md).
- [astrbot_plugin_at_gate (AstrBot plugin that merges split messages and silences repeated @ pings, with Jev deciding)](https://github.com/Meru-tea/astrbot_plugin_at_gate) - AstrBot chat-bot plugin for two common group-chat problems: users who send one sentence in several pieces (it waits and merges them, images included) and users who keep @-ing the bot while a long answer is generating (it stops the queued "I'm here" replies). Whether a fragment is finished or a ping needs a reply can be decided by Jev, another configured model, or offline rules. Chinese-language docs. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/astrbot-at-gate.md).
- [astrbot_plugin_jev_gate (AstrBot plugin: Jev decides when a group-chat bot should speak up)](https://github.com/Sodapopper-pixel/astrbot_plugin_jev_gate) - AstrBot plugin that has Jev score each group-chat message and triggers one proactive reply only when it is worth joining in, with persona, questions, thresholds, cooldowns, daily caps and a dry-run audit mode; the reply itself is still written by AstrBot's main LLM. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/astrbot-jev-gate.md).
- [astrbot_plugin_jev_router](https://github.com/maolbsMd/astrbot_plugin_jev_router) - AstrBot plugin: TypeSafe Jev picks the sub-agent (model, persona, tools) for each chat message. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/astrbot-jev-router.md).
- [astrbot_plugin_smart_wake](https://github.com/Mikachiyo/astrbot_plugin_smart_wake) - Chinese AstrBot plugin that lets a group-chat bot join conversations without being @-mentioned: topic keywords trigger a weighted dice roll, then TypeSafe Jev judges reply value × naturalness × heat × energy before waking the bot; Jev also debounces when a conversation ends and confirms 'please be quiet' requests to enter a muted state, with the last 200 decisions in the dashboard. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/astrbot-smart-wake.md).
- [Athena Research (pre-registered experiments on AutoML decisions, including whether Jev can choose explanation methods and search depth)](https://github.com/ercanholasoglu/athena-research) - Evidence repo for articles on LLM-assisted decisions in tabular machine learning: designs, pre-registrations, prompts, code, run records and statistics. The JEV study asks whether Jev can make bounded AutoML choices; richer semantic descriptions lifted one decision from 22.6% to 58.1% on the original benchmark, but on six unseen datasets nothing it added was significant after correction. Other studies compare chain, tree and graph prompting and semantic context with local and hosted LLMs. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/athena-research.md).
- [atoma](https://github.com/atoma-run/atoma) - Agent coordination platform (hosted atoma.run or self-hosted, AGPL-3.0) that turns a request into checked work; TypeSafe Jev takes bounded decisions such as which agent or recipe takes a task and whether a plan is approved. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/atoma.md).
- [AudioJev-Inference (Python library and System One-style HTTP server for the open AudioJev audio decision model)](https://github.com/SihanLv/AudioJev-Inference) - Inference library and FastAPI service for AudioJev, an open audio decision model described in an arXiv paper: give it an audio clip and natural-language questions and it returns candidate probabilities, yes/no judgments or ordinal scores, several questions per request. The server exposes a `POST /v1/systemone` endpoint in the Jev request shape, and a standard-library client is included. Runs locally on an NVIDIA GPU. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/audiojev-inference.md).
- [auth-audit-jev](https://github.com/ianlintner/auth-audit-jev) - Observation-only Python EventPlugin: TypeSafe Jev audits allowlisted OAuth/IAM authn/authz events and emits advisory alerts (never enforces). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/auth-audit-jev.md).
