- [hyalo](https://github.com/ractive/hyalo) - Rust CLI for Markdown knowledgebases (LLM Wiki pattern, frontmatter queries, links) whose `hyalo-tidy` agent skill can, when you explicitly ask, have TypeSafe Jev suggest missing types and existing folders for selected documents — audit-only suggestions the agent checks against local schemas. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/hyalo.md).
- [Hybrid Brain (classroom demo: Jev routes, an LLM answers)](https://github.com/sina-778/system1-demo) - Live classroom demo of System 1 decision models: you type a task and two routers pick which GWDG supercomputer model should answer, Jev (via OpenRouter) or a free local CLM-8B answering typed Choice, Noul and Score questions with probabilities versus an LLM generating its routing decision as JSON, showing decision time, probability bars, cost and whether the JSON was usable; a gatekeeper trusts Jev only above a confidence slider. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/hybrid-brain-system1-demo.md).
- [hybrid-search-rag (measured RAG ablation over near-duplicate docs, with Jev as a reranker)](https://github.com/Guilherme-pxt/hybrid-search-rag) - Small local RAG pipeline (BM25 plus dense vectors fused with RRF, an LLM reranker, cited answers and a deterministic citation checker) measured stage by stage on a synthetic corpus full of near-duplicate documents; one experiment swaps the slow LLM reranker for Jev, asking one yes/no question per passage, and reports quality, latency and cost with confidence intervals. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/hybrid-search-rag-jev-rerank.md).
- [ICDLens](https://github.com/Advaidv121/icdlens) - Python SDK for ICD-10 lookup where TypeSafe Jev scores catalogue candidates for human-reviewable coding suggestions. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/icdlens.md).
- [imajev](https://github.com/mohit67890/imajev) - Open Jev-style multimodal typed-decision models (photos+records+text → options with can't-tell). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/imajev.md).
- [infer (self-hosted inference gateway that routes LLM, Decisions and System One / Jev calls through one place)](https://github.com/addomam/inference-gateway) - Rust gateway for watching and managing inference API calls centrally: native passthrough to providers including TypeSafe's `/v1/systemone`, model resolution for chat, OpenAI Decisions and System One requests, translation between Decisions and System One question types, and support for local Jev-style models on llama.cpp. Configured with a TOML file and separate proxy and control tokens. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/inference-gateway-addomam.md).
- [ING AI Guild Spring AI demos (compliance RAG where Jev screens and reranks passages)](https://github.com/tzolov/ing-guild-meetup-demo) - Two Spring AI demos shown at the ING AI Guild meetup on 27 October 2026, built on use cases ING runs on its GenAI platform: a compliance assistant for new products in a naive RAG and a modular RAG version, and a customer-support agent. In the modular version Jev screens the retrieved passages for injection, contradiction and relevance and reranks them before Gemini answers. Slides included. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/ing-spring-ai-jev-demo.md).
- [InspireJev](https://github.com/q1325833367/inspire-jev) - Browser-automation execution toolkit (Chinese and English docs) where the main agent plans and verifies while TypeSafe Jev picks Playwright actions from candidates actually observed on the page; GPT, Pi and MCP entries, with optional local `/v1/systemone` deciders in the 1.1 RC. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/inspire-jev.md).
- [IntelligentDocumentProcessing (Jev + Haiku)](https://github.com/DanielHuette/IntelligentDocumentProcessing) - Hybrid document processing where TypeSafe Jev decides type and completeness and Claude Haiku extracts, benchmarked on 250 documents. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/intelligent-document-processing.md).
- [Intent-Router](https://github.com/angel291592/Intent-Router) - Agent skill that compiles vague requests into typed IntentSpec contracts (probe/ask/halt) as input for Jev/Laya routers. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/intent-router.md).
- [IntentLock](https://github.com/BrienAustinClayton/intentlock) - Service that uses TypeSafe Jev (`TypeSafeClient`, default `https://api.typesafe.ai`) as the “smart” decision path for intent locking / approval-style checks alongside other LLM lanes. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/intentlock.md).
- [intune-why (the few fixes that clear most Intune noncompliance, with Jev classifying custom settings)](https://github.com/dallascrilley/intune-why) - Reads Microsoft Intune compliance data through read-only Microsoft Graph and builds a fix plan: each step picks the setting whose fix makes the most additional devices compliant, with a what-if view of projected compliance; rules map standard settings, and custom compliance settings that no rule matches can optionally go to Jev for a remediation category. Live demo with a fictional tenant. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/intune-why.md).
- [invalidate](https://github.com/chopratejas/invalidate) - Semantic TTL for agent memory: TypeSafe Jev checks every stored fact against new evidence; code marks superseded memories without rewriting text. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/invalidate.md).
- [IronBee Express](https://github.com/ironbee-ai/ironbee-express) - Goal-driven browser agent: each step is one TypeSafe Jev decision over the page's offered controls plus one IronBee DevTools call that acts and returns the next snapshot, so model output never becomes a selector, coordinate or script; after the run it reviews the final page and API responses (and backend traces with IronBee), and runs can be recorded and replayed without the engine. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/ironbee-express.md).
- [IronBee Gamer](https://github.com/ironbee-ai/ironbee-gamer) - Plays browser games live (2D canvas, Phaser, PixiJS, Cocos, HTML): a perception adapter turns what the page draws into a small JSON state and a decision engine picks every input — hosted TypeSafe Jev with your key, or Laya fine-tuned per game and run locally — with a web UI showing each state, action and probability, a coding-agent trainer for new games, and a shared Hugging Face library. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/ironbee-gamer.md).
- [is-lesion](https://github.com/hemanth/is-lesion) - npm package that evaluates MRI and other medical scan images against Hugging Face dataset labels with Jev 1.14 (Noul, Choice, Score and Box), or local OpenJev vision models, returning a label, probability and bounding box; can score dataset rows directly. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/is-lesion.md).
- [is-malicious](https://github.com/luantak/is-malicious) - Scans source/config/CI files with TypeSafe Jev for deceptive or data-stealing behavior and points to suspicious lines. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/is-malicious.md).
- [J++](https://github.com/Towow-ai/jpp) - Experimental language and Rust runtime for composing semantic Jev questions with exact methods as values; offline fixtures included. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jpp.md).
- [J3v](https://github.com/JGalego/J3v) - Edge-compiled System One decision runtime ('J3v is to Jev as k3s is to k8s') with Rust compiler/firmware demos. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/j3v.md).
- [japanese-jev-lint](https://github.com/pankona/japanese-jev-lint) - Go CLI (`jjl`) that flags Japanese sentences with TypeSafe Jev Noul probabilities (plus local です/ます regex); no rewrite generation. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/japanese-jev-lint.md).
- [Jarvis (Amphion)](https://github.com/AmphionTeam/Jarvis) - Research system and paper ("Coordination Deserves a Brain of Its Own") for full-duplex voice agents: JEV decides each turn (answer, memory, worker, clarify, control, ignore or wait) so you can keep talking while background tasks run. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jarvis-amphion.md).
- [JarvisCore](https://github.com/Prescott-Data/jarviscore-framework) - Python multi-agent runtime (peer-to-peer mesh, durable state, integration atoms) with a `typesafe` extra that injects a shared Jev client as `self.decisions` and can route subagents, task complexity and RAG passages with Jev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jarviscore.md).
- [JCR](https://github.com/NiazMorshed2007/jcr) - Jev Capability Resolver: TypeSafe Jev searches a nested capability tree and returns deterministic command documentation for agents (MCP + harnesses). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jcr.md).
- [jear](https://github.com/iJ03l/jear) - Rust Jev-routed client for NEAR AI Cloud inference and IronClaw agents using budget/quality/sensitivity decisions. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jear.md).
- [Jeeves (PostHog)](https://github.com/PostHog/jeeves) - Open 9B Jev-like reasoning classifier (noul/choice/score) with a Jev-compatible API and training code; independent of TypeSafe-hosted Jev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/posthog-jeeves.md).
- [jeff (Claude Code router)](https://github.com/saembit/jeff) - Claude Code plugin that uses Jev to route every subtask: model tier, Claude or Codex, which skill, write access, and parallelism, then asks Jev to verify each result and escalates misses. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jeff-claude-code.md).
- [Jeff (firelex)](https://github.com/firelex/jeff) - Jeff: an open 0.8B "System 1" decision model (MIT code, Apache-2.0 weights on Hugging Face) that uses the same request format as Jev, with swappable LoRA adapters (guard, triage, tools, grounding, spam…), GGUF files for llama.cpp, and Jeff-Code; independent and not affiliated with TypeSafe. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/firelex-jeff.md).
- [jeff (Viperwow)](https://github.com/Viperwow/jeff) - Single-binary Rust classifier server with an admin page: one `/v1/systemone`-shaped API in front of Jev-compatible models — TypeSafe Jev in the cloud, a local Contrastive-LM (CLM) it installs, or Laya/PipeLLM/any compatible server — with access keys and a playground. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/viperwow-jeff.md).
- [Jeffort](https://github.com/andrewstephenson-v1/Jeffort) - Claude Code plugin: TypeSafe Jev scores each prompt and sets Claude effort without touching the model (MIT). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jeffort.md).
- [jegrep](https://github.com/can1357/jegrep) - Semantic grep CLI: describe code intent and get Jev-ranked matches via TypeSafe or OpenRouter, without an embedding index. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jegrep.md).
- [jes](https://github.com/everafterlabs/jes) - Open-source guardrails for AI agents that ask Jev to judge prompts, retrieved content, tool calls and responses for prompt injection, jailbreaks and secret/PII leaks. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jes.md).
- [jev (drpaneas)](https://github.com/drpaneas/jev) - Go package for calling TypeSafe Jev System One typed questions. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/drpaneas-jev.md).
- [jev (okooo5km)](https://github.com/okooo5km/jev) - Stdlib Python CLI + Agent Skill for TypeSafe Jev `yes`/`pick`/`score` via TypeSafe API or OpenRouter (distinct from typesafe-cli / typesafeai-cli). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/okooo5km-jev.md).
- [jev (polidog)](https://github.com/polidog/jev) - Unofficial Rust CLI for TypeSafe Jev across TypeSafe, Cloudflare, and Vercel providers (CLI shape follows stefafafan/jev)—distinct from feder-cr/jev (jevos). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/polidog-jev.md).
- [jev (stefafafan)](https://github.com/stefafafan/jev) - Unofficial provider-neutral Go/Unix CLI for TypeSafe Jev (Choice/Noul/Score) via TypeSafe, Cloudflare, or Vercel. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/stefafafan-jev.md).
- [jev (taifoon-io)](https://github.com/taifoon-io/jev) - Grade an AI agent job with TypeSafe Jev: fact checks in code, four closed questions, receipt with probabilities, optional on-chain records (`@taifoon/jev`). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/taifoon-io-jev.md).
- [Jev + Claude Code case study](https://github.com/gsaini/jev-claude-code-case-study) - Independent learning project testing what "Jev + Claude Code" means with two working programs: Triage Desk, a support-ticket app asking Jev six typed questions per ticket in one request with low-confidence cases going to human review, and Jev Guard, a Claude Code PreToolUse hook asking five redacted `noul` questions about ambiguous shell commands that never answers `allow`; both have offline evals and keyword baselines. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-claude-code-case-study.md).
- [Jev + Claude email triage guide (step-by-step: set up Jev with Claude Code and build a read-only Gmail follow-up sorter)](https://github.com/byui-library/jev-email-triage) - A guide for people who don't write code: install Claude Code and the TypeSafe skill, try Jev on sample emails, connect Gmail read-only, and get a ranked report of the last two weeks sorted into piles such as Reply needed, Waiting on them, Check and Possible phishing. Includes a guided /setup command, customisation and an honest-testing chapter. Tested on Windows; Mac steps written but untested. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-email-triage-guide.md).
- [Jev 1.14 comparison benchmark (etsabary)](https://github.com/etsabary/jev-1.14-comparison-benchmark) - 1,136 deterministic text decisions across 25 families run on eleven services (Jev 1.14 and 1.13, Luna Decisions, Luna, Mercury, Gemini Flash-Lite, Qwen, Granite and others) with matched diagnostics, latency percentiles and versioned results. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-1-14-comparison-benchmark.md).
- [Jev Account Filter](https://github.com/caiweihan/jev-account-filter) - Screens video-platform accounts for dataset curation with Jev title ranking followed by multimodal account-category decisions. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-account-filter.md).
- [Jev Ad Block](https://github.com/lingengyuan/jev-adblock) - Chrome MV3 ad blocker that combines network rules with Jev judgements of candidate page elements. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-adblock.md).
- [JEV ADK](https://github.com/abyakod/JEV_ADK) - Agent Development Kit patterns for TypeSafe Jev: guardrails, dual-brain routing, and PR triage blueprints (System One decisions in code). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-adk.md).
- [Jev Agent Builder](https://github.com/enzotripoli/jev-agent-builder) - Single-file visual builder for Jev requests: compose Choice, Score and Noul questions with `{{parameters}}`, validate against documented limits, and export the exact `/v1/systemone` JSON, cURL, a TypeScript function or a reusable agent definition. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-agent-builder.md).
- [Jev Agent Control](https://github.com/Krzysztof-Cieslak/jev-agent-control) - OpenCode V2 plugin that uses Jev to route requests between Plan, Build, and custom primary agents, with automatic handoff at execution boundaries. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-agent-control.md).
- [Jev Agent Kit (cloud-f1)](https://github.com/cloud-f1/jev-agent-kit) - Claude Code plugin that prunes long Bash output with local rules plus an optional Jev relevance pass, keeping originals recoverable. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/cloud-f1-jev-agent-kit.md).
- [JEV AgentNetwork](https://github.com/Kelly-Leeee/AgentNetwork-jev) - Event-driven multi-agent network where TypeSafe Jev reassigns pending work whenever any agent finishes. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/agentnetwork-jev.md).
- [Jev Ants (a thousand Jev-steered ants that form the word or picture you give them)](https://github.com/scalefreegan/jev-ants) - Browser simulation where every ant is its own Jev agent: it sees its nine neighbouring cells, how many ants each still wants and its neighbours' headings, and Jev answers which way to move and whether to settle, so about a thousand decisions turn a random scatter into a typed word or uploaded image; an image overseer and spend and time caps are built in. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-ants.md).
- [Jev as an LLM Safety Guardrail (USC study: Jev as input filter and selective output reviewer for a small LLM)](https://github.com/ANRGUSC/llm-safe-jev) - Reproducibility code and data for a USC paper testing Jev as a guardrail around a Qwen2.5-1.5B pipeline on 970 AdvBench and XSTest prompts: the LLM alone, a Jev input gate, and a selective setup that blocks clearly harmful requests, passes clearly safe ones and reviews the answers in between. Reports how many harmful prompts got answers and how many safe ones were refused, with threshold sweeps rebuilt from cached Jev scores. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/llm-safe-jev.md).
- [Jev Behavior for Unity](https://github.com/gbesse/unity-jev-behavior) - Guarded asynchronous decision nodes for Unity Behavior using a server-side DecisionPacks gateway for Jev-style decisions. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/unity-jev-behavior.md).
- [Jev Bench (cocodedk)](https://github.com/cocodedk/jev-bench) - Configurable workbench for repeatable TypeSafe Jev latency and accuracy experiments, with published results at jev-bench.cocode.dk. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/cocodedk-jev-bench.md).
- [Jev bias audit (134,772 paired answers testing how one identity word shifts Jev's decisions)](https://github.com/isaacbitran/jev-bias-audit) - Counterfactual bias audit of `jev-1.13.0` and `jev-1.14.0`: prompts that differ only in one identity sentence (82 identities across nine attributes, 50 names, placebos) over hiring, lending, trust and other templates, with bootstrap intervals, Benjamini-Hochberg correction and a birth-month placebo floor. The author finds explicit labels move decisions by 3 to 15 points while names barely do. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-bias-audit.md).
- [JEV Book Tags](https://github.com/iamjonatha/jev-book-tags) - Calibre plugin for AI-assisted book tagging with TypeSafe Jev (single-book and batch; GPL-3.0-or-later). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-book-tags.md).
- [Jev Browser (openqa-cn)](https://github.com/openqa-cn/jev-browser) - Indexed Playwright automation: TypeSafe Jev chooses control/op; CodexQA skill with replay and HTML reports. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/openqa-jev-browser.md).
- [Jev Browser (tontoko)](https://github.com/tontoko/jev-browser) - Integrates Jev field selection and source-backed extraction with a Playwright SDK, CLI, MCP server, and explicit assertions. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-browser-tontoko.md).
- [Jev Browser (Ying-Kai-Liao)](https://github.com/Ying-Kai-Liao/jev-browser) - Turns caller-defined browser goals into Jev-selected Playwright actions with a library, CLI, and MCP server. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-browser-ying-kai-liao.md).
- [Jev Browser Agent](https://github.com/ali-master/jev-browser-agent) - Bun and TypeScript browser agent with an indexed action space: a typed decision model (Jev, local or cloud) picks the operation and element each step, a chat helper writes a checklist, and a live inspector streams Chromium as it works. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-browser-agent.md).
- [Jev Browser MCP (saurabhgangurde)](https://github.com/saurabhgangurde/jev-browser-mcp) - Local MCP server that lets a coding agent run bounded browser tasks in a dedicated Chrome window: `browser_task` follows a goal through Jev-selected page actions drawn only from the request and live page evidence, with the full Chrome DevTools MCP operation set also exposed. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-browser-mcp-saurabh.md).
- [Jev Browser Skill](https://github.com/zurfyx/jev-browser-skill) - Claude Code/Codex skill where TypeSafe Jev chooses browser operations and targets from a code-built element table (reference implementation; explainer site included). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-browser-skill.md).
- [Jev Bug Hunter](https://github.com/BillNDD/jev-bug-hunter) - Bounded first-pass bug hunting for source files powered by TypeSafe Jev — a quick sanity check, not a replacement for tests or review. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-bug-hunter.md).
- [Jev by Example](https://github.com/ReallyArtificial/jev-by-example) - Ten runnable JavaScript lessons on agent decisions between steps; Jev supplies typed judgments and code owns policy (offline fixtures by default). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-by-example.md).
- [Jev Chromium](https://github.com/kolawong/jev-chromium) - Electron browser agent that collects data across pages: Jev picks each click, focus, navigation or done step from the page's actionable elements, an OpenAI-compatible text model plans, fills and verifies, and results export as a cited table or CSV. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-chromium.md).
- [Jev Classificador (Obsidian note classification with Jev)](https://github.com/matteoandradeboschi/jev-classificador) - Obsidian plugin that sends one request to Jev to pick a field (13 options) and subfield (89 options) for the current note from a described taxonomy in English or Brazilian Portuguese, writes them to frontmatter, and can add 1 to 3 topic tags from a closed wordlist. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-classificador.md).
- [Jev Classification for n8n](https://github.com/khmuhtadin/n8n-nodes-jev-classification) - Adds typed classification, scoring, yes/no checks, and batched questions to self-hosted n8n, with configurable review routing. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-classification-n8n.md).
- [Jev Classifier for Obsidian](https://github.com/ItsBen321/obsidian-jev-classifier) - Obsidian plugin: classify note properties with TypeSafe Jev using an editable guide (one note or whole vault). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/obsidian-jev-classifier.md).
- [jev Claude Code plugin (yinjs)](https://github.com/yinjs/claude-jev) - Claude Code plugin that hands small frequent decisions to Jev at six points — permission requests, prompt-injection warnings on fetched content, a 'done' re-check, prompt nudges, and jev-route effort/model routing — each one ~1 s call that changes nothing when Jev is unsure; Jev via OpenRouter (default), TypeSafe or Vercel AI Gateway. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/yinjs-claude-jev.md).
- [Jev Claude Code Starter Kit (Jev setup, tests and templates for sales systems)](https://github.com/onittindwani-ai/jev-claude-code-starter-kit) - Starter kit for adding Jev to an AI sales or support system with Claude Code or Codex: setup guide and prompts, a tested Python client, a 300-reply test set with scoreboard and cost calculator, ten decision templates, a Claude Code skill and five worked examples, all runnable offline against a fake Jev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-claude-code-starter-kit.md).
- [jev CLI (vilinski)](https://github.com/vilinski/jev) - Rust CLI that sends typed questions to Jev and other System One providers and prints JSON answers. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/vilinski-jev-cli.md).
- [Jev Code Reviewer (egma-ai)](https://github.com/egma-ai/jev-code-reviewer) - Local PR triage: TypeSafe Jev prioritizes changes; OpenAI explains; Chrome overlay posts nothing to GitHub. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/egma-ai-jev-code-reviewer.md).
- [JEV Codex Pilot](https://github.com/Charlyhno-eng/jev-codex-pilot) - Local Codex Kanban workspace with Jev-model routing, contextual compaction, and ticket lifecycle control. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-codex-pilot.md).
- [Jev Consensus Gate](https://github.com/kallurayaankit/jev-consensus-gate) - Agent action gate that asks Jev about each proposed action three independent ways (safety, legitimacy, adversarial) and only allows it when all three agree; any block wins and any escalation goes to a human. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-consensus-gate.md).
- [Jev Content Moderation](https://github.com/kallurayaankit/jev-content-mod) - Content moderation demo with asymmetric approve / shadow / remove thresholds on Jev probabilities, so irreversible removal needs more certainty than approval. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-content-mod.md).
- [Jev Cookbook (Datawhale)](https://github.com/datawhalechina/jev-cookbook) - Chinese System One/Jev cookbook: notebooks, 18 recipes, docs translation (CC BY-NC-SA 4.0 — free educational / non-commercial; not OSI open source). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-cookbook.md).
- [Jev custom connector for Power Platform (Power Automate and Copilot Studio)](https://github.com/markus-tobler/model-one-connectors) - Open-source Power Platform custom connectors for TypeSafe Jev and Microsoft-Decision-1: define yes/no, choice and score questions in the designer and use the typed answers as dynamic content in Power Automate flows and Copilot Studio agents. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/model-one-connectors.md).
- [JEV Damocles for AstrBot (group-chat plugin where Jev decides whether the bot should speak)](https://github.com/ch7flyer/astrbot_plugin_jev_damocles) - AstrBot plugin (Chinese) that hands the 'should the bot reply to this group message?' decision to a Jev / System One decision model plus an explicit group-chat state machine: messages are normalized, grouped into topic threads and merge windows, then asked in one multi-question call and thresholded before the LLM is allowed to answer. Distinct from the AizenvoltPrime Damocles listing. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-damocles-astrbot.md).
- [Jev Decides, Claude Writes (Claude Code hook that delegates each message to Haiku, Sonnet, Opus or Fable)](https://github.com/TechPreacher/jev_router) - Claude Code `UserPromptSubmit` hook: Jev answers one Choice question about each message (the smallest model that can do the job) and the main session delegates to one of four helper agents; short follow-ups, low-confidence picks, slow answers and messages that look like they contain a secret stay with the main session. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-decides-claude-writes.md).
- [Jev Decision](https://github.com/Coding-Dev-Tools/jev-decision) - Portable, budgeted Jev client for coding-agent harnesses (Claude Code, Codex, Cursor, Gemini CLI, OpenCode, any MCP or shell client) with a shared daily budget ledger, explicit `unavailable` results and harness recipes; advice, never authority. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-decision.md).
- [Jev Decision Gateway (kartikanand73)](https://github.com/kartikanand73/jev-decision-gateway) - Governed withdrawal PoC: TypeSafe Jev scores, deterministic policy signs, executor only accepts signed decisions; bench vs GPT-6 Sol and rules — distinct from kuldeepsinh19/jev-decision-gateway. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/kartikanand73-jev-decision-gateway.md).
- [JEV Decision Hub](https://github.com/gbonnet65923/jev-decision-hub) - Zero-dependency Python TypeSafe Jev client with key-pool rotation, a small web dashboard, and ready examples (moderation, routing, trades). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-decision-hub.md).
- [Jev decision-model benchmarks (vilinski)](https://github.com/vilinski/jev-benchmarks) - Reproducible decision-model benchmark: a hand-labelled own set (60 tasks x3) and a 1,100-item public sample, run against Jev and other System One routes, with raw results, pricing snapshots and generators for a published report and interactive model explorer. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/vilinski-jev-benchmarks.md).
- [Jev Decisions Plugin for Hermes](https://github.com/bojansandhaus/jev-decisions-hermes) - Hermes (and other agent) plugin adding Jev-backed tool-risk reviews, approval recommendations, source checks, and task-completion checks. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-decisions-hermes.md).
- [Jev Deep Research](https://github.com/sunyasheng/JevDeepResearch) - Research agent pattern: GPT plans searches; TypeSafe Jev judges document regions in parallel and returns original evidence passages. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevdeepresearch.md).
- [Jev DevTools (React inspector for Jev requests)](https://github.com/diegoag64/jev-devtools) - Drop-in React inspector for TypeSafe Jev, inspired by TanStack Query Devtools: a drawer that records your app's Jev requests and shows animated flows from context to questions to answers, choice probability bars, score and noul values, request history, retries and raw payloads. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-devtools.md).
- [Jev Engineering Cookbook](https://github.com/Jev-Engineering/cookbook) - Curriculum of Jupyter notebook recipes for building with Jev one bounded decision at a time — state in, typed `choice` / `noul` / `score` questions out — with offline fixture runs that need no API key, a live mode, and explicit provenance rules separating synthetic pipeline checks from measured Jev results. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-engineering-cookbook.md).
- [Jev evidence triage (screens PubMed abstracts for a systematic review with Jev, rules and confidence gates)](https://github.com/anondo1969/jev-evidence-triage) - Demo pipeline that screens life-science abstracts for one systematic-review question (can faecal microbiota transplantation or a live bacterial product improve immune checkpoint inhibitor outcomes in cancer?). Plain code rules run first, Jev answers six questions per abstract in one call, and code confidence gates route each record to include, exclude or human review; an optional LLM pre-read only helps the human queue. Includes 24 fictional sample abstracts with planted traps, gold labels, evaluation with threshold sweeps and an offline mock. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-evidence-triage.md).
- [Jev Filter (Apixly)](https://github.com/apixly-ai/jev-filter) - Semantic filtering for AI agent tools: npm package `@apixly/jev-filter` (CLI, Python library, MCP server) turns large tool outputs, retrieved records, code and logs into compact typed TypeSafe Jev decisions with source IDs, local originals and receipts before they enter an agent's context; batched requests and maintainer-run A/B benchmarks. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/apixly-jev-filter.md).
- [JEV Flaky Detective](https://github.com/JevForge/jev-flaky-detective) - GitHub Action: TypeSafe Jev classifies failing tests as regression/flaky/environment/unknown (never auto-reruns). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-flaky-detective.md).
- [Jev Flow](https://github.com/daltonrpj/jev-flow) - Standalone Node.js studio for typed Jev workflows (Studio, Compendium, Battle Arena; distinct from jev-flow-harness). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-flow.md).
- [Jev for Claude](https://github.com/VoidLight00/jev-for-claude) - Dependency-free Claude Code plugin with nine user-invoked engineering skills (safety preflight, rule rubric, browser QA, evidence completion and more), conservative local hooks, and an explicit opt-in typed TypeSafe Jev judge adapter. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/voidlight-jev-for-claude.md).
- [Jev for Claude Code (brookcs3)](https://github.com/brookcs3/jev--system-one-for-claude) - Unofficial Claude Code plugin exposing TypeSafe Jev typed judgments (noul/choice/score) plus corpus→eval loops. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/brookcs3-jev-system-one-for-claude.md).
- [Jev for Claude Code (pachelbel23)](https://github.com/pachelbel23/claude-code-jev) - Unofficial one-file installer that adds Jev hooks to Claude Code: skill suggestion and task-type classification per prompt, optional subagent model tier choice and shell risk confirmation, plus a jev-vision CLI for image yes/no, choice and Box questions. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pachelbel23-claude-code-jev.md).
- [jev for DuckDB (judoaseeta)](https://github.com/judoaseeta/duckdb-jev) - DuckDB port of pg-jev: ask TypeSafe Jev yes/no, probability, choice and score questions about table rows from SQL (distinct from prasanthj/duckdb-jev). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/judoaseeta-duckdb-jev.md).
- [Jev for Excel](https://github.com/paulobrien/jev-excel) - Unofficial Excel add-in that calls TypeSafe Jev from worksheet formulas — `=JEV.ASK("choice"|"score"|"noul", A2, …)`, shortcut functions and `JEV.STATE` for multi-field rows — as an async Office add-in for Microsoft 365 or a Windows VBA edition. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-excel.md).
- [Jev for Home Assistant](https://github.com/AboveColin/HA-Jev) - Turns judgments over selected Home Assistant state into sensors and automation responses, with usage accounting. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/ha-jev.md).
- [Jev for Marketers (free guide, Claude skill and recipes for marketing-ops decisions with Jev)](https://github.com/brent-phillips/jev-for-marketers) - Free, hands-on guide to using Jev for the small yes/no calls in marketing ops (route this lead, tag this asset, is this reply interested), plus a Claude skill with four editable recipes (lead routing, reply triage, on-message check, asset tagging), a no-install batch script that runs a recipe over a CSV, and a 50-lead sample with an answer key for scoring. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-for-marketers.md).
- [Jev for Splunk](https://github.com/sispehar/jev-for-splunk) - Splunk app adding a `jev` search command for noul/choice/score over events via TypeSafe Jev with KV-store cache (Apache-2.0 LICENSE; GitHub SPDX unset). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-for-splunk.md).
- [Jev Foundry Judge](https://github.com/nguyennhianhtri/jev-foundry-judge) - Drop-in Azure AI Foundry agent evaluators powered by TypeSafe Jev, plus a BYOK demo app and model-router bench. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-foundry-judge.md).
- [Jev Game Engine](https://github.com/King4s/jev-game-engine) - Early Rust desktop prototype for controlling games through bounded goals: TypeSafe Jev selects a goal, a local executor handles movement, validation and stopping, and every decision is traced, timed and replayable; Minecraft Java 26.2 is the first integration, with an offline fixture preview. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-game-engine.md).
- [Jev Gate (lymagics)](https://github.com/lymagics/jev-gate) - GitHub Action/CLI that asks TypeSafe Jev yes/no quality questions over selected scopes and fails the pipeline on threshold breaches (distinct from other listed jev-gate projects). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/lymagics-jev-gate.md).
- [Jev Gate (royalskynet)](https://github.com/royalskynet/jev-gate-mcp) - MCP server and Claude Code/Codex plugin that prunes tool output, compacts conversations and gives task/review advice with TypeSafe Jev; optional local Laya pilot (distinct from other jev-gate projects). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/royalskynet-jev-gate.md).
- [Jev Gatehouse (Kinde)](https://github.com/kinde-starter-kits/jev-agent-authorization) - Kinde identity + permissions with TypeSafe Jev typed gates before every MCP tool call. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-gatehouse.md).
- [Jev GitHub Action](https://github.com/cachix/jev-action) - GitHub Action: install pinned Jev CLI, run typed judgments on an event/JSON request, expose answers (no issue mutation). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-action.md).
- [Jev Graph Search](https://github.com/Emlembow/jev-graph-search) - CLI and agent skill that retrieves evidence from local Obsidian vaults, Logseq folders, or JSON memory snapshots: a local shortlist is reranked by Jev (TypeSafe or OpenRouter). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-graph-search.md).
- [Jev Harness (Apixly)](https://github.com/apixly-ai/jev-harness) - Python runtime for open-ended goals: a primary AI defines tools, parameter providers and completion checks, then TypeSafe Jev chooses the next tool and arguments from the goal, history and fresh state while code executes, verifies and enforces bounds. Install from Git; the PyPI name jev-harness is an unrelated project. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/apixly-jev-harness.md).
- [Jev Highlight Cutter](https://github.com/gnipbao/jev-highlight-cutter) - Codex agent skill and stdlib Python CLI (Chinese docs) that cuts long interviews, podcasts and streams into an evidence-backed highlight reel of a target length: transcript windows are scored by Jev against your preferences, selections are traceable, and FFmpeg makes a local rough cut. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-highlight-cutter.md).
- [Jev Highlight for DeepSeek Harness (highlight the parts of long AI answers worth reading)](https://github.com/Zorien-ai/dsh-jev-highlight) - Web plugin for DeepSeek Harness that leaves AI answers unchanged but highlights what to read first: it splits an answer into units, asks a decision model four yes/no questions about each (conclusion, action, risk, relevance) and shades them by priority; StartLux-Decision is the default model and TypeSafe Jev is a supported option. Chinese-language docs. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/dsh-jev-highlight.md).
- [Jev IDS](https://github.com/jev-sec/jev-ids) - Few-shot network intrusion detection with Jev, benchmarked on NSL-KDD against an LLM and classic ML. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-ids.md).
- [Jev Improv Piano (a piano that composes and plays bar by bar, with Jev choosing each bar)](https://github.com/tripodxu/jev-piano) - Static web piano that turns a one-line idea into a piece and plays it live: once per bar, Jev answers eight questions in parallel (harmonic function, chord, left-hand texture, rhythm, direction, development, intensity, whether to breathe) from a music-theory-legal candidate set, and a deterministic engine renders the notes. Plays offline with a seeded local decider when there is no key. Modelled on cocktailpeanut/jevthoven; Chinese-language docs. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/tripodxu-jev-piano.md).
- [Jev in Large Language Gibbs](https://github.com/eumemic/jev-gibbs) - Replication of the TruthfulQA consistent-reasoning experiment from Large Language Gibbs (arXiv 2606.19264) with Jev supplying per-claim conditionals, plus controls, GSM8K, knights-and-knaves, entity resolution and kinship tests of where Gibbs helps. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-gibbs.md).
- [Jev Internal Links](https://github.com/NicoSKOOL/jev-internal-links) - Claude Code skill: crawl a site, ask TypeSafe Jev which internal links to add, apply local rules, export a report. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-internal-links.md).
- [Jev Java SDK (dmishra2022)](https://github.com/dmishra2022/jev-java-sdk) - Community Java 26 client for the System One API with typed noul, choice and score questions, retries, timeouts and virtual threads, plus an optional structured-concurrency module and Spring Boot 4 sample. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-java-sdk.md).
- [Jev kit by Mr-heka (record browser or Mac app chores and replay them with Jev)](https://github.com/Mr-heka/jev-kit) - Skill for Claude Code and Codex: the agent records a browser or Mac app chore into a recipe once, then a runner replays it at machine speed, sending only fuzzy calls (right page, right result, which option fits) to Jev through OpenRouter. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/heka-jev-kit.md).
- [Jev Lab](https://github.com/jammaru/jev-lab) - Local Hundred NPC-town and Jev Shogi labs where TypeSafe Jev chooses the next legal action (Rules mode without a key). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-lab.md).
- [Jev Lab by Nijal AI (45-minute French workshop app that pits Jev against an LLM on speed, cost and calibration)](https://github.com/Nijal-AI/jev-lab) - Web workshop for a room of participants on phones or laptops, no account or code: try Jev and an LLM on support, scam and moderation scenarios, play a scam-filter mission where you set allow/review/block limits on 22 fictional messages, then a duel on errors, latency, cost per 1,000 decisions and calibration. Facilitator tab with a leaderboard and a cache-only fallback. French UI; works in simulated mode without keys. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/nijal-jev-lab.md).
- [Jev LLM Quality Tester](https://github.com/aydinozcekic-maker/jev-llm-quality-tester) - Prototype dashboard and FastAPI service that scores LLM-generated answers against explicit criteria with a Jev/TypeSafe structured-scoring adapter, SQLite history and a no-key deterministic demo mode. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-llm-quality-tester.md).
- [Jev LLM router](https://github.com/dbokatov/jev-llm-router) - Python lab that asks TypeSafe Jev to route a query to an OpenAI model, then has a local Ollama judge review the same choice. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-llm-router.md).
- [Jev Logs](https://github.com/reachjalil/jevlogs) - Scores OpenTelemetry logs for a separate analysis branch, with mock mode and conservative error handling. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevlogs.md).
- [Jev Lucid Memory](https://github.com/JayYun98/jev-lucid-memory) - Research prototype of wake–sleep agent memory: an LLM drafts lessons, a decision model (TypeSafe Jev via the official SDK or OpenRouter, or local Clef-Flash) decides what to keep and when a lesson applies, and code enforces evidence and scope. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-lucid-memory.md).
- [JEV Mail Filtering](https://github.com/albertcas/jev-mail-filtering) - Local read-only IMAP inbox triage with TypeSafe Jev (needs reply / worth reading / scam / unsure). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-mail-filtering.md).
- [Jev Mario by imink (Super Mario Bros played by Jev decisions, with a full-campaign replay site)](https://github.com/imink/local-jev-mario) - Super Mario Bros in a real NES emulator where structured game state goes to a Jev Choice that picks the controller input; a browser replay site plays back all 32 stages and 6,275 recorded decisions with their answers, without calling a model; bring your own legally obtained ROM. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/local-jev-mario.md).
- [Jev MCP (asimons81)](https://github.com/asimons81/jev-mcp) - Small community MCP server that exposes three bounded Jev decision tools (yes/no, choose and score) over stdio or authenticated loopback HTTP, with text-only inputs, no search or action execution, and credentials read only from the environment. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/asimons81-jev-mcp.md).
- [Jev MCP (Freepik)](https://github.com/freepik-company/jev-mcp) - Go MCP server for typed decide/classify/verify/rerank with Jev via OpenRouter or TypeSafe (binary/container releases). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/freepik-jev-mcp.md).
- [Jev MCP (tmbmartell)](https://github.com/tmbmartell/jev-mcp) - Portuguese-language MCP server that exposes Jev (TypeSafe System One) to agents such as Hermes, Claude Code, and Cursor as a typed judgment tool for noul, choice, and score questions, routed via OpenRouter or TypeSafe. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-mcp-tmbmartell.md).
- [Jev MCP Server (keysersoft)](https://github.com/keysersoft/jev-mcp-server) - AnythingMCP connector exposing TypeSafe Jev yes/no, classification, and scoring tools to Claude, ChatGPT, and other MCP hosts (hosted or self-hosted). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/keysersoft-jev-mcp-server.md).
- [JEV MLX](https://github.com/itsodeleo/jev-mlx) - Typed decisions from existing MLX-LM and MLX-VLM models on Apple Silicon without fine-tuning or extra decision weights. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-mlx.md).
- [Jev Mode](https://github.com/tiffygk/jev-mode) - Claude Code skills and study course for building with TypeSafe Jev (PolyForm Noncommercial; not all skills call Jev). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/tiffygk-jev-mode.md).
- [Jev Model Router](https://github.com/davila7/claude-code-templates/tree/main/cli-tool/components/mods/productivity/jev-model-router) - Early-access Claude Code mod that uses Jev task assessments and configurable confidence thresholds to route subagent models and main-conversation reasoning effort. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-model-router.md).
- [Jev Model Router (Codex skill)](https://github.com/mrhsiung2015/jev-model-router) - Codex skill (Chinese and English docs) where the parent session condenses a non-trivial task and asks TypeSafe Jev to recommend the model tier and reasoning effort for an execution subagent; a standard-library Python script validates the answer against local policy and outputs one JSON decision, keeping the current model on timeout or invalid responses. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/mrhsiung-jev-model-router.md).
- [Jev Model Router by Akamai Developers (WebAssembly model router with Spin and TypeScript, Jev picking the model)](https://github.com/akamai-developers/jev-model-router) - Sample from Akamai's developer team: a single Spin WebAssembly component exposing `POST /ask` that asks Jev (via OpenRouter) which model should handle each prompt, forwards the prompt to that model on an Ollama instance and returns the answer. Accompanies the blog post "Build a Model Router with Jev, Spin & TypeScript". [Project guide](community/projects/tools/akamai-jev-model-router.md).
- [Jev Model Router by zerone-agents (OpenAI- and Anthropic-compatible gateway where Jev picks the model)](https://github.com/zerone-agents/jev-model-router) - Go model-routing gateway for agents: OpenAI Chat Completions and Anthropic Messages clients connect to it, and either name a model or let Jev choose from editable model cards; it streams text and tool calls, stores configuration in SQLite and ships a CLI, bilingual dashboard, Docker Compose quickstart and an agent skill. Distinct from the separately listed Claude Code and Codex routers of the same name. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/zerone-jev-model-router.md).
- [Jev Model Routing Lab](https://github.com/goldytech/jev-model-routing) - TypeScript lab: TypeSafe Jev typed signals drive confidence-aware Claude/Kimi model routing with simulated offline demos. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-model-routing.md).
- [Jev Moderation](https://github.com/abishakkodi/jev-moderation) - TypeScript and Python SDKs for text moderation with TypeSafe Jev: a bundled, versioned community policy or your own filters, each rule compiled to a Noul question, with review/block thresholds and a policy test CLI (validate, eval, compare with quality gates). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-moderation.md).
- [Jev Moderation API](https://github.com/Noah-Haf/jev-moderation-api) - Small FastAPI proof of concept that moderates text against configurable policies with Jev Noul questions via the official TypeSafe Python SDK. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-moderation-api.md).
- [Jev Moderation for Laravel (confidence-aware content moderation)](https://github.com/phoenix1331/laravel-jev-moderation) - Laravel package that classifies any Eloquent model with Jev when it is saved and uses per-category probability thresholds to pass it, hold it for human review or block it, keeping an append-only history of every decision and review. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/laravel-jev-moderation.md).
- [Jev NetHack](https://github.com/statico/jev-nethack) - TypeSafe Jev plays NetHack 5.0: code lists legal moves, Jev picks one (dashboard + optional Hardfought). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-nethack.md).
- [Jev Observer](https://github.com/LimePencil/jev-observer) - Local proxy and dashboard for TypeSafe Jev decisions (history, versions, latency/cost) with an embedded web UI. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-observer.md).
- [JEV OpenFast Browser](https://github.com/samuelyossef/jev-openfast) - Local chat-driven browser agent built on Jev Ultrafast: Jev (via OpenRouter) picks each operation and target from a numbered element table in a real Chrome tab, checks results before reporting success, and hands over for logins or CAPTCHAs. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-openfast-browser.md).
- [Jev Orchestrator](https://github.com/petercr/jev-orchestrator) - Small Node/TypeScript experiment testing whether Jev can make useful next-step decisions around a coding agent: decision-only by default (inspect the repo, send compact state to Jev via TypeSafe, Vercel or OpenRouter, apply deterministic thresholds, record a JSONL trace), with an explicit `--orchestrate` mode for a bounded, manually approved loop of safe searches, reads, read-only git, validation scripts and delegation to Codex CLI or Claude Code. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-orchestrator.md).
- [JEV Orchestrator (Claude Code plugin where Jev picks the model, effort and tools for every subagent)](https://github.com/hiyoku/jev-orc) - Claude Code plugin that intercepts every Agent spawn and asks Jev which model (Haiku, Sonnet or Opus), effort level and tool profile the subagent needs, plus a /jev-orchestrator:run command that executes a prompt file wave by wave with parallel subagents routed the same way. Failed tasks stop only the waves that depend on them, and commits happen only when you ask. Supports a TypeSafe key or an OpenRouter key, stored in the system keychain. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-orc.md).
- [Jev Outlook Cleaner](https://github.com/jimmyLC/jev-outlook-cleaner) - Local web app that triages older Outlook Inbox messages into keep, review and delete candidates with Jev plus local mail signals, auto-moves only high-confidence bulk mail to Deleted Items, and never deletes permanently. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-outlook-cleaner.md).
- [Jev Paired-Replay Study](https://github.com/poisson-labs/jev-replay) - Pre-registered study of whether identical replays flip Jev 1.13's action at shipped thresholds: 12,705 calls on 847 near-threshold items from Banking77, CLINC150 and HelpSteer2 found about 18.5% flips at 0.5 and 0.6 and 5.3% at 0.9, with flips nearly cancelling out. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-paired-replay.md).
- [Jev Playground (xingqiux)](https://github.com/xingqiux/jev-playground) - Local, standard-library Python test bench for Jev: enter text, JSON or images plus Noul, Choice, Score or Box questions, run one request, and see probabilities, raw request/response, cURL and a choice-order sensitivity check, with 60 built-in examples. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/xingqiux-jev-playground.md).
- [Jev Playground by gmassello (step-by-step Spanish-language Jev lessons via Vercel AI Gateway)](https://github.com/gmassello/jev) - Small local web playground, written in Spanish, that teaches Jev step by step through the Vercel AI Gateway: you try typed questions, see confidence and per-call cost, and read practical notes on calibrating thresholds, measuring limits and keeping the key server-side; it also compares the native TypeSafe API and Cloudflare Workers AI routes. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-playground-gmassello.md).
- [jev plays snake](https://github.com/hoanganh25991/typesafe-jev) - Browser Snake where code computes legal moves and exact facts (food distance, flood-fill reachable cells, dead ends) and TypeSafe Jev picks one move per tick with a single `choice` question through a small Hono proxy; misses the deadline → the snake goes straight. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-plays-snake.md).
- [Jev RAG Chinese Benchmark](https://github.com/vicfei/jev-rag-zh-benchmark) - Reproducible Chinese RAG reranking benchmark for TypeSafe Jev (T2Reranking, pinned model, cost caps, per-call logs). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-rag-zh-benchmark.md).
- [JEV Reasoning Navigator](https://github.com/AndreuVM/jev-reasoning-navigator) - Cognitive supervision runtime: TypeSafe Jev semantic judgment plus policy engine, capability receipts, and sandboxed tool execution (license unspecified). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-reasoning-navigator.md).
- [JEV Research MCP](https://github.com/Merth1470/jev-research-mcp) - Local-first MCP research tool: TypeSafe Jev filters search hits and page blocks before dumping them into an agent context. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-research-mcp.md).
- [Jev resume screener (Claude Code plugin)](https://github.com/brent-phillips/jbp-plugins) - Claude Code plugin that screens a folder of resumes against a job description with Jev: fit ranking, copied-resume clusters, flags for resumes mirroring the job description, and a human review queue. The output is a sorted reading list, never a rejection. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-resume-screener.md).
- [Jev Review](https://github.com/NiazMorshed2007/jev-review) - MCP server for experimental software-quality rubric scores and comparisons; requires `JEV_API_KEY` and sends supplied code context to TypeSafe. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-review.md).
- [Jev Review (Dev Agrawal)](https://github.com/devagrawal09/jev-review) - Screens JavaScript and TypeScript changes or source files with staged Jev judgments and displays evidence-linked findings in a local dashboard. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-review-devagrawal.md).
- [Jev Review Action](https://github.com/fatwang2/jev-review-action) - Configurable GitHub Action: catalog or PR classification with TypeSafe Jev only (no text-gen), one template comment. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-review-action.md).
- [Jev review gate](https://github.com/RomanXSad/jev-review-gate) - GitHub Actions gate: local rules + TypeSafe Jev questions on the diff; exits 0/1 (does not merge). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-review-gate.md).
- [Jev Review Markers for Premiere](https://github.com/gbesse/jev-premiere-markers) - Premiere UXP panel that turns typed Jev review findings on an SRT caption export into timeline markers: Jev flags issues and picks the exact caption, and every marker is added in one undoable transaction with the caption text copied byte for byte. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-premiere-markers.md).
- [JEV robot (Unitree G1 in MuJoCo with Jev motor decisions and Gemini planning)](https://github.com/arterialist/jev-robot) - Simulation-only demo of a Unitree G1 humanoid in MuJoCo: Jev chooses velocities and bounded joint offsets (full control) or named movements (primitives) while Gemini plans from the live camera frame, all shown in a browser control room; an offline mode runs without keys. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-robot.md).
- [Jev Router for Windows](https://github.com/pouramin/jev-router-windows) - One-click Windows GUI for TypeSafe Jev routing with Codex (jev-codex-bridge) and assisted Claude Code plugin setup. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-router-windows.md).
- [Jev Rules for WordPress](https://github.com/gbesse/wordpress-jev-rules) - Experimental WordPress plugin that queues posts for Jev classification against versioned rule packs and keeps an administrator review history; posts are never changed, and other plugins can enqueue posts and react to decisions through hooks. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/wordpress-jev-rules.md).
- [Jev Runway](https://github.com/IPECTER/jev-runway) - Local Codex proxy that uses TypeSafe Jev to trim stale tool output and save tokens. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-runway.md).
- [Jev Score](https://github.com/a-Fig/jev-score) - Local CLI/web scoreboard that grades document revisions against your criteria with TypeSafe Jev via OpenRouter Decisions. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-score.md).
- [Jev Second Brain](https://github.com/fellowship-dev/jev-second-brain) - Local-first Markdown vault CLI: FTS index, source-linked related-note suggestions, optional TypeSafe Jev judgments via Vercel AI Gateway. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-second-brain.md).
- [JEV Sees](https://github.com/CharlesFeng0314/JEV_sees) - Give TypeSafe Jev eyes: one call, many object judgments from camera/video frames. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-sees.md).
- [JEV Shield by JamesANZ (Jev-scored prompt-injection and command gate for coding agents)](https://github.com/JamesANZ/JevPromptShield) - Guard that sits outside the model: it scores incoming prompts and pasted text for injection with Jev, and checks proposed shell commands (local rules first, Jev for the rest), then ordinary code maps the probabilities to allow, ask or block, including as a Claude Code permission hook; the README walks through a Gandalf password-game test. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jamesanz-jev-shield.md).
- [Jev Sift](https://github.com/kbhuw/jev-sift) - Screens files, public webpages, and tool descriptions with Jev before an agent reads selected content; requires a TypeSafe key, and upstream licensing is unspecified. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-sift.md).
- [Jev Skill Router (kelasdev)](https://github.com/kelasdev/jev-skill-router) - OpenCode plugin that uses TypeSafe Jev (two gated `/v1/systemone` calls) to inject at most one relevant `SKILL.md` into the system prompt. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/kelasdev-jev-skill-router.md).
- [Jev Skills (n23eos)](https://github.com/n23eos/jev-skills) - Fourteen installable Claude Code and Codex skills plus a CLI where TypeSafe Jev makes one bounded choice (skill, model, context file, first test, bug component or plan) and the coding agent does the work; distinct from the listed jev-skills plugin. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/n23eos-jev-skills.md).
- [Jev Skills Retrieval](https://github.com/aimmetal-tech/jev-skills-retrieval) - Pi coding agent extension that retrieves skills per request: Jev scores skill metadata once per turn, and Pi then reads only the selected skills' bodies, with pinned skills and explicit `/skill:name` taking priority. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-skills-retrieval.md).
- [JEV SOC alert triage (classifies exported Windows security events with Jev and routes uncertain ones to review)](https://github.com/ahmed35636/jev-triage) - Python CLI that classifies exported Windows security events, assigns investigation priority and routes uncertain or high-risk results into a review queue, writing one JSONL decision trail per event (detector hits, backend and model, confidence, severity, routing reasons). Jev is the model backend; a rule baseline runs without credentials and an optional Ollama stage writes investigation notes. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/ahmed35636-jev-triage.md).
- [Jev Starter](https://github.com/simplybychris/jev-starter) - Express + React starter for small web apps that make Jev decisions via OpenRouter. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-starter.md).
- [Jev Starter](https://github.com/YanfLIZi56/jev-starter) - Browser console to configure TypeSafe Jev Noul/Choice/Score questions, test live, and export Python/JS/cURL (or example JSON). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/yanflizi56-jev-starter.md).
- [Jev Support Ticket Router](https://github.com/Zafer-Liu/jev-demo-router) - Support-ticket routing demo (English/Chinese) that asks Jev department, urgency and tone in one call and sends low-confidence tickets to a human queue. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-demo-router.md).
- [Jev Symfony Bundle](https://github.com/vbcherepanov/jev-symfony-bundle) - Unofficial Symfony bundle for TypeSafe Jev: typed client, validator constraints, Messenger, Workflow guards, and profiler. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-symfony-bundle.md).
- [Jev Talking Head Workflow (talking-head video production skills)](https://github.com/HaoyuJiangdasak/jev-talking-head-workflow) - Reusable workflow of agent skills for Chinese talking-head videos, from script to rough cut, B-roll, subtitles, mixing and QA, where Jev is the structured review layer comparing takes, flagging fragments and repetition and suggesting layouts, while humans confirm every edit. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-talking-head-workflow.md).
- [Jev Task Router](https://github.com/hainalstefan-coder/Jev_Router) - npm CLI that asks Jev, through the AI SDK and Vercel AI Gateway, which eligible coding model should handle a task, then hands the task to your local Codex or GitHub Copilot CLI session after you confirm. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-task-router.md).
- [Jev the Janitor](https://github.com/kylehovance-ai/jev-the-janitor) - Markdown vault janitor: TypeSafe Jev votes on each note; code adds frontmatter or quarantines secrets (dry-run default; offline mode). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-the-janitor.md).
- [JEV ToC search (search a PDF through its own table of contents, with Jev ordering each branch of a depth-first walk)](https://github.com/bakubay/jev-dfs-document-search) - Experiment in long-document search inspired by PageIndex: parse a PDF with Datalab, recover its real table of contents and match printed page numbers to PDF pages, then let Jev order the branches at each level of a depth-first search. Low-probability branches are never pruned, and in the worst case every page is checked before it says no evidence was found. Includes grounded answer evaluation and interactive traces. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-dfs-document-search.md).
- [Jev Trader](https://github.com/jarrodwatts/jev-trader) - Studies Jev market-direction choices, simulated fills, and on-chain order execution through a Bun trading experiment. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-trader.md).
- [JEV Trading Command Center](https://github.com/mars-tw/jev-trading-command-center) - Paper-trading research app (README in Traditional Chinese) where Jev decisions pass validation and risk checks before a simulated broker acts, shown in an animated 3D command center with model 'pets' and an offline demo that uses only synthetic data. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-trading-command-center.md).
- [Jev Ultrafast](https://github.com/browser-use/jev-ultrafast) - Selects browser operations and observed targets with Jev, using a separate text model for text entry. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-ultrafast.md).
- [Jev Unpack](https://github.com/lucaminudel/jev-unpack-hook) - Codex/Claude Code Stop hook where Jev detects multi-topic, multi-ask agent messages and makes the agent pace them one ask at a time. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-unpack-hook.md).
- [Jev Valley (simulated valley where rabbits, deer and wolves decide with Jev)](https://github.com/Sarthak8696/jev-valley) - Small ecosystem simulation where every animal picks its next action once per valley hour with one Choice question and a few Score and Noul questions, watched live in a 3D browser viewer, with replays and month-long result notes. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-valley.md).
- [Jev Vampire Survivors](https://github.com/oldmoldycake/jev_vampire_survivors) - TypeSafe Jev picks character, stage, level-ups, and walk direction in real Steam Vampire Survivors. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-vampire-survivors.md).
- [Jev vehicle classifier (Korean 12-class traffic survey)](https://github.com/kh20134/jev-vehicle-classifier) - Episode 1 of a Korean ITS series: vision-style features (axles, units, trailer type, load tons) become a JSON state and Jev picks one of the 12 official Korean road-traffic vehicle classes with probabilities; low confidence goes to an unclassified bucket for review (README in Korean). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-vehicle-classifier.md).
- [Jev Video Lab (ask structured questions about video footage with Jev and evaluate it on annotated clips)](https://github.com/EventumAI/jev-video-lab) - Experimental local app for asking video questions with Jev: event search across indexed videos (before, after, overlap), multiple-choice questions on a segment, object presence and Box locations drawn on frames, procedure checks that flag missed steps for a person, recording alignment, human review and exports. The author's first study on Perception Test reports 463 of 658 questions right (70.4%) and weak precise event localization. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-video-lab.md).
- [Jev Vision Benchmarks](https://github.com/MaceTenth/jev-vision-benchmarks) - Benchmarks of Jev 1.14 image input on marketplace tasks (product-photo moderation, photo-to-listing matching, classification, package-label reading) against GPT vision models, AWS Rekognition and OpenAI moderation, with checked-in labels and results. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-vision-benchmarks.md).
- [Jev Voice Browser](https://github.com/moritzkremb/jev-voice-browser) - Experiments with typed Jev decisions over partial voice transcripts and page elements to control Playwright, with a local decision inspector. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-voice-browser.md).
- [Jev vs Basal insurance benchmark](https://github.com/marcinnowak79/jev-basal-insurance-benchmark) - Polish motor-insurance (autocasco) decision benchmark: 325 synthetic typed decisions built from public policy terms, with code-computed labels, run against Jev, Basal 2.0 and Gemini models with accuracy, calibration (ECE) and latency. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-basal-insurance-benchmark.md).
- [Jev vs Parallel Decisions (two real Jev gates scored against answer keys fixed before either tool ran)](https://github.com/VedantBhayani/jev-vs-parallel) - Benchmark of Jev against Parallel's new web-searching Decisions API on the questions two of Eric Siu's production Jev gates ask: M&A and ICP criteria for 50 YC companies, and whether a content topic has already been published. Answer keys come from the YC directory and the site's own pages, fixed before either tool ran; results are reported as balanced accuracy. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-vs-parallel.md).
- [Jev vs. LLM (Banking77)](https://github.com/0xGondarxyz/jev-vs-llm) - Small, fair test of the "up to 200x faster" claim: 100 real Banking77 customer messages sorted into 77 categories by Jev, Claude Haiku 5.5 and Claude Fable 5.1, measuring accuracy, off-list answers, median and p90 latency, and cost. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-vs-llm-banking77.md).
- [Jev WCAG Auditor](https://github.com/ctrimm/jev-wcag-auditor) - axe-core WCAG checks plus TypeSafe Jev adjudication of judgement calls on public/.gov pages. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-wcag-auditor.md).
- [JEV WHERE?](https://github.com/cesarroger/jev-where) - Claude Code plugin that finds where Jev could make decisions in your app and writes the typed Choice/Score/Noul question for each (analysis only). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-where.md).
- [Jev Workflow by cathy-kim (Claude Code plugin and guide for designing Jev decisions offline)](https://github.com/cathy-kim/jev-workflow) - Community toolkit for designing typed decision workflows: a portable skill with Claude Code, Codex and Hermes adapters, offline examples, an illustrated guide, and an opt-in live client and stdio MCP server for TypeSafe System One, OpenAI Decisions and OpenRouter. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/cathy-kim-jev-workflow.md).
- [Jev Workflows for Codex and Claude Code](https://github.com/integrate-your-mind/jev-codex-plugin) - Open-source Codex and Claude Code plugin that consults Jev about tools, models, tasks, skills, context and custom decisions, diagnoses failed commands and checks completion claims against evidence; eight MCP tools, lifecycle hooks and three standalone skills. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-workflows-codex-claude.md).
- [Jev Workshop (notebooks and deck)](https://github.com/fnusatvik07/jev-workshop) - Teaching kit for Jev: five notebooks executed live against the API, a 42-slide deck with speaker notes, and a cited reference covering the primitives, confidence and common patterns. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/fnusatvik07-jev-workshop.md).
- [Jev Workspace (give Jev a mission and some links; it judges every item live and hands you a results page)](https://github.com/freakngenius/jev-workspace) - Local app: type a goal, paste links or drop files (sites, sitemaps, feeds, CSV or Google Sheets, PDFs, Instagram exports), let Claude draft a yes/no gate plus breakdown questions, and watch Jev judge every item live with counters, throughput and cost. Matches, rejects and an uncertain band that Claude reviews; filters, CSV/JSON export and an optional Mac app wrapper. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-workspace.md).
- [Jev x AI SDK Form Router](https://github.com/ashiruus/jev-and-ai-sdk) - Next.js form router that uses Jev to route submissions by context, with an LLM fallback for uncertain cases. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-and-ai-sdk.md).
- [jev-a2a (Jev router for Paseo agents)](https://github.com/skhlo/jev-a2a) - Personal-prototype router that sends work between coding agents running in Paseo sessions on several machines and keeps one record of what was asked, who took it and what came back; when a request names no recipient, TypeSafe Jev chooses the participant. Includes a web board and design docs; published as a worked example, not a product. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-a2a.md).
- [jev-adb](https://github.com/srsergi0/jev-adb) - MCP server for Android control over ADB where TypeSafe Jev makes typed UI decisions behind consent and safety gates. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-adb.md).
- [jev-advisor (gjc-plugin)](https://github.com/jjongguet/gjc-plugin) - gajae-code (gjc) plugin set whose jev-advisor asks TypeSafe Jev for fast/balanced/strong tier advice on subagent delegations, in shadow mode by default, with offline reports. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/gjc-jev-advisor.md).
- [jev-advisor-gateway](https://github.com/1arley/jev-advisor-gateway) - Local gateway that routes coding-agent tool decisions through Jev and can attach a read-only advisor (GPT or Claude) without giving the advisor write authority. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-advisor-gateway.md).
- [jev-agent-browser](https://github.com/mhingston/jev-agent-browser) - Confidence-gated browser actions for `agent-browser` with TypeSafe Jev (or Gateway/Cloudflare/custom providers). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-agent-browser.md).
- [jev-agent-failure-benchmark](https://github.com/TokenTrim/jev-agent-failure-benchmark) - Benchmarks TypeSafe Jev on Who&When Pro agent-failure attribution (who/when/what) against published LLM baselines. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-agent-failure-benchmark.md).
- [jev-agent-kit](https://github.com/walidboulanouar/jev-agent-kit) - Zero-dependency CLI + MCP tools (route/triage/guard/grep/rank/compact/judge/…) on TypeSafe Jev (`@walidboulanouar/jevkit`; distinct from Rust jevkit). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-agent-kit.md).
- [jev-agent-kit (nanoDBA)](https://github.com/nanoDBA/jev-agent-kit) - Evidence-layer hooks for Claude Code/Codex/Hermes: TypeSafe Jev scores risky tool calls in shadow mode by default — distinct from walidboulanouar/jev-agent-kit. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/nanodba-jev-agent-kit.md).
- [jev-agent-kit by bitskc (self-hosted Jev MCP toolbox and skills for oh-my-pi and Claude Code)](https://github.com/bitskc/jev-agent-kit) - One Cloudflare Worker exposing 15 Jev-backed MCP tools, plus 7 agent skills and an optional guard hook, for the judgment calls coding agents make all day: is this shell command safe, is this sub-agent the right size, did the agent really verify before saying done. Builds on jkudish/jev-mcp and jonathanavis96/jev-kit; deterministic rules handle what a model shouldn't guess. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/bitskc-jev-agent-kit.md).
- [jev-agent-memory (no vector DB: Jev picks which past chats and profile facts a Claude Code agent recalls)](https://github.com/hojeongna/jev-agent-memory) - Unattended memory for a Claude Code agent you talk to through Telegram or Discord: hooks capture the chat, Claude Haiku summarizes it into daily Markdown logs every 30 minutes, and on each message Jev picks the topic and which past excerpts would help, injecting at most four. A daily job distills up to three evidence-backed traits into a user profile. Unofficial; English and Korean docs. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-agent-memory.md).
- [jev-agent-router](https://github.com/CiroVilmer/jev-agent-router) - Claude Code mod that picks the model and effort of every subagent from its task type, with Jev as an optional classifier. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-agent-router.md).
- [jev-agent-skill (RosarioDiBartolo)](https://github.com/RosarioDiBartolo/jev-agent-skill) - Agent skill connecting Jev and Kev System One models to Codex and AI agents for fast tool routing, classification, and bounded decisions. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/rosariodibartolo-jev-agent-skill.md).
- [jev-agent-tools](https://github.com/jkudish/jev-agent-tools) - Jev transport/provider layer with fail-closed validation; shared by jkudish/jev-browser and jkudish/jev-mcp. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jkudish-jev-agent-tools.md).
- [jev-ai-demo (Jev primitives walkthrough)](https://github.com/georgeykalangi/jev-ai-demo) - Small Python walkthrough of Jev's Choice, Score and Noul primitives: a quickstart, atomic scoring and confidence routing examples that replay the sample responses from TypeSafe's docs without a key, then call the live API when one is set. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-ai-demo-georgeykalangi.md).
- [Jev-AI-Skill](https://github.com/King4s/Jev-AI-Skill) - Claude Code/Codex/Hermes skill + MCP: TypeSafe Jev gates for agent decisions. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-ai-skill.md).
- [jev-ai-use-cases (atliq)](https://github.com/atliq/jev-ai-use-cases) - LangChain notebook: TypeSafe Jev for triage, routing, reply guards, tool selection, finance-inbox checks. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/atliq-jev-ai-use-cases.md).
- [jev-align](https://github.com/sutro-sh/jev-align) - CLI to build calibrated AI Functions from human labels using TypeSafe Jev evaluation and GEPA optimization. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-align.md).
- [jev-android](https://github.com/dougsong/jev-android) - Kotlin Android UI-agent SDK: TypeSafe Jev or DeepSeek selects accessibility actions; local Maven sample (not on Maven Central). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-android.md).
- [jev-answers](https://github.com/AmooEbrahim/jev-answers) - MCP server + CLI: send files and typed questions to TypeSafe Jev and get the raw answer back (one session folder per request). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-answers.md).
- [jev-apply](https://github.com/Noface07/SystemOne-Apply) - Job-application form filler: rules plus a decision backend (local Laya default, TypeSafe Jev selectable) choose facts/options from your profile; stops before Submit. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-apply.md).
- [jev-audit (agent skill)](https://github.com/idogoldd/skills) - Agent skill that audits a codebase for brittle fact-guessing rules and writes a ranked `JEV_OPPORTUNITIES.md` with evidence, fit scores, a replacement sketch that keeps reliable checks in code, and a measurement plan; makes no Jev calls itself. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-audit-skill.md).
- [jev-auto-approve](https://github.com/BasmaAbouzied0/jev-auto-approve) - Claude Code hook: TypeSafe Jev auto-approves read-only shell commands in milliseconds; everything else still prompts you (fail-open). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/basmaabouzied0-jev-auto-approve.md).
- [jev-auto-mode (OpenCode plugin where Jev judges permission requests)](https://github.com/ttyobiwan/jev-auto-mode) - OpenCode V2 plugin that adds an auto mode: edits are approved, and Jev judges other permission requests for task relevance, data destruction, secret exposure, data transmission and unauthorized remote or system changes, denying anything uncertain. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/opencode-jev-auto-mode.md).
- [jev-autopilot](https://github.com/hmcdaniel03/jev-autopilot) - Claude Code plugin that keeps unattended sessions moving: TypeSafe Jev answers Claude's routine questions, screens every non-read-only tool call, and holds risky ones until you tap Proceed or Block on your phone via Telegram; falls back to the terminal when Jev is unreachable. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-autopilot.md).
- [jev-backed-function-calling](https://github.com/gogoymh/jev-backed-function-calling) - Thin Python layer that uses TypeSafe Jev as a verifier behind function-calling flows (`TYPESAFE_API_KEY` / `JEV_API_KEY`), so tool arguments can be checked with typed System One questions. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-backed-function-calling.md).
- [jev-backend-qa](https://github.com/diamitani/jev-backend-qa) - Backend QA agent with PAL/Jev adjudication to BLOCK/WARN/PASS (CLI + GitHub Action). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-backend-qa.md).
- [jev-bench (stern9)](https://github.com/stern9/jev-bench) - Benchmark harness that asks Jev and general LLMs (via system-one-adapter) the same routing, yes/no and rating questions and reports accuracy, p50/p95 latency, calibration and cost. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/stern9-jev-bench.md).
- [jev-blindspot](https://github.com/jsk4581/jev-blindspot) - Claude Code / Codex side panel: TypeSafe Jev gates whether a prompt needs a second look, then optional blind-spot cards (issue #400). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-blindspot.md).
- [jev-box-tracking](https://github.com/BenCaunt/jev-box-tracking) - Python library and webcam demo for real-time object tracking: Jev Box answers give the absolute position, optical flow tracks motion between answers, and a Kalman filter rewinds and fuses late answers. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-box-tracking.md).
- [jev-brain (one GPU serving chat and Jev-compatible decisions from one open model)](https://github.com/wcole3/jev-brain) - Self-hosted gateway in front of a single vLLM process that serves OpenAI-style chat and Jev-compatible `/v1/systemone` decisions at the same time from one loaded model, with nine profiles across Gemma 4 and Qwen models in several quantizations (some with image decisions) and published Decision Index and latency results; independent of hosted Jev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-brain.md).
- [jev-broker](https://github.com/AlekseiUL/jev-broker) - Local HTTP MCP broker for Hermes agents: validate questions, call TypeSafe Jev via OpenRouter, return structured judgments (agent still decides). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/alekseiul-jev-broker.md).
- [jev-browse (cooper667)](https://github.com/cooper667/jev-browse) - Claude Code plugin: plain-English Playwright checklist QA judged by TypeSafe Jev on Cloudflare Workers AI (distinct from other jev-browse listings). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/cooper667-jev-browse.md).
- [jev-browse (danielnc)](https://github.com/danielnc/jev-browse) - browser-harness helpers: TypeSafe Jev decides each click for agent-delegated website sub-tasks (distinct from cooper667). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/danielnc-jev-browse.md).
