- [jsort](https://github.com/keltokhy/jsort) - Semantic sort CLI: order lines along a plain-English dimension via pairwise TypeSafe Jev comparisons and a Bradley–Terry scale. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jsort.md).
- [Juardrails](https://github.com/abhaybhargav/juardrails) - Go guardrails service for TypeSafe Jev policies (YAML/UI, namespaces, audit, REST/CLI); repository license unspecified at review. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/juardrails.md).
- [judge_rails](https://github.com/just-the-v/judge_rails) - Rails gem that stores TypeSafe Jev Noul/Choice/Score answers as ordinary, indexable ActiveRecord attributes kept up to date, with SQL scopes such as `urgency_above(0.8)`; also a plain Ruby client. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/judge-rails.md).
- [judgelint (lint code against plain-English conventions with Jev)](https://github.com/jpcamara/judgelint) - Linter that checks code against conventions you write as bullets in a README: a decision model such as Jev judges whether each file breaks each rule, and judgelint reports violations with line numbers, confidence and an exit code. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/judgelint.md).
- [judgelint by filip-a-9 (lint and fuzz typed decision questions for Jev or a local llama.cpp model)](https://github.com/filip-a-9/judgelint) - Tests your decision questions before they ship: static rules (missing escape option, overlapping options, counting or date questions), option-order bias from every rotation of the options, out-of-scope probes that should not get confident answers, and noise and injection checks. A plan command shows call count and Jev cost first, a hard spend cap per run, and a non-zero exit so CI can gate on it. Different project from the Ruby judgelint already listed. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/judgelint-question-fuzzer.md).
- [judgment (Rust)](https://github.com/chussenot/judgment) - Rust crate that turns TypeSafe System One (Jev) answers into verified, testable Rust types — typed choice/noul/score questions, a `Fake` backend that checks scripted answers like a server would, and `.jud` rubric documents that keep thresholds beside the questions. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/judgment.md).
- [Juger vite, bloquer juste (talk demo: a mail agent hit by prompt injection, then guarded by Jev)](https://github.com/lorenzorama/juger-vite-bloquer-juste) - Code from an IndabaX Madagascar 2026 talk: a DeepAgents + Claude mail agent attacked through prompt injection, run in two configs. In A, Jev only watches; in B, Jev quarantines mails that address the AI and blocks drafts that stray from the request or leak a secret. Includes 16 scenarios (10 attacks, 6 benign tasks), a golden set, a Jev-as-judge scorer and two replays that run without keys. French docs. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/juger-vite-bloquer-juste.md).
- [juna](https://github.com/safzanpirani/juna) - Lean Pi coding agent profile that keeps context small, with TypeSafe Jev pruning tool output. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/juna.md).
- [jurl](https://github.com/rayoplateado/jurl) - curl that reads the page for you: `jurl -q "question" <url>` fetches a page (with a headless browser when it needs JavaScript), and TypeSafe Jev picks the passages, links or code that answer, with Cloudflare Clef-flash for images; no chatbot, prose stays verbatim. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jurl.md).
- [Kafka Jev Connector](https://github.com/smatiolids/kafka-jev-connector) - Kafka Connect sink connector that sends each record from your input topics to TypeSafe Jev with a question set you configure and publishes an enriched record (original message, answers, trace and dedupe IDs) to an output topic; at-least-once delivery, dead-letter topic, Confluent Cloud custom-connector or self-managed Connect. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/kafka-jev-connector.md).
- [Kaizen.Jev](https://github.com/kaizen-hq/Kaizen.Jev) - .NET 11 client for the TypeSafe Jev API (noul, choice, score) published as `KaizenIO.Jev`. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/kaizen-jev.md).
- [kariyer-decision-bench (Turkish job-posting benchmark of Jev, tau-4b and prompted LLMs)](https://github.com/kubrakrcn/kariyer-decision-bench) - Benchmark harness from Kariyer.net R&D that asks the same seven typed questions (seniority, department, work mode and others) of Turkish decision models and prompted LLMs over 600 real job postings. Reported results: Jev 1.13.0 had the best accuracy (71.5%) and seniority error, while the open tau-4b model was better calibrated (ECE 0.093 against 0.156); a hand check of 40 errors found most were label or taxonomy problems. README in Turkish. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/kariyer-decision-bench.md).
- [Kassad](https://github.com/jacob-berendsohn/kassad) - .NET LLM guardrails: TypeSafe Jev typed checks return Allow/Flag/Review/Block with calibrated confidence (ASP.NET middleware). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/kassad.md).
- [Keeping an NPC on quest](https://github.com/ghosthat-studio/keeping-an-npc-on-quest) - Educational companion code: TypeSafe Jev checks every line of a local-LLM NPC for leaked facts, inventions and broken character. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/keeping-an-npc-on-quest.md).
- [Keepsake (X saves for your agents)](https://github.com/brianbolze/keepsake) - Local, searchable library of the posts you like and bookmark on X: a Chrome extension captures saves, a Node service stores them in SQLite and Markdown, and Jev classifies each save and ranks the library for natural-language questions from a CLI and MCP server. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/keepsake.md).
- [KenteiScript](https://github.com/receptron/kenteiscript) - Small JSON quiz/test DSL (“DSL as harness”) where an LLM authors items and TypeSafe Jev grades free-text answers; includes MulmoKentei, a sample Cloudflare Worker runtime. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/kenteiscript.md).
- [Kestra TypeSafe plugin](https://github.com/kestra-io/plugin-typesafe) - Kestra plugin for TypeSafe System One typed evaluations inside orchestrated workflows (not Scala Typesafe). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/kestra-plugin-typesafe.md).
- [Kettei Kart](https://github.com/sqwishai/kettei-kart) - Camera-only SuperTuxKart harness for decision models: each frame returns a probability for each of nine controls and the highest wins, with recorded head-to-head results and adapters for Sqwish's Kettei Fat, Liquid D1 Ultra, and TypeSafe Jev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/kettei-kart.md).
- [kev-onnx-cpu](https://github.com/Kazuhito00/kev-onnx-cpu) - Minimal-dependency CPU inference for the open Kev 0.6B typed-decision model (ONNX, `onnxruntime` + `numpy` only), with choice/score/noul questions in one pass and verification against the `open-jev` reference; independent of hosted Jev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/kev-onnx-cpu.md).
- [kevala](https://github.com/bvolpato/kevala) - Zero-dependency Rust→WASM decision models (Laya/Kev/…) in any web page with WebGPU; independent of official Jev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/kevala.md).
- [KevDB](https://github.com/ElyasEbrahim/KevDB) - Semantic SQL functions in DuckDB powered by open-weight decision models, benchmarked under pre-registered criteria against the Jev API and LLMs. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/kevdb.md).
- [Kimi-JevCompact](https://github.com/kkaminsk/kimi-jevcompact) - Verbatim, Jev-guided context compaction for Kimi Code CLI (OpenSpec proposals). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/kimi-jevcompact.md).
- [KiroGraph](https://github.com/davide-desio-eleva/kirograph) - Local semantic code knowledge graph and MCP server for Kiro (experimental support for other agents) whose opt-in memory-relation, wiki-contradiction and auth-detection modes can use TypeSafe Jev typed classification. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/kirograph.md).
- [Kkdev92.Jev](https://github.com/kkdev92/jev-dotnet) - Unofficial .NET 10 SDK for Jev that validates a typed decision plan once, reuses the encoded bytes on every call, and returns answers through typed handles only after contract checks. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/kkdev92-jev.md).
- [Klassify](https://github.com/fajarnuha/klassify) - Kotlin Multiplatform DSL/SDK and Native CLI/MCP for TypeSafe System One Noul/Choice/Score (distinct from jev4k). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/klassify.md).
- [KLibJev (Kotlin Multiplatform Jev client)](https://github.com/BoD/KLibJev) - Kotlin Multiplatform client for the Jev API that handles authentication and evaluates text, structured data and images with Noul, Choice, Score and Box questions, returning typed answers with probabilities as a Kotlin `Result`. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/klibjev.md).
- [ko-decision-bench](https://github.com/phykn/ko-decision-bench) - Reproducible Korean benchmark for Jev and System One-style decision models: same questions, prompts and metrics across 7 KLUE, KoBEST, KorNLI and UnSmile tasks, with accuracy, Brier, ECE and latency, and a `/v1/systemone` adapter. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/ko-decision-bench.md).
- [koishi-plugin-group-audit (QQ group moderation with OpenAI Moderation, Jev or an LLM)](https://github.com/DNAlec/koishi-plugin-group-audit) - Koishi plugin for QQ groups: first-stage filters for QR codes, contact and group cards and Tencent online docs, then content review by OpenAI Moderation, TypeSafe Jev or an OpenAI-compatible LLM, each optional, with message recall and escalating mutes when a rule matches. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/koishi-group-audit.md).
- [kojev (Kotlin Multiplatform client that returns your own enums)](https://github.com/ItisNoMatter/kojev) - Unofficial Kotlin Multiplatform client for Jev: you describe choices as your own enums or sealed types and get typed answers and probabilities back, with no string keys or casts. Targets JVM, Android and iOS. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/kojev.md).
- [kormem (local agent memory ranked by Jev)](https://github.com/kushalsinha/kormem) - Local long-term memory for coding agents with QMD-style commands: it files chats and documents into an Obsidian-compatible vault, and Jev judges the top 30 local matches for each question in parallel so the agent gets ranked, relevant sources in about 0.3 s. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/kormem.md).
- [L2S1](https://github.com/LuticaCANARD/L2S1) - Local GGUF chat-model → typed binary/choice/ordinal decisions (Rust CLI + Python/TS/C++ SDKs); independent of hosted Jev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/l2s1.md).
- [langgraph-jev](https://github.com/iroy2000/langgraph-jev) - LangGraph/LangChain integration for TypeSafe Jev decision API (distinct from blacksinisterx/jev-langgraph). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/langgraph-jev.md).
- [langgraph-regression-guard (LangGraph PR regression checks with node cassettes and a Jev KPI judge)](https://github.com/AMOHYZ/langgraph-regression-guard) - Early-stage regression guard for LangGraph agents: records each node's inputs and outputs as cassettes and replays them on every PR to separate root from propagated drift, scores KPIs with a typed Jev judge whose low-confidence verdicts defer to a human, and keeps an accept, reject or override review loop. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/langgraph-regression-guard.md).
- [language-plugin-dsh (DSH plugin that pins chat and artifact languages, with Jev as an optional language judge)](https://github.com/farchanjo/language-plugin-dsh) - DeepSeek Harness (DSH) plugin that pins two languages independently: the chat reply language (by default it follows what you write) and the artifact language (identifiers, comments, docs, commits and PR bodies, `en-US` by default). Guards judge language with an offline stopword detector or Jev, which can name any language. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/language-plugin-dsh.md).
- [Laravel AI](https://github.com/laravel/ai) - Provides typed classification and a TypeSafe provider for Laravel applications, with fake responses for application testing. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/laravel-ai.md).
- [Lath](https://github.com/lath-agents/lath) - Agent loops with on-chain payment records where TypeSafe Jev, not the agent, decides when the goal is achieved. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/lath.md).
- [Laya for Home Assistant](https://github.com/allenporter/home-assistant-laya) - Fully local Home Assistant Assist conversation agent using open-weight Laya (sibling architecture to TypeSafe Jev HA; independent of hosted Jev). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/home-assistant-laya.md).
- [Laya Spring Boot Starter](https://github.com/kelsonthony/laya-springboot-starter) - Community Spring Boot 4 starter that auto-configures a `LayaClient` (Spring MVC + RestClient + Jackson 3) for self-hosted Laya's Jev-compatible `POST /v1/systemone`, with typed `choice`, `score` and `noul` questions and several decisions in one request; inspired by the Jev Spring Boot Starter but an original implementation. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/laya-springboot-starter.md).
- [Laya × Jev ticket classification](https://github.com/Mike-Animal-Counseling/laya-jev-lab) - Helpdesk ticket classification with a TypeSafe Jev engine or a local Laya model: Python SDK, REST service, optional shared inbox and Zendesk sync. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/laya-jev-lab.md).
- [laya-browser-agent](https://github.com/miniproaissa/laya-browser-agent) - In-browser Jev-like multi-backend agent with open checkpoints and Jev compatibility tests. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/laya-browser-agent.md).
- [laya-candle](https://github.com/Trystan-SA/laya-candle) - Pure-Rust Candle port of Laya for local typed Choice/Score/Noul decisions (no Python; independent of hosted Jev). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/laya-candle.md).
- [laya-compaction](https://github.com/bussolabs/laya-compaction) - Port of the listed fast-jev-compaction that swaps TypeSafe Jev for Laya, the open Jev-compatible decision model: a Claude Code plugin and npm library that replaces the compaction summary with per-item decisions — every tool call and result is scored, stale ones are dropped or truncated, and everything kept (including all user and assistant text) stays verbatim; Laya runs locally via a shared `laya-serve` or on your own server through `LAYA_URL`. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/laya-compaction.md).
- [laya-guardrails](https://github.com/javimp2003/laya-guardrails) - Self-hosted Laya System One guardrails (input/tool/output) for ES/PT agents—typed probabilities, deterministic policy; independent of hosted Jev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/laya-guardrails.md).
- [laya-jev-GraphRAG](https://github.com/bodepudimuneendra-netizen/laya-jev-GraphRAG) - Graph-DB-agnostic Agentic GraphRAG pipeline that uses swappable System One models (local Laya / cloud Jev) for Score/Noul/Choice across ingestion, traversal, and post-retrieval. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/laya-jev-graphrag.md).
- [laya-packet-analyser](https://github.com/esterhuizen/laya-packet-analyser) - Stdlib Python packet security analyser: deterministic detectors plus local Laya (Jev-compatible /v1/systemone) triage and live dashboard. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/laya-packet-analyser.md).
- [laya-php](https://github.com/marcreichel/laya-php) - PHP/Laravel SDK for local Laya typed Choice/Score/Noul decisions (Jev-compatible `/v1/systemone` alternative; self-hosted). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/laya-php.md).
- [laya-router](https://github.com/bussolabs/laya-router) - Port of gargpratyush/jev-router (listed) that takes per-turn model routing decisions from Laya — the open, Jev-compatible System One model — instead of TypeSafe Jev: `laya-claude` and `laya-codex` launch the real Claude Code / Codex CLIs behind a local proxy that sends simple turns to the fast tier and hard ones to the strong tier, running Laya locally with no API key or against your own `LAYA_URL` server. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/laya-router.md).
- [laya-skill](https://github.com/StevenLi-phoenix/laya-skill) - Claude Code skill/plugin for System One: local Laya (incl. WebGPU) or hosted TypeSafe Jev, plus fine-tune helpers. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/laya-skill.md).
- [laya-vs-jev](https://github.com/zaferayan/laya-vs-jev) - Head-to-head local Laya (MLX) / Ollaya vs TypeSafe Jev on ~900 cases across 3 tasks and 6 languages. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/zaferayan-laya-vs-jev.md).
- [layagrep (pipeline to train the open Laya model on jevgrep's code-relevance questions, using Jev as a teacher)](https://github.com/gak4u/layagrep) - Training pipeline aimed at running jevgrep locally: it records every question jevgrep sends to Jev (and Jev's answer) on SWE-bench Verified tasks, gives each question only the code it is about, labels questions from the fix and test diffs, mixes 0.7 x the SWE-bench label with 0.3 x Jev's probability, and fine-tunes the open Laya System 1 model. Laya serves the same `/v1/systemone` protocol, so a good checkpoint could replace hosted Jev with no key. Only a small smoke run is reported so far. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/layagrep.md).
- [layajev](https://github.com/metalagman/layajev) - Local Jev-compatible `/v1/systemone` server running verified Laya in-process (Go/ONNX; subset API). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/layajev.md).
- [ldraw-nova](https://github.com/anteloc/ldraw-nova) - Agent tooling that turns a model idea into an LDraw LEGO model; part and example-model search is re-ranked by TypeSafe Jev through jev-rerank (AGPL-3.0). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/ldraw-nova.md).
- [Leash](https://github.com/CMaintz/leash) - Jev-powered coding-agent guardrail that judges each turn against un-lintable project rules with a one-way accepted-debt ratchet. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/leash.md).
- [legends-jev (Jev decision, OCR and signage-verification adapter for the cto-legends agent toolkit)](https://github.com/avalonreset/legends-jev) - Dependency-free Python adapter in the cto-legends module set: given a short text observation and explicit candidate actions, or PNG and JPEG screenshots, documents or photos, it returns validated Choice, Score, Noul and Box answers from Jev (`jev-1.14.0`), including original-image pixel boxes; it captures and executes nothing. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/legends-jev.md).
- [leis (working rules as enforced laws for Claude Code)](https://github.com/leonardocandiani/leis) - Claude Code plugin (in Portuguese) that turns working rules into short named laws with a yes/no test, hooks that log every break, per-law observe or block modes and a measurement command, with Jev judging the checks that need judgement rather than a text rule. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/leis-claude-code.md).
- [letsplay2048 (decision models, including Jev, play 2048 live on OpenRouter)](https://github.com/mprpic/letsplay2048) - Code and a daily-refreshed copy of every game on letsplay2048.lol, where models on OpenRouter's Decisions API, including Jev 1.13 and 1.14, play 2048 live at one move every three seconds; the player skips the model when only one move is possible. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/letsplay2048.md).
- [lev (Abhinavexists)](https://github.com/Abhinavexists/lev) - Open Qwen3.5-4B LoRA System One decisions (Choice/Noul/Score) over `/v1/systemone`; independent of hosted Jev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/lev.md).
- [libsemop](https://github.com/jasonduncan/libsemop) - C++17 port of semantic-operators built on libtypesafe: write a typed semantic judgment once (Boolean, Choice, Score, enum), run it on TypeSafe Jev or another System One provider, and get "don't know" instead of a guess below a confidence threshold; filtering, reranking, cascades and benchmarks included. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/libsemop.md).
- [libtypesafe](https://github.com/jasonduncan/libtypesafe) - Unofficial C++17 client for the TypeSafe API with compile-time-checked answer types, non-blocking `Pending<T>` calls pumped from your loop, and pluggable HTTP transport. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/libtypesafe.md).
- [licgate](https://github.com/maromocooo/licgate) - Dependency license gate (TUI and CI CLI) for npm, Python, Go, Rust, PHP, and SBOMs that flags incompatibilities with your intended license; `--jev` sends license texts the built-in detector cannot place to Jev, keeping low-confidence answers as REVIEW. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/licgate.md).
- [Lichen](https://github.com/Mushroom-Systems/lichen) - Local API-compatible Jev/System One replacement (choice/noul/score + images) on open weights—independent of TypeSafe-hosted Jev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/lichen.md).
- [Lintel](https://github.com/Capzay/Lintel) - Change gate for coding agents: TypeSafe Jev reviews each proposed choice and you approve before code is written. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/lintel.md).
- [lintent](https://github.com/pietervp/lintent) - Plain-language lint rules judged by TypeSafe Jev, scoped with tree-sitter. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/lintent.md).
- [LiteLLM Jev classifier (route gateway requests by complexity with Jev)](https://github.com/BerriAI/litellm) - LiteLLM, the open-source gateway that calls 100+ LLM APIs in OpenAI format, can use Jev as the classifier in its complexity router: one typed choice call grades each incoming request into a tier, and the proxy sends it to the models you mapped to that tier. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/litellm-jev-classifier.md).
- [llama-index-jev](https://github.com/WiktorB2004/llama-index-jev) - Python integrations for LlamaIndex passage reranking and query-engine selection, with configurable error and selection behavior. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/llama-index-jev.md).
- [LLM Context Optimizer](https://github.com/enigma49/llm-context-optimizer) - TypeScript SDK that selects document context before OpenAI, Anthropic, or Gemini requests, with a local heuristic by default and TypeSafe Jev as an optional relevance backend. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/llm-context-optimizer.md).
- [LLM Debugger (VS Code)](https://github.com/mohsen1/llm-debugger-vscode-extension) - VS Code extension that finds bugs by driving the real debugger — breakpoints, stepping, live values — with Jev answering each step's 'what next?' and a generation model only for hypotheses and fixes. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/llm-debugger-vscode.md).
- [LLM vs Jev](https://github.com/codergarten/llmvsjev) - Teaching demo that races Gemini against TypeSafe Jev on the same 1,000 bank transactions into 12 categories, side by side, scored on speed and accuracy, with a no-key mock mode and a tiny Node proxy for the Jev key. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/llmvsjev.md).
- [llm-as-jev (Colab reproduction that fine-tunes Qwen3-0.6B into a Jev-style decision model)](https://github.com/hasnain1241/llm-as-jev) - Colab-friendly reproduction of the LLM-as-Jev recipe on `Qwen/Qwen3-0.6B` (full fine-tune, no LoRA), turning a causal LLM into a decision model that outputs a probability distribution over numbered options. In a single run the author reports macro ECE on six external sets falling from 0.262 to 0.118 and Banking77 accuracy rising from 0.185 to 0.603, and compares trends with the paper on 231 public JevBench items. Data and checkpoints are on Hugging Face. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/llm-as-jev.md).
- [llm-openrouter-decisions (LLM CLI plugin for Jev and other models on the OpenRouter Decisions API)](https://github.com/aborruso/llm-openrouter-decisions) - Plugin for Simon Willison's `llm` command line that registers every decision model on the OpenRouter Decisions API, Jev included, so you can ask yes/no, category, score, image box and multi-question requests from the shell or Python, classify CSV rows and save reusable templates. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/llm-openrouter-decisions.md).
- [llm-typesafe](https://github.com/simonw/llm-typesafe) - Simon Willison LLM CLI plugin for TypeSafe Jev noul/choice/score (`jev` / `typesafe/jev-latest`). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/llm-typesafe.md).
- [llmbridge](https://github.com/dragonlin-ai/llmbridge) - OpenAI-compatible LLM routing gateway with TypeSafe Jev as the L2 decision layer. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/llmbridge.md).
- [loadout](https://github.com/erik-hedstrom/loadout) - Picks the model tier, skills and MCP servers for each Claude Code or Codex session from a large library using Jev, then launches the CLI with that selection through official flags without proxying agent traffic. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/loadout.md).
- [Logit Classifier (local zero-shot classifier that accepts TypeSafe System One requests unchanged)](https://github.com/Blakeem/logit-classifier) - Local zero-shot classifier for text and images: one state and a set of questions go in, a probability per option comes out, read from a general model's logits at a single token position with no generated text. Its HTTP service accepts the System One request format, so a Jev request body works unchanged; the README is explicit that its numbers do not match Jev's. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/logit-classifier.md).
- [Luna Decision Model](https://github.com/fitz-s/luna-decision-model) - Local service that keeps Jev-shaped decision callers working against GPT-6 Luna through a Codex subscription. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/luna-decision-model.md).
- [Lynkgrids AI for Jev](https://github.com/lynkread/Lynkgrids-AI-for-Jev) - Question pack, CLI, agent skill and Claude Code plugin that make typed TypeSafe Jev decisions on a Lynkgrids LinkedIn-leads pipeline — lead fit, reply intent, draft check and meeting readiness — each one Jev request with an `act` / `review` / `human` route set by confidence. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/lynkgrids-ai-for-jev.md).
- [macos-computer-use-kit](https://github.com/Sur-Cai/macos-computer-use-kit) - AX-first macOS computer-use MCP/CLI with optional TypeSafe Jev semantic guards before irreversible actions. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/macos-computer-use-kit.md).
- [maddy-jev-filter (sort incoming mail for the maddy server into IMAP folders with Jev)](https://github.com/lesderid/maddy-jev-filter) - Mail filter for the maddy mail server: Jev answers a few questions about each incoming message (spam, phishing, like what you've put in Not interested) and rules in a config file turn the answers into a folder, such as Junk, Suspicious or Not interested, marked read or unread. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/maddy-jev-filter.md).
- [maibot-jev-gate (MaiBot plugin: Jev pre-judges ambiguous group-chat messages)](https://github.com/HaoxiangXia/maibot-jev-gate) - MaiBot plugin that asks Jev about group-chat messages only when the host's local reply gate is undecided, blends Jev's reply probability with the local one weighted by Jev's confidence, and can mark background chatter as read without waking the planner; multiple provider profiles can be saved and switched in the WebUI; Chinese documentation. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/maibot-jev-gate.md).
- [mail-classifier (Outlook)](https://github.com/MichaelRingelstein/mail-classifier) - Local Outlook triage app: TypeSafe Jev classifies unread mail, then you confirm flag/file/trash actions. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/ringelstein-mail-classifier.md).
- [MakerAi](https://github.com/gustavoeenriquez/MakerAi) - Native Delphi AI framework (agents, RAG, MCP, multi-provider chat) with `TAiJev` and eight Jev adapters for routing, tool/input guardrails, RAG reranking, eval scoring, bulk labeling and model routing — hosted Jev or Ollama `/v1/systemone` decision models. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/makerai.md).
- [Malkuth](https://github.com/newfull5/malkuth) - Open-weight multilingual System One–style decision models (Korean focus) served via Kev `/v1/systemone`. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/malkuth.md).
- [Master-JEV Hook](https://github.com/KeepGlide/master-jev-hook) - Local gateway and MCP server that lets Claude Code, Claude Desktop and Codex hand explicit choices to Jev (which approach, source or next action; classify, verify, score or rank a batch), with Claude Code hooks for risky-command and unverified-completion spot checks and a local query dashboard. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/master-jev-hook.md).
- [Mastering TypeSafe Jev (book code)](https://github.com/udooz/mastering-jev-and-kev-book) - Companion repositories and mini-projects for the Mastering TypeSafe Jev book, including triage and rerank labs with Jev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/mastering-jev-book.md).
- [mayi](https://github.com/AidenHadisi/mayi) - Permission gate for Claude Code/Cursor/Codex: TypeSafe Jev scores tool calls; dialog on unsafe (errors deny). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/mayi.md).
- [mcp-agent-openjev](https://github.com/ChristofMilius/mcp-agent-openjev) - Local OpenJev Choice/Noul/Score decision service for agents via MCP + CLI (LM Studio logprobs; not TypeSafe-hosted). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/christofmilius-mcp-agent-openjev.md).
- [Mechanical Jev](https://github.com/Lasimeri/Mechanical-Jev) - Rust client/`mjev` CLI/eval harness for System One Noul/Choice/Score against local Intel Phi Jev or compatible `/v1/systemone`. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/mechanical-jev.md).
- [meilisearch-jev](https://github.com/gbesse/meilisearch-jev) - Meilisearch companion CLI that gates top hits with a Jev yes/no question and splits accepted hits from a review queue. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/meilisearch-jev.md).
- [meiseki](https://github.com/bamboo-nova/meiseki) - Japanese clarity-rewrite Agent Skill; optional TypeSafe Jev layer settles contextual judgments before the LLM (v0.9.0+). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/meiseki.md).
- [mem (instinctual-memory)](https://github.com/sahilshingate01/instinctual-memory) - Git-first durable memory for coding agents: turns past Claude Code, Codex and OpenCode sessions and your AGENTS.md files into sourced facts in a plain Git repo, searchable over MCP, with Jev reranking search results. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/instinctual-memory.md).
- [memory-gate](https://github.com/jsilva74/memory-gate) - Claude Code project memory that keeps only facts still true (SQLite, revalidated on use), plus an injected-context mod where Jev answers one yes/no per rule file to decide which long domain rules enter the prompt. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/memory-gate.md).
- [memsearch](https://github.com/zilliztech/memsearch) - Persistent, Markdown-backed memory layer for Claude Code, Codex, DeepSeek Harness, OpenClaw and OpenCode (Milvus index), with optional TypeSafe Jev reranking that scores every recalled candidate in one `/v1/systemone` request; off by default and enabled only in global config. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/memsearch.md).
- [MetaDecision-31B-Rain (self-hosted /v1/systemone decision service built for JevBench)](https://github.com/chenyuwang166-oss/MetaDecision-31B-Rain) - Self-hosted decision service that serves `POST /v1/systemone` for the unchanged `typesafe` adapter of the official JevBench harness, using the pinned MeTask-Rain Gemma-4-31B LoRA weights. The entry changes only how the service uses them: a drafting pass for a share of decisions with a 9 s deadline, order-reversed re-reads for close choices, per-type post-calibration and a cost fuse. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/metadecision-31b-rain.md).
- [metajev](https://github.com/YYTbit/metajev) - Cache the decision, compute the policy: record System One distributions under state/question/model keys; change thresholds without re-running Jev (MIT). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/metajev.md).
- [Metis](https://github.com/Ayush0054/metis) - GitHub Action and Python CLI that triage new issues with TypeSafe Jev category labels and missing-detail follow-ups. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/metis.md).
- [Micdrop](https://github.com/Godefroy/micdrop) - TypeScript voice conversation SDK (browser/RN/Node) with official `@micdrop/typesafe` Jev turn classifier (BYOK). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/micdrop.md).
- [Midscene JEV Runner](https://github.com/KiritoKing/midscene-jev-runner) - Community Midscene/Playwright runner: TypeSafe Jev (OpenRouter Decisions) chooses browser ops on a caller-owned page. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/midscene-jev-runner.md).
- [Milvus Model](https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus-model) - Python reranker adapter that sends candidate documents as Jev Noul questions in one request, then sorts the returned scores and preserves original document indices. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/milvus-model.md).
- [MinGuard (paper artifacts for data-minimization guards on LLM text-to-SQL agents, with Jev as a guard)](https://github.com/tusu-code/Minguard) - Code, workloads, guard scores and human labels for the MinGuard paper (ICDE 2027 submission) on calibrated checks that stop an LLM database agent from returning more personal data than a request needs. Guards combine a deterministic SQL fact layer with single-fact questions answered by hosted Jev 1.13, the open JEV-27B, LLM judges and small classifiers, under conformal risk control. All scores are stored, so tables and figures rebuild without API keys. Code comments partly in Chinese. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/minguard.md).
- [MiniCPM-OCR (open OCR models from OpenBMB, with a 2B Jev-style decision model that scores OCR correctness)](https://github.com/OpenBMB/MiniCPM-OCR) - Open-source document parsing from OpenBMB: a 1.3B OCR model for text, tables and formulas, plus MiniCPM-OCR Jev, a 2B decision model trained from Qwen3.5-2B that returns a 0-1 quality score for an OCR result against the original region image. It can pick the best of several OCR models' outputs per region and flag doubtful tables and formulas for human review before they enter a RAG index. OpenBMB reports 93.6 AUROC on its JevOCRBench. Weights and code under Apache-2.0. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/minicpm-ocr-jev.md).
- [minifactory](https://github.com/juan-campuzano/minifactory) - Small pluggable software factory where declarative YAML workflows of plugin steps fence AI agents in with budgets and real gates; its default router asks Jev which workflow fits a plain-language request. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/minifactory.md).
- [minijev (a Jev-style typed decision in about 50 lines from a small local LLM's next-token probabilities)](https://github.com/hisuzuya/minijev) - Experiment and lightning talk (AI-driven development Osaka, 29 October 2026): reproduce Jev's request shape (state plus named noul, choice and score questions) by reading a small local LLM's next-token probabilities for lettered options, then compare it with the real Jev and Opus 5.5 on 300 public questions and operational metrics. Slides included; Japanese docs. Not affiliated with TypeSafe. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/minijev.md).
- [Ministry of Truth](https://github.com/LukasPodhorny/ministry-of-truth) - Spin-doctor web game: over a 15-day campaign you write cover stories in free text and five literal-minded Jev factions score every word — affect, credibility, salience, compliance, narrative fit — fully playable offline with a mock ensemble or live through a local key-holding proxy. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/ministry-of-truth.md).
- [Minos.NET](https://github.com/MarcelRoozekrans/Minos.NET) - Unofficial .NET client for Jev `/v1/systemone` (direct or OpenRouter) with source-generated, Native AOT-friendly typed questions. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/minos-net.md).
- [Misogi](https://github.com/nadsous/Misogi) - Second-opinion sidecar for Claude Code/Codex/Kimi: TypeSafe Jev judges each "done" claim before you accept it. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/misogi.md).
- [MM3](https://github.com/mvp-scale/mm3) - Memory and decision layer for coding agents (Claude Code plugin, npm `@mvpscale/mm3`, CLI) that wraps TypeSafe Jev: the agent asks batches of yes/no and decision questions about a scoped piece of code and gets calibrated answers recorded in a ledger. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/mm3.md).
- [mnemon-memory-agent](https://github.com/Grivn/mnemon-memory-agent) - Agent long-term memory judged with TypeSafe Jev System One over raw records. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/mnemon-memory-agent.md).
- [mobai-ci](https://github.com/MobAI-App/mobai-ci) - Run MobAI `.mob` / Maestro mobile UI flows in CI; `.mobflow` steps are judged/acted by TypeSafe Jev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/mobai-ci.md).
- [Mobile Jev](https://github.com/droidrun/mobile-jev) - Selects Android actions and exact input text through Mobilerun, with a local studio and task-specific verification demo. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/mobile-jev.md).
- [mobile-jev × AndroidWorld (Python port of mobile-jev run on the 116-task AndroidWorld benchmark)](https://github.com/fclab-skku/jev-using-mobile-androidworld) - Research port of droidrun/mobile-jev to AndroidWorld's action space. Each step turns the accessibility tree into a state and asks Jev, through OpenRouter, which operation to run and which element to target; a small chat model writes typed text and answers. Reported single runs: 37 of 116 tasks (31.9%) with `jev-1.13` and 39 (33.6%) with `jev-1.14`, about $0.25 per full run. Results, steps and run guide included. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/mobile-jev-androidworld.md).
- [Model Picker by Tatendaz (Jev model and effort suggestions for Codex and Claude Code)](https://github.com/Tatendaz/model-picker) - Plugin for Codex and Claude Code that, as a task grows harder, asks Jev which of the host's models and effort levels fit the work and shows the recommendation in chat; it never changes a setting, keeps its state local and uses only the Python standard library. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/tatendaz-model-picker.md).
- [Model Quality Radar (DeepSeek Harness plugin with optional Jev scoring)](https://github.com/lishinho/dsh-quality-lens) - DeepSeek Harness plugin that shows a quality and topic-coverage score on every answer, with an evidence-backed report and copy-ready prompt fixes; optional Jev mode scores five dimensions and checks coverage in one request. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/dsh-quality-lens.md).
- [model-router (Claude Code)](https://github.com/Akurganow/model-router) - Claude Code mod that asks TypeSafe Jev one Score question about each redacted subagent task before it starts and sets the subagent model to haiku, sonnet or opus; `suggest` mode only logs, and the hook runs no commands and writes no files. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/akurganow-model-router.md).
- [model-router (muhx)](https://github.com/muhx/model-router) - Claude Code mod that picks the model and reasoning effort for each task with Jev — subagent model at spawn, main-loop effort per turn, and (on by default in this fork) main-loop model — using TypeSafe's `/v1/systemone` (`jev-latest`) or the Vercel AI Gateway, with a risky-task `noul`, confidence-aware policy and a built-in fallback classifier. A derivative of claude-code-templates' `jev-model-router` (also listed) under MIT. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/muhx-model-router.md).
- [model-router for Pi (Jev sizes each message and routes small jobs to cheaper models, falling back when unsure)](https://github.com/DarkLvrd/model-router) - Pi coding-agent extension: before each message, Jev answers three questions in one request (smallest model size that can do the job, is the request clear, is it self-contained) and the session switches to the mapped model only when all three pass; any timeout, error or doubt keeps the default model. Ships switched off; a 2.5 s cap per call and a status command that shows routing and Jev cost. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/darklvrd-pi-model-router.md).
- [model-router-python](https://github.com/TheCoder30ec4/model_router_python) - Zero-dependency Python router: hard limit filters, then TypeSafe Jev chooses the cheapest capable model id. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/model-router-python.md).
- [model-selector (Claude Code subagent model picker)](https://github.com/menoncello/model-selector) - Claude Code PreToolUse hook that sends each subagent's task text to Jev before it runs and fills in the model (haiku, sonnet or opus) and effort, for both the Agent tool and `agent()` calls inside workflows, with a cost report over your transcripts. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/model-selector-claude-code.md).
- [ModelRudder](https://github.com/cybrking/modelrudder) - Free local community preview that adds model routing to the native Codex terminal: your own TypeSafe Jev key classifies each request so the strongest model is spent only where needed, while Codex keeps its tools, approvals and workspace permissions; observe, auto and pinned modes, checksum-verified release installer. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/modelrudder.md).
- [modelwire (async Python clients for the OpenAI Responses, Anthropic Messages and System One APIs)](https://github.com/eyayaw/modelwire) - Small async Python library with clients for the OpenAI Responses, Anthropic Messages and TypeSafe System One APIs behind one retry and error model, plus servers compatible with them for testing. `SystemOne(...).ask(model, state, questions)` returns typed answers from Jev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/modelwire.md).
- [modernbert-jev (Jev-class typed decisions on one CPU with a ModernBERT NLI model, TypeSafe-compatible)](https://github.com/KSonny4/modernbert-jev) - Tiny decision-model stack that answers choice, score and yes/no questions about text or JSON on one CPU with 2 GB of RAM: a ModernBERT-base NLI cross-encoder where entailment probability is the option's probability, plus distillation, a library, CLI and an HTTP server that speaks TypeSafe's `POST /v1/systemone`, so existing clients switch by base URL. A working preview; numbers are the author's local measurements. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/modernbert-jev.md).
- [mods (iuliandita)](https://github.com/iuliandita/mods) - Claude Code mods you can install one at a time, including optional TypeSafe Jev client injection. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/iuliandita-mods.md).
- [monet_gdt (5.5 MB on-device wallpaper model for mood, colors and shapes, trained on Jev labels)](https://github.com/Telosnex/monet_gdt) - On-device wallpaper model for libmonet apps: from a wallpaper it scores 10 mood words, picks a three-colour theme and scores 29 decorative shapes in one 5.55 MB ONNX graph (10-15 ms on one Apple-silicon core). Training labels come from Jev's vision questions on CC0, public-domain or Apache-generated images, distilled through a teacher into a small student. Weights on Hugging Face. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/monet-gdt.md).
- [monitor-calibration (how well Jev and other models report confidence when flagging harmful chatbot prompts)](https://github.com/sampan501/monitor-calibration) - Study of confidence quality on a safety-monitor task: a TF-IDF baseline, local open-weight models, commercial APIs, Claude and Jev each give P(harmful) for prompts from ToxicChat and WildGuardTest, compared on AUROC, Brier, ECE, risk-coverage and catch rate at a fixed audit budget. Jev is read natively through the TypeSafe SDK. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/monitor-calibration.md).
- [Moongate](https://github.com/brickfrog/moongate) - GitHub Action that evaluates committed diffs against JSON semantic rules with TypeSafe Jev and reports annotations from your thresholds. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/moongate.md).
- [Moreno.Jev](https://github.com/MorenoLand/Moreno.Jev) - Cross-platform MCP server and agent skill for TypeSafe Jev structured code review and debugging (review_code / review_diff tools). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/morenoland-moreno-jev.md).
- [Mori (RAG with whole-source context and Jev ranking)](https://github.com/tiagovasc/mori-rag) - RAG for books, articles, lectures and podcasts that keeps the argument around a passage: semantic search finds 500 passages, Jev ranks relevance down to 100, and the answer packet holds one complete source unit, five summaries and the selected passages, with every run saved for audit. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/mori-rag.md).
- [Movo](https://github.com/truehannan/movo) - Linux (Debian-family) desktop computer-use agent: AT-SPI observation, typed TypeSafe Jev decisions, floating PySide6 panel. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/movo.md).
- [mrouter (local and cloud model router)](https://github.com/radioboxtv/mrouter) - Zero-dependency Python proxy that routes OpenAI-compatible requests across LM Studio models and priced cloud gateways, using Jev to classify each prompt by use case and forcing sensitive prompts onto local models. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/mrouter.md).
- [mu](https://github.com/qybaihe/mu) - Early-stage coding agent built on pi (CLI + desktop app) with a judgment kernel: Jev answers typed questions at decision points such as tool approval, constraint checks, context admission and test-log trimming, each switchable active/shadow/off and logged to a verdict ledger. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/mu.md).
- [Mule 4 TypeSafe Connector](https://github.com/MuleSoft-Forge/mule4-typesafe-connector) - Mule 4 connector for TypeSafe System One / Jev typed noul/choice/score answers that drive Mule flows. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/mule4-typesafe-connector.md).
- [multi-modal-jev-browser](https://github.com/foklepoint/multi-modal-jev-browser) - Browser agent (CLI `mmjb`, Python library and 13-tool MCP server) where a decision model picks each step — Jev via OpenRouter reading the element list, or Clef reading a numbered screenshot — and a vision LLM takes over only when the decision model is unsure, claims nothing can move, or the page stops changing; writes are off by default. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/multi-modal-jev-browser.md).
- [mushy-lint](https://github.com/napisani/mushy-lint) - Semantic code linter that extracts complete constructs with tree-sitter, asks Jev (TypeSafe or OpenRouter) narrow yes/no questions about them, and reports the raw probability of yes with the source evidence — for conventions that depend on meaning rather than syntax. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/mushy-lint.md).
- [mysql-ailike](https://github.com/maayanlevy/mysql-ailike) - MySQL native `AILIKE` / `ailike` UDFs for natural-language row filters and joins via TypeSafe Jev (Linux plugin; GPL-2.0). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/mysql-ailike.md).
- [N+1 Bench (can Jev spot N+1 queries in Django REST endpoints just by reading the code?)](https://github.com/RoRep1ay/django-n-1-jev) - Benchmark of 58 small Django REST Framework list endpoints, some with N+1 query problems and some that only look like it, with ground truth measured by serving each one at 5 and 10 rows. Jev and Claude Code (with a bundled audit skill) review the code without running it; the author reports Jev at about 91% accuracy for $0.03 across 174 checks versus Claude at 100% for $7.56–$14.95, and a CI check script. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/django-n-1-jev.md).
- [n8n-nodes-jeff](https://github.com/saembit/n8n-nodes-jeff) - Unofficial n8n community node for Jev with ready-made decisions (rank a list, triage a message, guard text before an LLM, match two records, verify a claim against a source) plus a plain Ask operation. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/n8n-nodes-jeff.md).
- [n8n-nodes-jev](https://github.com/vibe-with-me-tools/n8n-nodes-jev) - n8n community node for TypeSafe Jev: classify, route, and score workflow text (distinct from khmuhtadin and @typesafe-ai packages). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/n8n-nodes-jev.md).
- [n8n-nodes-system-one](https://github.com/diegohh0411/n8n-nodes-system-one) - n8n community node for System One decision models across providers — TypeSafe Jev directly, Jev via OpenRouter (no TypeSafe account needed), Cloudflare Workers AI Clef, or any custom `/v1/systemone` endpoint — with typed questions and flat probability outputs for IF/Switch nodes. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/n8n-nodes-system-one.md).
- [n8n-nodes-typesafe](https://github.com/Biztactix/n8n-nodes-typesafe) - n8n community node for TypeSafe System One noul/choice/score questions over workflow text or JSON. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/n8n-nodes-typesafe.md).
- [n8n-nodes-typesafe-ai](https://github.com/typesafe-ai/n8n-nodes-typesafe-ai) - Official TypeSafe AI n8n community nodes for System One Evaluate/Route (Jev and other System One models); distinct from Biztactix/khmuhtadin community packages. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/n8n-nodes-typesafe-ai.md).
- [NanoJev](https://github.com/TianyuCodings/NanoJev) - Studies independent Qwen-based typed decision heads, local serving, and game controllers with recorded comparisons. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/nanojev.md).
- [naturalcodz](https://github.com/SuparvaCode/naturalcodz) - npm natural-logic helpers (`is`/`pick`/`rate`/`classify`/guards) powered by TypeSafe Jev with confidence thresholds. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/naturalcodz.md).
- [Navigator (qf-studio)](https://github.com/qf-studio/navigator) - Context-engineering plugin for Claude Code (loop mode, research, memories, live `/nav` pane) whose keyword prompt gates can get an optional typed TypeSafe Jev judge — one request per prompt, decisive answers override, the rest fall back to keywords. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/qf-studio-navigator.md).
- [neo4jev](https://github.com/jexp/neo4jev) - Explores Neo4j paths using next-hop choices and goal judgments, with notebooks and a Streamlit interface. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/neo4jev.md).
- [neuras (conversational agent experiment where Jev decides and an LLM writes)](https://github.com/saembit/neuras) - Experiment in splitting judgment from generation: a local Ollama LLM only writes candidate notes and replies, while one Jev request per turn appraises the message (correction, worth a note, hostile, affect scores, contradictions with stored notes) and picks among candidates, keeping a persistent self file and traces with ablations to score the effect. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/neuras.md).
- [NeuroLink](https://github.com/juspay/neurolink) - TypeScript multi-provider AI SDK with a `decide`/`tryDecide` inference type powered by TypeSafe Jev for routing and gating. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/neurolink.md).
- [nf-jev](https://github.com/nextflow-io/nf-jev) - Nextflow plugin exposing TypeSafe Jev noul/choice/score as pipeline functions (beta). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/nf-jev.md).
- [nifi-jev](https://github.com/gbesse/nifi-jev) - Apache NiFi RouteWithJev processor: TypeSafe Jev semantic yes/no routing with review and failure lanes. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/nifi-jev.md).
- [Nirnay (CPU-only decision model compatible with the Jev / Laya /v1/systemone format)](https://github.com/nandukmelath/nirnay) - Independent small decision model that classifies, rates and answers yes or no about text or JSON on CPU in one pass, with calibrated probabilities and a server that speaks the Jev / Laya `/v1/systemone` wire format; the README compares it with Laya checkpoints and with Jev's published figures. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/nirnay.md).
- [node-decision-model](https://github.com/fraserxu/node-decision-model) - Zero-dependency Node.js client and CLI for decision models: TypeSafe Jev via OpenRouter or the native TypeSafe API, plus Cloudflare Clef, with typed noul/choice/score questions. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/node-decision-model.md).
- [Noma](https://github.com/blackdromeai-labs/noma) - Noma (Blackdrome AI Labs): open-weight MPL-2.0 System One decision model served by `pip install blackdrome-noma` / `noma serve` on the same `/v1/systemone` API as Jev, returning per-option probabilities plus abstain and uncertainty signals, with a paper on Zenodo. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/noma.md).
- [Note Filer](https://github.com/someka-vrc/obsidian-note-filer) - Obsidian plugin that classifies notes with TypeSafe Jev and moves them into Thema/IAB taxonomy folders after review. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/obsidian-note-filer.md).
- [Noullama (local desktop workbench and API for Jev-style decision models on llama.cpp)](https://github.com/parasite6/noullama) - Desktop app for running native decision models locally: download a GGUF from the Hub tab or load your own, start llama.cpp from the Runtime tab, try noul, choice and score questions in a Playground with saved presets, and connect apps to a local API whose `/v1/systemone` route follows Jev's request format. Does not call TypeSafe. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/noullama.md).
- [NucleiSniper](https://github.com/MorDavid/NucleiSniper) - Security-testing pipeline that fingerprints each authorised target and has TypeSafe Jev score every Nuclei template's relevance (0–4) in batches, then runs Nuclei with all templates ordered highest-relevance first, so likely findings surface early; scans are resumable. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/nucleisniper.md).
- [Nudge](https://github.com/NekomyaDev/nudge) - Typed, replayable, budget-aware language for LLM agents that compiles to Python/TypeScript and includes `decide` blocks targeting `/v1/systemone` Jev-family servers. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/nudge.md).
- [NullPrime (a negative-control dataset for models that claim to know when they don't, tested on Jev and LLMs)](https://github.com/bmiedlar/nullprime) - Dataset of integers labelled prime or composite where the only learnable surface pattern has been removed, so any model should be at chance: a negative control for uncertainty, abstention and calibration claims. Includes a certificate run with standard learners, an LLM study and a Jev evaluation using a Noul probability and a Choice with an abstain option. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/nullprime.md).
- [obsidian-task-scan (find Obsidian tasks a swamp extension could do, using Jev)](https://github.com/puddle-works/obsidian-task-scan) - Swamp extension that lists the open tasks in an Obsidian vault (skipping templates and daily notes), withholds any that look like they contain a secret, and asks Jev whether a swamp extension could do each one, marking tasks candidate (at 0.7 confidence or more), not_candidate, withheld or unclassified; runs on a schedule or on demand. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/obsidian-task-scan.md).
- [oc-one-guard (OpenCode plugin that asks a System One judge before tool calls that could leak data)](https://github.com/HumanFace-Tech/oc-one-guard) - OpenCode v1 plugin that checks proposed tool calls for possible data exfiltration (uploading keys, dumping environment variables, staging a script that sends files out). Local checks let ordinary calls through; calls that need review go to a System One judge as one `noul` question, and configurable bands allow, hold for review, deny or stop the run. Reviewed calls block when the judge is offline. A heuristic guardrail, not a firewall. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/oc-one-guard.md).
- [OCR Gateway](https://github.com/alvarovillalbaa/ocr-gateway) - Self-hosted document parsing gateway for TypeScript, Python, React and agent tools that routes PDFs, scans, images and office files across hosted OCR and vision providers or local parsers, using Jev to choose providers from metadata and returning location citations and embedding-ready chunks. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/ocr-gateway.md).
- [Octolib](https://github.com/Muvon/octolib) - One Rust API for 30+ AI providers (chat, tools, structured output, embeddings, reranking, media) whose `evaluation` feature asks TypeSafe Jev typed noul/choice/score questions — directly (`typesafe:jev-latest`) or billed through Cloudflare AI Gateway (`cloudflare:typesafe/jev`). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/octolib.md).
- [oh-my-jev (apetcu)](https://github.com/apetcu/oh-my-jev) - oh-my-pi plugin: TypeSafe Jev tool-call gate (default), optional model router, and latency telemetry — distinct from MassiveLabsNet/oh-my-jev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/apetcu-oh-my-jev.md).
- [olla-jev](https://github.com/nvkudva/olla-jev) - Ollama-style local CLI/server for Hugging Face System One models behind the Jev /v1/systemone API. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/olla-jev.md).
- [Ollaya](https://github.com/ollaya-dev/ollaya) - 'Ollama for decision models': one Rust binary that pulls open decision models by name (Laya, winnow, decider, NLI, GLiClass), serves them from a local daemon and speaks TypeSafe's `/v1/systemone`, `/v1/decisions` and `/v1/models` wire format, so existing Jev clients and the official SDK work by pointing `TYPESAFE_BASE_URL` at localhost. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/ollaya.md).
- [omo-jev-plugin](https://github.com/brianhong-dev/omo-jev-plugin) - OmO / senpi plugin: TypeSafe Jev advises on skill fit, next tools, loops, and completion (shadow/advise/act modes; does not replace permissions). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/omo-jev-plugin.md).
- [OMP Jev Auto Mode](https://github.com/suzuuuuu09/omp-jev-auto-mode) - Experimental Jev tool-call guard for Oh My Pi adapted from pi-jev-auto-mode, defaulting to ask on uncertainty and blocking escalations without a UI. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/omp-jev-auto-mode.md).
- [omp-jev-compaction](https://github.com/jerryfane/omp-jev-compaction) - omp plugin: verbatim TypeSafe/OpenRouter Jev-scored tool-context reduction with sticky cache-friendly rewrites (distinct from fast-jev-compaction / jev-compact). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/omp-jev-compaction.md).
- [omp-jev-tier](https://github.com/charliemartin0/omp-jev-tier) - Extension for the omp coding agent that uses omp's native judge role (normally `typesafe/jev-latest`) for subagent model tiering and automatic plan mode, without patching omp or changing configured model roles, fallbacks or usage reserve; it refuses judgments from non-Jev judge models. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/omp-jev-tier.md).
- [omp-maestro (oh-my-pi setup in one command, with a Jev Router choosing models)](https://github.com/hsmoviich/omp-maestro) - One-command oh-my-pi setup with curated public plugins and skills, three switchable model ensembles and the author's own Jev Router extension, which asks Jev which model should take each request so that, in the author's words, Claude thinks, GPT codes and Jev decides who plays. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/omp-maestro.md).
- [OneJev](https://github.com/OmniJev/OneJev) - Multimodal System One decision model (text/image/video → calibrated option probabilities in one forward pass); independent of official Jev; distinct from listed Jev-Omni and PlayJev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/onejev.md).
- [onesie](https://github.com/frodi-karlsson/onesie) - Unix-pipeable System One CLI for TypeSafe Jev (also OpenRouter/Berget): pipe text, ask a typed question, script the answer. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/frodi-karlsson-onesie.md).
- [Onion or Not?](https://github.com/andrisgauracs/onion-or-not) - Local web app that asks whether a headline is Onion satire or real news and shows three answers side by side: Jev, AnyJev L0 over a local Qwen3-8B, and raw Qwen3-8B, with an option to swap the answer order. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/onion-or-not.md).
- [ontoken-auto](https://github.com/priaculun/ontoken-auto) - Pi package `ontoken/auto`: TypeSafe Jev classifies each prompt and picks an OnToken model slot and thinking level, with mid-turn promotion. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/ontoken-auto.md).
- [ontoken-auto-omp](https://github.com/priaculun/ontoken-auto-omp) - Native OMP extension that routes prompts across OnToken model slots with TypeSafe Jev classification (distinct from ontoken-auto). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/ontoken-auto-omp.md).
- [Open Alternative to Jev](https://github.com/ikermoel/open-alternative-jev) - Studies typed decisions from open models using packed or separate inference and optional calibration; independent of TypeSafe Jev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/open-alternative-jev.md).
- [Open Jev Bridge](https://github.com/louis-szeto/open-jev-bridge) - Zero-dependency Node MCP + Claude Code/Codex hooks for hosted Jev or local Kev/Laya System One (compaction and completion gates). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/open-jev-bridge.md).
- [Open Medical Jev](https://github.com/FeiLiuEM/open-medical-jev) - Jev-class medical judgments from frozen open models (no fine-tuning): dual-reader fusion, conformal routing, and published comparisons vs hosted Jev on licensing exams. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/open-medical-jev.md).
- [Open Memory (pi)](https://github.com/codersdfs/open-memory) - Local-first Pi agent memory where an optional TypeSafe Jev judge scores which sentences stay useful. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/open-memory-pi.md).
- [Open Systemone (CLIProxyAPI plugin that routes System One decision requests like Jev to local or hosted nodes)](https://github.com/ducdao98z/cpa-plugin-systemone) - Plugin for CLIProxyAPI that adds System One decision nodes: a `{model, state, questions}` request goes to the node chosen by the model prefix and its answer comes back unchanged. Nodes can be local servers or hosted lanes (OpenCode, OpenRouter and others) with several keys, headers and per-node proxies; a management page checks latency and builds test decisions, and a Hugging Face tab lists models from the Jev Decision Index and downloads them. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/cpa-plugin-systemone.md).
- [Open WebUI Router (Jev boilerplate)](https://github.com/foteev/open-webui-router-jev-boilerplate) - Single-file Open WebUI router pipe that asks Jev which specialist model should answer, with sticky follow-ups, prefix overrides and a clarification fallback. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/open-webui-router-jev-boilerplate.md).
- [open-jev](https://github.com/nico-martin/open-jev) - Runs independent Kev and DeBERTa typed-decision models locally through Transformers.js, with browser WebGPU/WASM support; does not use official Jev weights. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/open-jev.md).
- [open-jev-analysis (study of Open-Jev 2B and 9B for safety classification, guardrails and search reranking)](https://github.com/iosifidisvasileios/open-jev-analysis) - Research code testing how well the open Open-Jev 2B and 9B decision models handle six safety targets (prompt injection, harmful requests, toxicity, jailbreaks, XSTest, moderation categories) and BEIR reranking on top of BM25 and EmbeddingGemma 2. Reports accuracy, per-class recall, false positives, calibration and latency; the author finds task-dependent strengths and cases where high accuracy hides poor detection of rare positives. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/open-jev-analysis.md).
- [open-source-finder](https://github.com/Tanishk901/open-source-finder) - Rank a GitHub repo's open issues for good-first-issue fit with TypeSafe Jev (size, clarity, project knowledge, claim flags). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/open-source-finder.md).
- [openai-decisions-vs-jev-vs-laya](https://github.com/mateusfonsek/openai-decisions-vs-jev-vs-laya) - Independent Portuguese-language benchmark of decision models — OpenAI Decisions API (`gpt-6-luna`), TypeSafe Jev (`jev-1.13.0`) and local Laya (with option rotation) — on agent tool routing, prompt-injection detection and LLM-as-judge in PT-BR: 450 cases per provider (2026-10-07), with accuracy, macro-F1, ECE, latency with and without network, and cost per 1k. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/openai-decisions-vs-jev-vs-laya.md).
- [OpenChoice-9B (CPU-first open decision model with a System One-compatible API)](https://github.com/lutr0/openchoice) - Open decision model built from Qwen3.5-9B plus a decision LoRA, merged to GGUF (about 5.8 GiB with the vision projector) and served on CPU: it returns probabilities for Choice, Score and Noul questions over a wire format compatible with TypeSafe's SDK, reads long inputs, and ships a LoRA fine-tuning kit that exports back to the same server. Not affiliated with TypeSafe; official JevBench scores pending. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/openchoice-9b.md).
- [openclaw-jev (Hyper-AI-Lab)](https://github.com/Hyper-AI-Lab/openclaw-jev) - OpenClaw control plane: intake routing with TypeSafe Jev (≠ yousan openclaw-jev-* plugins). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/hyper-ai-lab-openclaw-jev.md).
- [openclaw-jev-leakguard](https://github.com/yousan/openclaw-jev-leakguard) - OpenClaw outgoing-message guard: TypeSafe Jev (or local Kev) blocks client/credential leaks by destination tier. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/openclaw-jev-leakguard.md).
- [openclaw-jev-trigger](https://github.com/yousan/openclaw-jev-trigger) - OpenClaw automations: plain-language triggers judged by TypeSafe Jev each tick so the model wakes only when it matters. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/openclaw-jev-trigger.md).
- [openclaw-typesafe-ai](https://github.com/Olli0103/openclaw-typesafe-ai) - OpenClaw plugin registering optional `typesafe_decide` for explicit TypeSafe Jev decisions (SecretRef credentials; no hooks). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/openclaw-typesafe-ai.md).
- [OpenClaw.NET](https://github.com/clawdotnet/openclaw.net) - Self-hosted .NET AI agent runtime with desktop companion; includes opt-in TypeSafe Jev decision routing (shadow mode first, safety floors, fallback to the existing router). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/openclaw-net.md).
- [OpenCode Decisions plugin](https://github.com/matthewpetela/opencode-decisions-plugin) - OpenCode V2 plugin exposing a `decisions_evaluate` tool so an agent can ask typed questions of Jev (text, through OpenCode Console's Jev models) or OpenAI Decisions (text and images); the agent keeps the taxonomy and interprets the probabilities. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/opencode-decisions-plugin.md).
- [OpenCode Security Guard](https://github.com/koppert/opencode-security-guard) - Linux OpenCode shell permission guard: local check + TypeSafe Jev read-only score ≥0.90 for auto-allow. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/opencode-security-guard.md).
- [opencode-auto-classifier](https://github.com/schemaitat/opencode-auto-classifier) - OpenCode plugin that wraps shell, read, and edit tools with TypeSafe Jev classifications folded into permission decisions. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/opencode-auto-classifier.md).
- [opencode-auto-effort](https://github.com/justmytwospence/opencode-auto-effort) - opencode plugin where TypeSafe Jev rates, in about 200 ms before each turn, how much careful reasoning a prompt needs (0–3) and sets opencode's model variant (low → xhigh) accordingly; cache-safe and sharing one settings file with the pi and Claude Code ports. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/opencode-auto-effort.md).
- [opencode-auto-question (opencode plugin that answers an autonomous agent's questions with Jev)](https://github.com/jflowers/opencode-auto-question) - opencode plugin for autonomous runs: when an agent on your list asks the `question` tool, a fast Jev Choice or Noul pass answers from the question and options, and a second pass can gather context with tools before answering; agents not on the list still ask the human. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/opencode-auto-question.md).
- [opencode-condense (opencode context pruning by a scorer model, local or Jev)](https://github.com/condottab/opencode-condense) - opencode plugin that keeps long sessions alive without summaries: a scorer model decides which old tool calls and results can be dropped or truncated while user and assistant text stays verbatim; designed for a small local GGUF scorer, with TypeSafe Jev as the built-in default endpoint. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/opencode-condense.md).
- [opencode-decision-mode (decision tools for OpenCode)](https://github.com/Hacklone/opencode-decision-mode) - OpenCode plugin that gives the coding agent `decision`, `decision_map` and `decision_results` tools, so judgement calls on one item or on every record of a CSV or JSONL file go to Jev (through OpenRouter or TypeSafe directly) instead of being read through the controller model's context. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/opencode-decision-mode.md).
- [opencode-effort-router](https://github.com/draxxris/opencode-effort-router) - OpenCode 2 plugin that picks a reasoning effort (low to max) per request for allowlisted models using the OpenRouter Decisions API, defaulting to TypeSafe Jev, with cache-preserving Anthropic and OpenAI adapters. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/opencode-effort-router.md).
- [opencode-history (searchable OpenCode session history with an optional Jev recall judge)](https://github.com/StevenJPx2/opencode-history) - OpenCode plugin that makes earlier sessions searchable from OpenCode's own database (SQLite FTS5 over prompts, replies and compaction summaries), adds a frozen recent-work block to each session, and optionally asks Jev before each message which earlier passages would actually help, adding up to three of them. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/opencode-history.md).
- [opencode-inference (OpenCode plugin giving agents decide, generate and models tools, with Jev as the default decision model)](https://github.com/rndmcnlly/opencode-inference) - OpenCode V2 plugin that adds an `inference` Code Mode namespace: `decide` asks trained decision models (OpenRouter, Tinfoil) questions about some state and returns native probabilities, `generate` runs a one-turn tool-free sub-call on any registered model, and `models` lists what each supports so agents avoid failed calls. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/opencode-inference.md).
- [opencode-jev-compaction](https://github.com/radqnico/opencode-jev-compaction) - OpenCode compaction plugin that prunes stale tool calls/results with TypeSafe Jev instead of LLM summarization (port of tamaratran/fast-jev-compaction)—distinct from roshan-shaik-ml/fast-jev-opencode. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/radqnico-opencode-jev-compaction.md).
- [opencode-jev-guard](https://github.com/CogFlux/opencode-jev-guard) - OpenCode 2 plugin: TypeSafe Jev triages every local/FarHand shell command before run (distinct from leepokai/jev-guard). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/opencode-jev-guard.md).
- [opencode-jev-plugin (fsodanogm2dev)](https://github.com/fsodanogm2dev/opencode-jev-plugin) - OpenCode/OmO plugin: local Jev broker + System One hooks for safety gating, routing, and aggressive token saving. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/fsodanogm2dev-opencode-jev-plugin.md).
- [opencode-jev-pre-llm](https://github.com/murilogteixeira/opencode-jev-pre-llm) - OpenCode V2 plugin that asks TypeSafe Jev whether a prompt is deterministic before LLM reasoning, injecting a pre-analysis and temperature 0 when it is; fail-open. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/opencode-jev-pre-llm.md).
- [opencode-jev-pruner (OpenCode V2 plugin where Jev drops irrelevant MCP tools and skills from each request)](https://github.com/theroich/opencode-jev-pruner) - Context-only middleware for OpenCode V2: before every model call it asks Jev which MCP servers, MCP tools and skills are relevant to the prompt and removes the rest from the outgoing request (Code Mode catalogs, native tool maps and skill lists), cutting input tokens and wrong-tool calls. Tool registries and connections are untouched. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/opencode-jev-pruner.md).
- [opencode-jev-router](https://github.com/robertn702/opencode-jev-router) - OpenCode Responses proxy: TypeSafe Jev picks reasoning effort then pins Astra/Luna/Sol upstream (distinct from opencode-jev-guard). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/opencode-jev-router.md).
- [opencode-jev-safe-compact (Jev-guided compaction for OpenCode)](https://github.com/lewistouchtech/opencode-jev-safe-compact) - OpenCode plugin whose `/jev-compact` command prunes a session's tool history with a decision model instead of an LLM summary: needed tool calls stay verbatim, stale outputs are shortened with a re-run marker, and the keep cut-off adapts because Jev's noul calibration drifts with state shape. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/opencode-jev-safe-compact.md).
- [opencode-shell-safety](https://github.com/alexandru/opencode-shell-safety) - OpenCode v2 plugin that asks Jev to classify shell permission requests against the active agent definition and its filesystem policy, via OpenCode Zen or the TypeSafe API. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/opencode-shell-safety.md).
- [opencode-smart-reasoning](https://github.com/d0nj/opencode-smart-reasoning) - OpenCode plugin: TypeSafe Jev (Zen SystemOne) picks per-request reasoning effort, fail-open without a key. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/opencode-smart-reasoning.md).
- [opencode-system-one](https://github.com/atvari-eu/opencode-system-one) - OpenCode plugin that screens every `webfetch` result with Jev for junk and prompt injection before it enters context, redacts known secret formats from tool inputs and prompts with plain regex, and gives the agent cached `jev_decide` and `jev_judge_many` tools. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/opencode-system-one.md).
- [opencode-toolrouter](https://github.com/Nsilswal/opencode-toolrouter) - opencode plugin: TypeSafe Jev picks which MCP tools each request needs so schemas stay small. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/opencode-toolrouter.md).
- [OpenComputerUse](https://github.com/IAmJSD/OpenComputerUse) - Background computer use for agents as an MCP server: start a session with a desktop app, drive it by window coordinates or accessibility elements while your own pointer, focus and frontmost app stay put, and run `run_recipe` step lists where each step's interactive elements become one Jev `choice` question (or Cloudflare Clef-flash), acting only when the model is confident. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/opencomputeruse.md).
- [OpenDecider](https://github.com/manjunathshiva/opendecider) - Open System One decision models (choice/score/yes-no) with optional Jev-compatible `/v1/systemone` serve—independent of hosted TypeSafe Jev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/opendecider.md).
- [openfox-jev](https://github.com/adriy-be/openfox-jev) - OpenFox plugin: deterministic policy plus a System One approval reviewer (TypeSafe Jev or a local Jev-style `/v1/systemone` server) for tool/approval decisions. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/openfox-jev.md).
- [OpenJev](https://github.com/lookski/openjev) - Local Jev-compatible typed decisions via masked-logit softmax (not TypeSafe-hosted). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/openjev.md).
- [OpenJev (alanhuangyoo)](https://github.com/alanhuangyoo/OpenJev) - OpenJev (`wev-ai` / `openjev-ai` on PyPI): open, local System One decision models OpenJev-1.7B/4B/8B on Hugging Face that answer the `POST /v1/systemone` request shape for general decisions and browser-agent steps; independent, distinct from other projects named OpenJev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/alanhuangyoo-openjev.md).
- [OpenJev (ejhshen)](https://github.com/ejhshen/OpenJev) - OpenJev-4B open reproduction of Jev-style open-vocabulary probabilistic decisions (not TypeSafe-hosted; distinct from lookski/openjev). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/ejhshen-openjev.md).
- [OpenJev toolkit (installer, server script and Python client for running OpenJev locally on Apple silicon)](https://github.com/geekychris/open_jev_toolkit) - Installs OpenJev MLX builds (27B 8-bit or 4-bit, or the 9B Flash builds) at pinned revisions with uv, starts a local `POST /v1/systemone` server on port 3000 and adds a stdlib Python client, CLI and worked examples for Choice, yes/no and Score questions. Profiles are chosen by RAM (16 GB for Flash 4-bit up to 64 GB for 27B 8-bit). MLX builds are text only. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/open-jev-toolkit.md).
- [OpenJev-Cactus](https://github.com/SiliconLabAI/OpenJev-Cactus) - High-perf CPU edge server with OpenAI chat completions plus System One `/v1/systemone` (Cactus/needle); distinct from other OpenJev listings. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/siliconlabai-openjev-cactus.md).
- [openjev-mcp (markylaredo)](https://github.com/markylaredo/openjev-mcp) - MCP server exposing Jev via the public OpenJEV API (`jev_ask` / choice / score / noul); built for DeepSeek Harness, works in any MCP client. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/markylaredo-openjev-mcp.md).
- [openjev-rs](https://github.com/codesoda/openjev-rs) - Rust libraries for local typed decisions from frozen GGUF logits (OpenJev/SemIf readout); CLI/HTTP moved to codesoda/systemone. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/codesoda-openjev-rs.md).
- [openjev-server (abhishekgahlot2)](https://github.com/abhishekgahlot2/openjev-server) - Decision API over open models (vLLM or MLX): one forward pass per question for choice/noul/score—the server behind OpenJev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/abhishekgahlot2-openjev-server.md).
- [openjev-sglang](https://github.com/ekzhang/openjev-sglang) - Implements a Jev-shaped HTTP decision API using Qwen and SGLang; independent model behavior and unspecified code licensing. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/openjev-sglang.md).
- [openjevx](https://github.com/muthuishere/openjevx) - Open-weight Jev-compatible System One server for jevx: local `/v1/systemone` (default :21118) from Laya fine-tune with RLCD; pairs with muthuishere/jevx. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/muthuishere-openjevx.md).
- [OpenKind](https://github.com/whit3rabbit/openkind) - Rust decision engine with CLI and server implementing the TypeSafe Jev protocol for locally hosted models, plus research into custom Qwen 3.5 decision models. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/openkind.md).
- [OpenPA Eval (prior authorization cascade)](https://github.com/dylanmarks/fhir-prior-auth-eval) - TypeScript workbench and MCP server that tests how much reasoning prior authorization needs on synthetic FHIR R4 records: rules first, then Jev typed decisions, then small and frontier LLMs, with every citation checked and uncertain cases sent to clinical review. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/openpa-eval.md).
- [OpenRouter Jev Decisions (OpenClaw)](https://github.com/eulrich101618/openclaw-plugin-openrouter-jev) - OpenClaw decision-provider plugin that routes boolean, choice and score batches to Jev models through OpenRouter's Decisions API. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/openclaw-plugin-openrouter-jev.md).
