- [openrouter-spend-jev (Jev model router for Hermes)](https://github.com/adymitruk/openrouter-spend-jev) - Desktop setup for a Hermes agent on Linux: a local OpenAI-compatible proxy asks Jev on OpenRouter which of three model tiers (GLM Flash, DeepSeek V4 Flash, DeepSeek V4 Pro) should take each request, next to an OpenRouter spend widget for the Omarchy and KDE bars and Android. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/openrouter-spend-jev.md).
- [openvons (open-Jev: an open decision layer for text, images and Japanese voice commands, with a Jev-compatible API)](https://github.com/genai-craft/openvons) - Open project that makes LLMs, vision encoders and speech models answer a finite set of options with a probability each (always including "none of the above"), then splits the calibrated result into execute, confirm or reject. Covers intent and tool choice (frozen LLM plus a trained head), image attributes and Japanese voice commands, and serves a TypeSafe Jev-compatible `POST /v1/systemone` endpoint. Measurements are the author's. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/openvons.md).
- [optim-jev (token savings for Claude Code and Codex)](https://github.com/Oqura-ai/optim-jev) - Plugin with three Jev-powered optimizers for long agent sessions: a compacter that drops stale tool calls instead of summarizing the whole chat, a router that sends each file edit to the right model by project rules, and a skill filter that sends only relevant skills per prompt. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/optim-jev.md).
- [orca-jev-advisor](https://github.com/ab2webco/orca-jev-advisor) - Orca Lab decision layer: TypeSafe Jev judges risky agent commands while local rules cover obvious cases (license unspecified at tip). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/orca-jev-advisor.md).
- [OSS Inbox](https://github.com/DibbayajyotiRoy/oss-inbox) - Self-hosted GitHub App and dashboard for maintainers: Jev answers typed questions about each issue, PR and CI report to label, route, prioritize and screen them, acting only above thresholds measured on your own repo history and never closing anything. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/oss-inbox.md).
- [OTEL Jev Log Signals](https://github.com/gbesse/otel-jev-log-signals) - OTLP/HTTP JSON relay that adds Jev decisions to OpenTelemetry logs grouped by template (for example Drain's `log.record.template`): every record is kept, each distinct template is evaluated once per cache lifetime, and low-probability results become `review`. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/otel-jev-log-signals.md).
- [Overseer and workflow gate (Claude Code plan runner and approval gate, with optional Jev routing)](https://github.com/VictorGjn/overseer) - Two Claude Code plugins: Overseer runs a project's PLAN.md through the sessions you pick and judges finished work against the plan within a weekly budget, and the workflow gate blocks the Workflow tool until you approve the plan; with a TypeSafe key, Jev suggests intent, model and agent type, scores review findings and checks whether spend delivered. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/overseer-workflow-gate.md).
- [Pad](https://github.com/PerpetualSoftware/pad) - Local-first project management (CLI, web UI, agent skill) for developers and coding agents; an optional TypeSafe decision provider asks Jev whether items need a human or are blocked and matches playbooks. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pad.md).
- [painpoints](https://github.com/prodBirdy/painpoints) - CLI, MCP server and native viewer that score every source file on layering, complexity, data-access cost, failure handling, UI cost and trust-boundary risk with a System One model (TypeSafe Jev or Cloudflare Clef), then hand coding agents a ranked, self-explaining technical-debt report. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/painpoints.md).
- [Paper Trellis Citation Verifier](https://github.com/MarissaFamularo/citation-verifier) - Citation checker for manuscripts: proves Claude-proposed quotes exist in the cited paper, then Jev scores supports/contradicts/says nothing. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/paper-trellis-citation-verifier.md).
- [paper-sift](https://github.com/lucasddmc/paper-sift) - Claude Code plugin that builds a reading list for learning a research area: it collects up to 800 candidate papers from Semantic Scholar, screens every abstract with Jev against the survey's rules, reads the best open-access papers, and reports a ranked list. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/paper-sift.md).
- [paperclip-jev](https://github.com/tbmsindemnify/paperclip-jev) - Paperclip fork that routes work tasks automatically with TypeSafe Jev (see TYPesafe issue-router docs). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/paperclip-jev.md).
- [Parallax (arXiv literature search plugin with Jev screening)](https://github.com/Zilize/parallax-dsh) - DeepSeek Harness plugin that harvests arXiv metadata into local SQLite and lets the agent build auditable paper sets with keyword recall, set operations and Jev screening and classification, where each paper gets an inclusion probability. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/parallax-dsh.md).
- [Parcel Lens (pre-registered test of spotting damaged parcels in synthetic images, with a Jev Box comparison)](https://github.com/RajaRuling/parcel-lens) - Reproducible study with claims written before training: a small trained model versus Jev (`jev-1.14.0`, experimental image input) on 150 synthetic parcel images. The trained model scored AUC 96 against Jev's 76 on damage yes/no, and Jev caught about 1 in 6 damaged parcels with no false alarms; Jev's Box overlapped the true damage by at least half 59% of the time but drew a box on all 75 clean parcels. Static results site in `docs/`. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/parcel-lens.md).
- [paseo-jev-compaction](https://github.com/hakilee/paseo-jev-compaction) - Paseo provider that adds Jev-first, conservative history compaction to Codex with automatic Codex fallback. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/paseo-jev-compaction.md).
- [patdown](https://github.com/tyler-dot-earth/patdown) - Fuzzy markdown-rule linter CLI with a swappable judge; default backend posts typed questions to TypeSafe Jev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/patdown.md).
- [PayWarden (payment firewall between an AI accounts-payable agent and PayPal, with Jev choosing payee and amount)](https://github.com/natrajexplore/paywarden) - PayPal AI Hackathon project: an accounts-payable agent reads invoices and job-done messages and proposes PayPal Payouts or Invoices without holding PayPal credentials, and a policy layer applies injection defence, payee and amount provenance, spend mandates, sanctions and structuring checks before executing, asking one or two humans with TOTP, or blocking. Jev picks the payee and total only from values that literally appear in the document and flags manipulation; Claude or an offline fallback can replace it. Every step goes to a hash-chained audit log. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/paywarden.md).
- [pbi-orchestration-jev](https://github.com/reeschan/pbi-orchestration-jev) - Claude Code skill that splits multi-file work into PBIs for Codex/Antigravity/Claude sub-agents with TypeSafe Jev gating on routing and review. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pbi-orchestration-jev.md).
- [PDF Race](https://github.com/goodrahstar/pdf-race) - Timed Docling→TypeSafe Jev vs Gemini document race with committed keyless replays (optional local Laya on Apple silicon). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pdf-race.md).
- [peira](https://github.com/david-engelmann/peira) - Open-source stress test for decision models and guardrails: it measures whether hostile input can flip a model's typed output, pairing every attack with a benign twin and reporting attack success rates with Wilson intervals; includes a Jev adapter. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/peira.md).
- [PerfectRecall](https://github.com/arslanr-com/perfectrecall) - Hermes/Python agent memory using TypeSafe or OpenRouter Jev evidence questions over local SQLite (Mnemosyne-compatible; no embeddings). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/perfectrecall.md).
- [Perpetual KYC (pKYC) Sandbox](https://github.com/munronai/pkyc-sandbox) - Compliance monitoring prototype where Jev acts as a fast gatekeeper on new registry filings, sanctions hits and adverse media: it checks same-entity, novelty and materiality, then discards noise, queues for an analyst, or triggers an LLM due-diligence report refresh. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pkyc-sandbox.md).
- [Petsona Catalogue AI (evidence-backed pet product tagging with Jev or Laya)](https://github.com/jeromtom/petsona-catalogue-ai) - AI slice of the Petsona Shopify app: product text, label OCR and manufacturer pages become evidence spans, code proposes values, and a decision model (Jev 1.13.0 or open-weight Laya) marks each one supported, contradicted, unknown or conflicting before a merchant reviews it. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/petsona-catalogue-ai.md).
- [pg-jev](https://github.com/realZachi/pg-jev) - Adds semantic predicates, probabilities, choices, and scores to PostgreSQL through a PL/Python extension. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pg-jev.md).
- [pg_typesafe](https://github.com/giuliosmall/pg_typesafe) - Pre-alpha PostgreSQL C extension for TypeSafe Jev Choice/Noul/Score with batched multi-text helpers; distinct from pg-jev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pg-typesafe.md).
- [pgdocket (durable AI decisions for Postgres)](https://github.com/ElyasEbrahim/pgdocket) - Python tool that stores Jev decisions about Postgres rows as versioned, calibrated data kept current as rows change, with a `check` command that measures how trustworthy a decision is on your own table and a Phase 0 benchmark of jev-1.13.0 through pg-jev on 2,000 complaint narratives. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pgdocket.md).
- [phase-exit](https://github.com/Know-Me-Tools/phase-exit) - Rust phase-completion engine with evidence-backed exit checks that call TypeSafe Jev (`jev-1.14.0` on `https://api.typesafe.ai/v1/systemone`), plus Agent Skills and native harness hooks. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/phase-exit.md).
- [PhonePilot](https://github.com/afetmin/PhonePilot) - Android automation where TypeSafe Jev chooses each UI step from the accessibility tree while Codex plans (Chinese docs). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/phonepilot.md).
- [Photoshop MCP](https://github.com/alisaitteke/photoshop-mcp) - Unofficial MCP server for Adobe Photoshop (100+ tools and one-step recipes); its built-in chat UI can opt in to TypeSafe Jev intent routing so confident, safe single commands run without an LLM call. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/photoshop-mcp.md).
- [Pi Adaptive Effort Router (XDeviation)](https://github.com/XDeviation/pi-jev-router) - Pi extension that changes thinking level only via TypeSafe Jev (distinct from philippdubach/pi-jev-router). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/xdeviation-pi-jev-router.md).
- [Pi Durable News Agent](https://github.com/disler/pi-durable-news-agent) - Proactive pi-durable news agent: a cron tick sweeps about 28 feeds plus topic searches, Jev throws out off-topic or low-value items and sorts the rest into Hype, Facts, and Signal, and research subagents dig into the strongest stories. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pi-durable-news-agent.md).
- [Pi Jev Compact (bounded Jev-assisted context compaction for Pi, plus a repeated tool-failure guard)](https://github.com/maccagnit/pi-jev-compact) - Pi package with two separate extensions: a compactor that replaces Pi's generated summary with a bounded, extractive checkpoint (your requests kept verbatim, selected exact evidence kept, full history recallable), using at most one Jev request per compaction and none without a key; and a loop guard that blocks the fourth identical tool call after three identical failures and offers `/loop-recover`. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pi-jev-compact.md).
- [Pi Pocket Pipelines (visual LLM workflow editor for Pi Pocket, with Jev decision nodes)](https://github.com/stilllostinla/pi-pocket-pipelines) - Drop-in extension for Pi Pocket that adds a drag-and-drop pipeline canvas: chained LLM calls, parallel scout agents, loops, branches, shell commands, human approvals and nested pipelines, checkpointed so a restart resumes a run; a Decision node uses Jev (direct or via OpenRouter) to pick a category or answer yes or no, returning unsure below its line. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pi-pocket-pipelines.md).
- [Pi Workspace Search (bounded code retrieval with optional Jev classification)](https://github.com/rodrigojager/pi-workspace-search) - Pi extension that gives the agent bounded workspace search: ripgrep for literal and regex queries, a local zvec index for concepts when present, and Jev to classify which code chunks answer a concept query when local results are not enough, keeping output small for the main model. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pi-workspace-search.md).
- [pi-advisor](https://github.com/philipbrembeck/pi-advisor) - Configurable Advisor/Executor Pi plugin with optional TypeSafe Jev consultation filter and proactive turn gate. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pi-advisor.md).
- [pi-automode-classifier](https://github.com/deepu105/pi-automode-classifier) - Auto mode for the Pi coding agent: built-in rules decide most tool calls, then a classifier model — hosted Jev (e.g. `openrouter` `typesafe/jev-1.13` through Pi's model registry) or local Kev / Laya on CPU via llama.cpp's `/v1/systemone` — scores unknown shell commands, and risky ones get a confirm prompt or are blocked. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pi-automode-classifier.md).
- [pi-classifier](https://github.com/abhishek944/pi-classifier) - Pi coding-agent extension for typed, probabilistic decisions across TypeSafe Jev, OpenAI Decisions, Perplexity Decisions, Cloudflare Clef and other SystemOne-compatible gateways, with one configured model and one provider request per decision. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pi-classifier.md).
- [pi-context-decisions](https://github.com/championswimmer/pi-context-decisions) - Pi extension where TypeSafe Jev or another decision model filters which skills enter context per prompt (pre-alpha). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pi-context-decisions.md).
- [pi-durable-bot (self-hosted AI coworker for team chat, with Jev deciding when a message needs it)](https://github.com/Crokily/pi-durable-bot) - Open-source AI coworker you run on your own machine: it logs every message, edit and event in the channels you invite it to into local SQLite, keeps one long memory, writes and runs its own code, sets its own reminders and resumes after a crash without double-sending. Before waking the main model on an unprompted message, Jev answers two questions: is this about me, and do I need to respond. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pi-durable-bot.md).
- [pi-extensions (narumiruna)](https://github.com/narumiruna/pi-extensions) - Pi Coding Agent extension monorepo with TypeSafe packages for typed Jev decisions, compaction selection, and FTS5+Jev search. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/narumiruna-pi-extensions.md).
- [pi-follow-through](https://github.com/Nabsku/pi-follow-through) - Pi extension that asks TypeSafe Jev whether useful work remains after a run and nudges only with verifiable unfinished evidence. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pi-follow-through.md).
- [pi-heed](https://github.com/Nyarlathoteppppp/pi-heed) - Pi extension that keeps conversational user constraints as structured policy and checks side-effecting tool calls with TypeSafe Jev help. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pi-heed.md).
- [pi-herdr-orchestrator (Pi subagents in herdr panes)](https://github.com/HaiYangMa13/pi-herdr-orchestrator) - Pi coding agent extension that runs each subagent as a real Pi session in a herdr terminal pane, returns results through written report files, and can add optional Jev gates that classify each handoff. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pi-herdr-orchestrator.md).
- [pi-jev](https://github.com/y0usaf/pi-jev) - Adds a TypeSafe Jev gate, output judge, and jev_ask tool to the Pi coding agent, with shadow mode by default and fail-open errors. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pi-jev.md).
- [pi-jev (kurowashi)](https://github.com/kurowashi/pi-jev) - Pi coding-agent extensions that use TypeSafe Jev for semantic checks on file edits and new-file placement. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/kurowashi-pi-jev.md).
- [pi-Jev-browser](https://github.com/laihenyi/pi-Jev-browser) - Pi extension where TypeSafe Jev chooses each Playwright browser action over a structured DOM observation in a bounded loop. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pi-jev-browser.md).
- [pi-jev-context](https://github.com/Nyarlathoteppppp/pi-jev-context) - Pi extension: cache-neutral write-time trimming of long tool outputs with TypeSafe Jev only when comparison cannot decide, plus lossless context_recall. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pi-jev-context.md).
- [pi-jev-control-chrome](https://github.com/JHXs/pi-jev-control-chrome) - Pi Agent extension where the main model hands a browsing task to a bounded loop: pi-control-chrome reads the page DOM, Jev picks one action from a finite candidate list, and the loop clicks, types, scrolls until done or a limit. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pi-jev-control-chrome.md).
- [pi-jev-effort](https://github.com/namenu/pi-jev-effort) - Pi extension: TypeSafe Jev scores prompt difficulty and sets thinking level, capped by remaining quota/burn (distinct from pi-jev-router). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pi-jev-effort.md).
- [pi-jev-ext-router](https://github.com/shourovrm/pi-jev-ext-router) - Pi extension where Jev (via OpenRouter) picks which lazily loaded extension a prompt needs and activates it before the model turn. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pi-jev-ext-router.md).
- [pi-jev-extension (rioliu)](https://github.com/rioliu/pi-jev-extension) - Pi agent extension adding a `jev_decide` tool for TypeSafe Jev typed choice/score/noul questions with session-model fallback. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/rioliu-pi-jev-extension.md).
- [pi-jev-governor](https://github.com/Pyromind-Dynamics/pi-jev-governor) - pi coding-agent governance layer using the JEV Decisions API for planning, model-tier routing, permissions, and verification. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pi-jev-governor.md).
- [pi-jev-lazy-load (Pi extension that keeps tools and skills out of the first prompt, with optional Jev picking what to load)](https://github.com/dantetekanem/pi-jev-lazy-load) - Pi extension that replaces most tool declarations and skill descriptions with a short index and loads them only when the prompt names them, the model asks, or (if you opt in) Jev decides the request needs them, shrinking the first model request. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pi-jev-lazy-load.md).
- [pi-jev-permit](https://github.com/kurihada/pi-jev-permit) - Pi extension: TypeSafe Jev judges each bash/write/edit call after local hard-deny and read-only fast paths. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pi-jev-permit.md).
- [pi-jev-router](https://github.com/philippdubach/pi-jev-router) - Pi extension: TypeSafe Jev classifies tasks and local policy routes OpenRouter models (Pareto profiles; shadow mode default). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pi-jev-router.md).
- [pi-jev-sentinel](https://github.com/harshwasan/pi-jev-sentinel) - Adds TypeSafe Jev checks for Pi, Claude Code, and Codex tool calls, tool outputs, and replies, with secret scrubbing and fail-closed asks when unconfigured. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pi-jev-sentinel.md).
- [pi-jev-thinking](https://github.com/lancejohnson/pi-jev-thinking) - Pi extension where TypeSafe Jev sets the thinking level for each prompt and logs decisions for tuning. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pi-jev-thinking.md).
- [pi-jev-thinking-router](https://github.com/brzewVCE/pi-jev-thinking-router) - Pi/omp extension where TypeSafe Jev picks per-turn thinking effort without switching models (distinct from pi-jev-thinking). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pi-jev-thinking-router.md).
- [pi-magic8ball](https://github.com/neruok/pi-magic8ball) - Pi extension that asks TypeSafe Jev or Clef for an advisory choice distribution over options using neutrally built context. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pi-magic8ball.md).
- [pi-next-prompt](https://github.com/justmytwospence/pi-next-prompt) - Pi extension with Claude Code–style next-prompt suggestions: the main model may end its final message with one `<next>…</next>` line, and TypeSafe Jev (through Pi's classifier models) vets whether it is a concrete, non-generic, not-yet-done next step; it is shown under the editor at p ≥ 0.6 and `Tab` fills it in without sending. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pi-next-prompt.md).
- [pi-reply-guard](https://github.com/amalavet/pi-reply-guard) - Pi extension that checks each agent reply against installed skills you choose: a classifier — Jev by default (`openrouter/typesafe/jev-1.13`) — returns the probability the reply adheres to each skill, and replies below your threshold are sent back for a rewrite (up to `maxRewrites`). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pi-reply-guard.md).
- [pi-rm-guard](https://github.com/sdougbrown/pi-rm-guard) - Pi extension: a decision model (TypeSafe Jev by default) auto-approves only safe recursive `rm` commands. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pi-rm-guard.md).
- [pi-router by JangMan-J (session-level model router for Pi, with Jev as a fallback decider)](https://github.com/JangMan-J/pi-router) - Pi virtual model (`router/auto`) that picks a model per session along three axes (depth, width, urgency) from a paired review by decision models on OpenRouter, detects when a conversation pivots using local telemetry and embeddings, and keeps a SQLite store of routing records for calibration and retrieval. Jev (`typesafe/jev-1.14`) is the first automatic fallback decider. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pi-router-3-axis.md).
- [pi-safely](https://github.com/dcowsill/pi-safely) - Pi package that re-implements the core of Claude Code's auto mode for the Pi agent, gating every non-allowlisted tool call through a System One decision model — Jev (TypeSafe or OpenRouter), Cloudflare Clef or any `POST /v1/systemone` backend — that returns calibrated yes/no probabilities. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pi-safely.md).
- [pi-shift-router](https://github.com/green-dalii/pi-shift-router) - Pi per-turn cheap/strong model router with failover and optional TypeSafe Jev decision-model judge. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pi-shift-router.md).
- [pi-skill-audit (Pi extension that reviews skills for tool conversion with Jev, the chat model, or both)](https://github.com/jamelwa/pi-skill-audit) - Pi extension with two advisory commands: `/skill-audit` reviews existing skills for possible conversion into tools or hybrid workflows using your current Pi chat model, TypeSafe's Jev classifier, or a side-by-side compare; `/skill-review` looks for a reusable procedure in the session that might deserve a new skill. Nothing is changed or executed automatically. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pi-skill-audit.md).
- [pi-skimmer](https://github.com/jon-devlapaz/pi-skimmer) - Pi coding-agent extension for goal-directed text and JSON filtering powered by Jev (via pi-typesafe), with explicit consent and recoverable results. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pi-skimmer.md).
- [pi-subagent-jev](https://github.com/G0-0000/pi-subagent-jev) - pi package: gate subagent dispatches through a Jev System One endpoint and expose jev_ask / jev_models tools (requires pi-subagents). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pi-subagent-jev.md).
- [pi-system-one-gate](https://github.com/kreeger/pi-system-one-gate) - Authorizer link for the Pi coding agent's permission system that asks a System One model (TypeSafe Jev or a Reflex server) whether a bash, MCP or tool call is safe and auto-approves only at P(safe) of 0.9 or more; everything else goes to your normal prompt. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pi-system-one-gate.md).
- [pi-taskmesh (task orchestration for Pi with optional Jev judgments)](https://github.com/0x1f/pi-taskmesh) - Task-aware multi-model orchestration extension for the Pi coding agent: it decomposes work and coordinates subagents, and can use Jev to judge whether a request should be delegated and which model each task gets. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pi-taskmesh.md).
- [pi-thinking-router-jev](https://github.com/Wh0rigin/pi-thinking-router-jev) - Pi coding-agent extension: TypeSafe Jev adapts thinking level (low→xhigh) from task start/failures with local-rule fallback. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pi-thinking-router-jev.md).
- [pi-thunder-guard](https://github.com/Mandalorian007/pi-thunder-guard) - Pi guard where bash calls and out-of-repo writes run only when TypeSafe Jev is confident they keep to the restrictions. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pi-thunder-guard.md).
- [pi-thunder-jev](https://github.com/Mandalorian007/pi-thunder-jev) - `jev_find` for Pi: TypeSafe Jev ranks the files and excerpts a code question is about in about a second. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pi-thunder-jev.md).
- [pi-triage (pi tool that decides how much work a request deserves before it spends a token)](https://github.com/tripplen23/pi-triage) - pi coding-agent tool that sorts each request into one of six routes (answer inline, worker, scout then worker, planner then worker, browser QA, or review) and returns the tier, confidence, verification level and route string, using Jev on OpenRouter first, a self-hosted Laya engine second and capped keyword rules last. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pi-triage.md).
- [pi-typesafe-approve](https://github.com/Davidasx/pi-typesafe-approve) - Pi extension that auto-approves routine Bash commands via a System One/Jev decision model and escalates the rest to a human. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pi-typesafe-approve.md).
- [pi-typesafe-bash-guard](https://github.com/gowthamgts/pi-stuff/tree/main/extensions/typesafe-bash-guard) - Pi extension that classifies bash tool calls and `!` shells with TypeSafe Jev before execution; supports 1Password `op://` API-key references. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pi-typesafe-bash-guard.md).
- [pi-verdict](https://github.com/jesset/pi-verdict) - Minimal Pi permission gate (allow/ask/deny) with an optional TypeSafe Jev decisions-model classifier adapter. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pi-verdict.md).
- [pi-vibe-guard (Pi extension that warns when a prompt reads as vague vibe coding, scored by Jev)](https://github.com/jordilopez/pi-vibe-guard) - Pi extension that scores every prompt with Jev before it reaches the model and warns when it reads as loose-spec vibe coding, with concrete fixes (add a measurement, an acceptance criterion or a target path); declining returns the text to the editor so you can tighten it. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pi-vibe-guard.md).
- [pi-warden](https://github.com/DevMortimer/pi-warden) - Combines local Pi guard checks with Jev action and rule judgments, configurable holds, corrective feedback and context retention. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pi-warden.md).
- [pi-watcher](https://github.com/Maolon/pi-watcher) - Pi extension that watches long-running builds, tests, training jobs, CI and deadlines from durable evidence and keeps the session quiet until a fact needs a decision; optionally, TypeSafe Jev answers six bounded questions about a sanitized evidence window (progress, blocker, needs a decision, repeating, claim vs evidence, enough context) that can raise or annotate an episode but never override hard facts. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pi-watcher.md).
- [pi-web-search (Pi agent search tool with optional Jev ranking, prompt-injection filtering and coverage checks)](https://github.com/GaganSD/pi-extensions) - Extension for the Pi coding agent that gives it one search tool over Exa, Parallel, Jina Reader, grep.app and Sourcegraph, keyless by default. On thorough searches an optional classifier, Jev via TypeSafe, Vercel AI Gateway or OpenRouter, receives the query and candidate titles, URLs and excerpts and answers yes/no and choice questions to rank results, suppress likely prompt injection or phishing, and judge whether the evidence covers the query. Code applies the weights and thresholds. Part of the pi-extensions monorepo. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pi-web-search.md).
- [pi-zereight-router](https://github.com/zereight/pi-zereight-router) - Jev-backed virtual model for Pi: each user message is classified by Jev (`openrouter/typesafe/jev-1.13`) as planning, research, codebase explore, review, implementation or other and routed to a fixed Cursor-provider model and effort, with a mid-turn handoff to the implementation model after the first file edit and a local phase decision log. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pi-zereight-router.md).
- [pickstep (fast browser tests where Claude Code plans, Jev picks each step and ego lite drives)](https://github.com/jinjin99/pickstep) - Browser-test runner that hands the slow look-click-look inner loop to Jev: Claude Code writes a spec of stages, inputs and budget, then for each step pickstep observes the page, Jev picks the next action from the visible options, and ego lite acts; Claude reads the report and takes over when Jev answers NONE or is unsure. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pickstep.md).
- [picode-decisions-jev (Jev for COGNIX[WS])](https://github.com/cognixws/picode-decisions-jev) - COGNIX[WS] extension that lets TypeSafe Jev answer the agents' fast browser decisions, such as which page element matches a description and yes/no checks about a page, so agents act without reading the whole page each step. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/picode-decisions-jev.md).
- [Pinot Jev](https://github.com/gbesse/pinot-jev) - Apache Pinot scalar-function plugin for semantic predicates backed by TypeSafe Jev (`jevAll`, `jevAny`, `jevProbability`), fusing up to eight conditions per row into one request with per-server caching. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pinot-jev.md).
- [plain](https://github.com/gabe4coding/plain) - Write browser, desktop and mobile end-to-end tests in plain English YAML: Playwright, xa11y or Appium perform the actions while Jev reads the accessibility tree to select the element each step describes and to judge each `expect` claim; coding agents can explore a flow via MCP and save it as a spec. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/plain.md).
- [plain-language-gate](https://github.com/Wujiaowang/plain-language-gate) - Agent skill/CLI: TypeSafe Jev runs six plain-language readability checks and returns pass/review/rewrite (author model rewrites). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/plain-language-gate.md).
- [plan-anchor](https://github.com/randlee/plan-anchor) - Claude Code and Codex skill that captures your planning statements as anchors, turns them into typed TypeSafe Jev questions, and checks both the plan and the delivered work for drift. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/plan-anchor.md).
- [PlayJev](https://github.com/OmniJev/PlayJev) - Plays ten browser games from the frame alone with an open 0.8B model that scores the moves the game lists in one forward pass; independent of official Jev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/playjev.md)
- [PlayJev (filed)](https://github.com/filedcom/playjev) - "Stagehand, but with Jev": natural-language Playwright automation where TypeSafe Jev selects real browser nodes (`check`, `choose`, `rate`, `act`) and deterministic code performs actions; distinct from the listed OmniJev/PlayJev game player. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/filedcom-playjev.md).
- [playwright-jev](https://github.com/criguex/playwright-jev) - Semantic Playwright Test assertions (`toMean`, `toMeanAll`, `toChoose`, `toBeScored`) powered by TypeSafe Jev with fail-closed ambiguity handling and record/replay. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/criguex-playwright-jev.md).
- [playwright-jev (AdriaanVE)](https://github.com/AdriaanVE/playwright-jev) - Structured TypeSafe Jev decisions for Playwright failure triage, smart retries, healer gating, snapshot pruning, locator healing, and test impact ordering. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/adriaanve-playwright-jev.md).
- [pm-jev](https://github.com/unbraind/pm-jev) - pm-cli extension/library for typed System One decisions on project items via the TypeSafe JS SDK, local Ollama by default. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pm-jev.md).
- [Pointsman](https://github.com/Kai-Leee/pointsman) - Jev-powered model and task routing for Claude Code and Codex: structured decisions become dependency DAGs that dispatch ready tasks to coding workers (formerly Jev Router). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pointsman.md).
- [PokéSurvivor x Jev learning bot](https://github.com/skyhilam/pokesurvivor) - Experimental bot for the browser survival game PokéSurvivor: a Chrome extension handles 60 FPS dodging locally, and a local bridge asks Jev for level-up cards, items and tactics, learning only from what it sees on screen (README in Cantonese). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pokesurvivor-jev.md).
- [PolicyJEV](https://github.com/AbbasRana001/PolicyJEV) - Frozen-protocol comparison of zero-shot Jev with TF-IDF logistic regression and fine-tuned DistilBERT on Jigsaw toxic comments: Jev ranks worse (mean AP 0.527 against 0.583 and 0.677), and as a three-way allow/review/action policy it sends the most comments to human review. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/policyjev.md).
- [Poltergeist](https://github.com/ghostsecurity/poltergeist) - Ghost Security's fast Go secret scanner with an opt-in advisory classification that asks Jev how likely each detected value is a real secret, without changing findings or exit codes. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/poltergeist.md).
- [polyjev](https://github.com/PraveenAShukla/polyjev) - Typed calibrated decisions (Noul/Choice/Score/Span) from any LLM with a drop-in Jev-compatible `/v1/systemone` server; independent of hosted Jev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/polyjev.md).
- [postgres-search](https://github.com/alexforman1/postgres-search) - Algolia-style product search in plain PostgreSQL (whole and partial words, typos, barcodes, typeahead, facets) with an optional TypeSafe Jev second stage asking two questions per search — keep or sink each result, and which spelling the user meant; the README doubles as a dated evaluation report on 440k USDA products with failures, costs and threats to validity. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/postgres-search.md).
- [PowerContext by OceanBase (context service for AI agents, with optional Jev memory reranking and advisory decisions)](https://github.com/oceanbase/powercontext) - Open-source context service that keeps an agent's decisions, constraints, evidence and progress outside any one conversation so the next session, agent or person can pick up from the current state. Python deployments can add an optional Jev reranker that filters retrieved memory candidates, and an example adapter plugs Jev (or the open Laya model) into the Runtime's advisory `DecisionModel` port to review code against recalled project rules. The README reports LoCoMo Plus gains of about 4 to 9 points with Jev filtering, scores supplied by the project owner without public run artifacts. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/powercontext.md).
- [pr-review by Arbio (evidence-backed PR reviewer with a Jev risk triage)](https://github.com/arbiolabs/pr-reviewer-agent) - Pull request reviewer that reports only findings it can back with code evidence and resumes after crashes; Jev scores the risk of each change during triage and can raise the review depth, never lower it. Runs as a CLI, a GitHub App service, a terminal UI or a macOS app. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/arbiolabs-pr-review.md).
- [PriceWatch (Thelia 3 competitor price watch where Jev judges only the narrow product-matching questions)](https://github.com/thelia-modules/PriceWatch) - Thelia 3 e-commerce module that collects competitor offers (Google Shopping via DataForSEO), matches them to catalogue variants and reports price gaps in the back office. Code decides what it can see (GTIN equality, price gap, currency) and only narrow semantic questions go to Jev: same article, same variant, comparable price. Large gaps send Jev-only matches to a human. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/thelia-pricewatch.md).
- [prime-typesafe-jev](https://github.com/japorto100/prime-typesafe-jev) - Host bridge for Prime Agent that adds Jev decisions behind a Unix-socket credential boundary, deterministic hard blocks, egress redaction and daily budget caps. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/prime-typesafe-jev.md).
- [Privatemode Decisions benchmark](https://github.com/edgelesssys/privatemode-decisions-benchmark) - Edgeless Systems' benchmark comparing its Privatemode Decisions library (GLM-5.3-Flash in an attested enclave) with TypeSafe Jev and local Laya on speed, accuracy, calibration and cost across 29 labelled public datasets, plus JevBench comparisons. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/privatemode-decisions-benchmark.md).
- [ProbabilityBench](https://github.com/ishida-lab/ProbabilityBench) - Benchmark of text worlds with exact ground-truth class probabilities (peer review, covert transfer, credit risk), with an example that scores decision models such as TypeSafe Jev against the true probabilities. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/probabilitybench.md).
- [Prompt Rejector](https://github.com/revsmoke/promptrejectormcp) - MCP/HTTPS screening for prompts, skills, and tool descriptions with TypeSafe Jev plus deterministic checks. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/prompt-rejector.md).
- [prompt2jev](https://github.com/sumleo/prompt2jev) - Agent skill and CLI that convert natural language, an LLM prompt, or prompt-running code into a TypeSafe Jev decision (typed questions + runnable script). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/prompt2jev.md).
- [prose-check](https://github.com/vgeshel/prose-check) - Claude Code / Codex / Muse Code plugin that checks each final reply against project writing rules with Jev and requests one rewrite on violations. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/prose-check.md).
- [protoc-gen-jev](https://github.com/bufbuild/protoc-gen-jev) - Experimental Buf (bufbuild) protoc plugin that turns Protobuf schemas annotated with questions, rubrics and thresholds into strongly typed Jev clients for Go, TypeScript and Python (or a language-agnostic `.jev.json` spec), mapping fields to noul, choice and score decisions. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/protoc-gen-jev.md).
- [Pulumi self-driving software factory](https://github.com/pulumi/devx-pulumi-software-factory) - Pulumi example from the Pulumi org that deploys a self-driving software factory on AWS: a Qwen3-8B model on your own GPUs, Mastra Factory for coding agents, a Mastra agent, and an issue triage service where Jev accepts, rejects or asks for detail on each new item. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pulumi-software-factory.md).
- [purge-email](https://github.com/bhdoggett/purge-email) - Gmail clean-up CLI: Gmail search does the cheap filtering (age, no attachments, not starred), then TypeSafe Jev judges whether each remaining message is likely personal correspondence, a receipt/financial record or an account/legal/medical/government record; `plan` only labels (`purge` below 0.1 keep-probability, `review` in between) and nothing moves to Trash until you run `apply --yes`. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/purge-email.md).
- [px-triage](https://github.com/cephalization/px-triage) - Keyboard-driven CLI for triaging a GitHub repository's `triage`-labelled issues and PRs: TypeSafe Jev reads each item and proposes the next step (ask for info, bug + owner, schedule, review, close), you confirm with one key or override; GitHub changes run in the background, decisions are logged and traced to Arize Phoenix. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/px-triage.md).
- [pydecide](https://github.com/gopaljigaur/decide) - One Python client for typed decisions (Choice, Score, Noul) over Jev, OpenRouter, OpenAI Decisions, Laya, MLX and cross-encoders, with a confidence-gated fallback chain. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pydecide.md).
- [Pyro](https://github.com/delvisorlabs/pyro) - Open-source, self-hostable policy engine that checks text before your AI application uses it: write word, pattern and semantic checks, test each branch and trace why each allow, review or block decision was made; semantic checks use TypeSafe Jev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pyro.md).
- [pytest-jev](https://github.com/allebee/pytest-jev) - Pytest plugin for semantic assertions: TypeSafe Jev judges whether text holds or lacks plain-language claims. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pytest-jev.md).
- [QAJev](https://github.com/HanamoriLabs/qajev) - Test a website, game or app the way a person uses it: write a plain-language goal and Jev (TypeSafe or OpenRouter) clicks through a real Chrome, iOS Simulator, Android emulator or Godot/Electron game, while your expectations — not Jev's "done" — decide pass/fail; read-only by default with a hard cost cap. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/qajev.md).
- [Qev (open Jev-like decision models fine-tuned from Qwen, 0.8B to 9B, with weights on Hugging Face)](https://github.com/QiqianFu/Qev) - Open decision models built by fine-tuning Qwen: give context, a question and candidate answers and get a choice with a probability for every option, across Choice, Noul and Score. Ships architecture, training and evaluation code, a Python interface and four checkpoint sizes on Hugging Face, with benchmarks against Jev and other open decision models (Kev, JevAny). Builds on Jared Palmer's Kev conventions. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/qev.md).
- [quackd](https://github.com/rokbenko/quackd) - Multi-robot CLI with LLM pilots and an optional TypeSafe Jev discrete stepper for closed-set skill choices. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/quackd.md).
- [Qualixar Jev Decision Layer](https://github.com/qualixar/jev-decision-layer) - Typed MCP decision layer for Claude Code/Codex/Hermes/VS Code agents: 20 tools, 36 recipes, offline fixtures; TypeSafe Jev primary with optional local Laya. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/qualixar-jev-decision-layer.md).
- [Quarkus LangChain4j decision routing (Jev/Kev/Laya)](https://github.com/kdubois/quarkus-langchain4j-jev-laya) - Proof of concept for Quarkus 4 + Quarkus LangChain4j 2: LangChain4j's `DecisionRouterPlanner` uses a small decision model to route car-rental questions to specialist chat agents and merge their answers, with the same typed decisions runnable against hosted TypeSafe Jev, local Kev, an included Laya sidecar or a deterministic stub — plus Jev-backed reply guardrails. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/quarkus-langchain4j-jev-laya.md).
- [quick-qa-helper](https://github.com/ouor/quick-qa-helper) - Android UI QA loop for coding agents: a planner model picks the next action, TypeSafe Jev picks the target element (Box on a screenshot when the UI tree lacks it) and independently verifies done, adb acts, and you get a pass/fail report with one summary image. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/quick-qa-helper.md).
- [QuickE2E](https://github.com/dmoka/quicke2e) - Plain-English end-to-end testing library for web apps: you write a goal and inputs, a small decision model picks each browser step — hosted Jev (`typesafe/jev-1.13` via OpenRouter, the default) or a local Shisa DE-1 engine — Playwright acts, and an explicit check (e.g. one SQL query) verifies the result; specs can be turned into generated Playwright code. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/quicke2e.md).
- [Quicksilver](https://github.com/UditAkhourii/quicksilver) - Claude Code skill that hands bulk judgment/shortlist work to TypeSafe Jev so Claude spends tokens on harder reasoning. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/quicksilver.md).
- [Ravnir (persistent AI agents on your own server with one reviewable shared memory, and optional Jev for browser actions)](https://github.com/ravnir-ai/ravnir) - Self-hosted agent workspace in public beta: give each agent a job, a model and its own browser, let it keep working when you close the tab and run routines on your server, and have what it learns land in a shared vault of Markdown notes in git that you can read, review and revert. Browser and desktop use can choose actions with the agent's model, a quick model, or Jev as an optional speed-up. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/ravnir.md).
- [rea-jev](https://github.com/juan-viox/rea-jev) - Claude Code plugin that pairs REA (Reverse Engineer Anything) with TypeSafe Jev as a System 1 reflex layer — routing the target and workflow, gating risky REA calls, scoring evidence after tools and checking whether "done" was earned — plus a `jev` rank/classify/verify CLI; every hook fails open. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/rea-jev.md).
- [ReadyCode Reader](https://github.com/ReadycodeAI/readycode-reader) - Local PDF/DOCX/XLSX evidence for agents: TypeSafe Jev checks passages before cited answers (MCP + browser). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/readycode-reader.md).
- [reasoning-router](https://github.com/robertn702/reasoning-router) - Per-step reasoning effort for OpenCode, Pi and Codex CLI picked by a classifier such as TypeSafe Jev, keeping the prompt cache intact. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/reasoning-router.md).
- [Reddit product sentiment](https://github.com/STVPJ/reddit-sentiment) - Daily pipeline that collects product subreddit posts and comments, asks Jev typed questions about each comment and product pair (is it about this product, a five-level sentiment score, five aspects and nine themes), stores answers in SQLite, and shows trends with a drill-down to the source text. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/reddit-product-sentiment.md).
- [Redline](https://github.com/vvedantb/redline) - Visual PR review for Vite and Next.js apps: builds two commits, screenshots every page, and reports changed, new, broken, and removed pages; with a TypeSafe key, Jev picks the changed file most likely behind each visual change. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/redline.md).
- [reflex (Alertmanager router)](https://github.com/Starttoaster/reflex) - Alertmanager webhook that routes alert groups to urgent or info Discord channels with one TypeSafe Jev Choice (lab project). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/starttoaster-reflex.md).
- [Reflex (Datadog Labs)](https://github.com/datadog-labs/reflex) - Datadog Labs' Rust library for control loops on observability data: Datadog metrics and Toto forecasts become typed state, a judge such as TypeSafe Jev recommends an action (open, probe or leave a circuit), and Reflex's state machine applies it only if guards and invariants pass; includes a circuit-breaker simulator and a `reflex-typesafe` judge crate. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/datadog-reflex.md).
- [Reflex (kaustav1996)](https://github.com/kaustav1996/reflex) - Pi coding agent with TypeSafe Jev gates on tool calls, model-tier/skill routing, completion checks, and outside-result screening; code owns thresholds. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/kaustav1996-reflex.md).
- [Reflex (ursuciprian)](https://github.com/ursuciprian/reflex) - Pre-execution risk gate and prompt-injection guard for coding agents; optional TypeSafe Jev (≠ kaustav1996/reflex). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/ursuciprian-reflex.md).
- [Reflex System One](https://github.com/JRodrigoTech/reflex-system-one) - Windows-first, one-click local server for open decision models (Laya, Decider, Sol, Nox) behind a System One–style `/v1/systemone` API, with a portable runtime, model manager and console demo; independent of hosted TypeSafe Jev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/reflex-system-one.md).
- [Reflex-1](https://github.com/matu79go/reflex-1) - Reflex-1: 4B open decision model aiming for frontier-LLM accuracy at Jev speed (instant classification + latent reasoning); independent of hosted Jev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/matu79go-reflex-1.md).
- [reflex-router](https://github.com/ziyacivan/reflex-router) - Alpha loopback proxy for Claude Code: at the start of each piece of work a decision model (TypeSafe Jev by default, local Laya or TypeLLM) judges how much reasoning the task needs; in opt-in route mode cheap work goes to a cheaper model when it will not break the prompt cache, and every decision is recorded with signals of whether it looked wrong afterwards (`reflex report`). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/reflex-router.md).
- [refsure (browser judgment for coding agents)](https://github.com/leovvay/leovvayRefsure) - CLI and agent skill that coding agents in Orca terminals or next to an agent-browser session call to answer browser questions they often get wrong (which control means X, how to pass a banner, whether a click landed), using Jev decisions over a hidden page in the agent's own tab. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/refsure.md).
- [ReJev](https://github.com/Joe-rq/ReJev) - Independent Jev-style decision-model post-training reproduction on MiniCPM5-2B (stage 1 Choice classification) with sealed holdout eval. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/rejev.md).
- [REPLAY](https://github.com/balue8246-maker/replay) - macOS computer- and browser-use toolkit (Chinese docs, English summary) splitting work fast and slow: a slow model such as Opus plans, handles exceptions and approves risky actions, TypeSafe Jev (~0.5–1 s per judgment) decides each screen — what to look at, click or fill, and whether it's done — and code acts through Ego Lite for web, cua-driver and Peekaboo for apps and native dialogs, with Apple Vision OCR as fallback; recorded demonstrations become reusable task chains. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/replay-jev.md).
- [Repo Graph](https://github.com/fakoli/repo-graph) - Local repository architecture diagrams and semantic search packaged for Pi, Codex and Claude Code, with an optional, off-by-default TypeSafe Jev reranker that judges a shortlist in one bounded batched request. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/repo-graph.md).
- [RepoFinder](https://github.com/SelmanBoray/RepoFinder) - Codebase map and cited code search where TypeSafe Jev optionally reranks BM25 hits. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/repofinder.md).
- [Responsible AI Harness](https://github.com/syabdulr/responsible-ai-harness) - Model-agnostic safety assessment harness: hard rules plus optional TypeSafe Jev judge for injection, leakage, unsafe tools, and policy bypass; checksummed evidence + report UI. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/responsible-ai-harness.md).
- [Retrace](https://github.com/akbokha/retrace) - GitHub Action that keeps a ledger of every review thread on a pull request and, on each push, marks threads as likely fixed, partially fixed, still open or made worse using one Jev call per changed thread. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/retrace.md).
- [Reverie](https://github.com/r3c0nc1l3r/reverie) - Spec-driven browser test agent: Markdown specs are run step by step by a multimodal pilot while hosted TypeSafe Jev (default, via OpenRouter) or local Laya makes each click and keystroke, with a text model escalating when the decision engine is unsure, commit-button guards and run evidence under `.reverie/runs/`. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/reverie.md).
- [riff](https://github.com/scale-venture-partners/riff) - Prose linter with ruff-style rule codes: local static rules plus optional TypeSafe Jev judgment rules (`riff-lint` 0.1.0). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/riff.md).
- [RimeJev](https://github.com/IIIIOvOIIII/RimeJev) - Context-aware Chinese candidate recommendations for Squirrel (鼠须管) + Rime + Wanxiang on Apple Silicon macOS: asynchronously reorders native candidates using cloud TypeSafe Jev, a local model, or both, with per-app overrides and enhancement off by default. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/rimejev.md).
- [rippy](https://github.com/mpecan/rippy) - Rust shell-command safety hook for AI coding tools; a separate opt-in `rippy-jev` build can ask Jev to approve only commands rippy is unsure about, never ones that require human approval. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/rippy.md).
- [ripwire-broker (budgeted MCP code context with optional Jev relevance)](https://github.com/aquental/ripwire-broker) - Local MCP server that turns Ripwire's 33 verbs into three task-moment tools with a token budget, deduplication and provenance, plus hooks for Claude Code and Codex; an optional online mode lets Jev rate the files Ripwire ranked for a task. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/ripwire-broker.md).
- [RLCD Gateway](https://github.com/JimmyWesley/rlcd-gateway) - Self-hosted Go gateway for LLMs and Jev/open-rlcd System One with pruning, audit, and a live dashboard. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/rlcd-gateway.md).
- [robo-jev](https://github.com/STEERIX-home/robo-jev) - Robotics / embodied decision bench configs that include TypeSafe Jev (`~typesafe/jev-latest` and related ids) among decision-model baselines for evaluating fast typed policies. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/robo-jev.md).
- [Robotics research models (which robot models cs.RO papers use, with a Jev survey of architecture trends)](https://github.com/bkolligs/robotics-research-models) - Study of which robotics models appear in arXiv cs.RO papers since January 2023 and what share of papers name each release (each release its own row), following the Interconnects arXiv-mentions method, with a site of charts. Separate surveys use Jev to classify architectural trends in titles and abstracts and frontier-LLM and Jev usage; analysis runs on Modal. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/robotics-research-models.md).
- [rreia-skill](https://github.com/GabOnezio/rreia-skill) - RREIA agent skill bundling a local rreia-jev Rust decision asset and Jev-local references. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/rreia-skill.md).
- [RSI-Jev](https://github.com/Shanghua-Gao/RSI-Jev) - Recursively self-improving research loop that trains Jev-style System One (noul/choice/score) models and serves them at `/v1/systemone`. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/rsi-jev.md).
- [ruby_decision_model](https://github.com/obie/ruby_decision_model) - Stdlib-lean Ruby client for Noul/Choice/Score decision models via Typesafe or OpenRouter. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/ruby-decision-model.md).
- [ruby_llm-typesafe](https://github.com/kieranklaassen/ruby_llm-typesafe) - RubyLLM 2 provider for TypeSafe System One / Jev structured judgments (Choice/Noul/Score). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/ruby-llm-typesafe.md).
- [ruling](https://github.com/bradAGI/ruling) - Turns an open local chat model into a Jev-style decision engine served on `/v1/systemone`, with training, eval, and calibration commands; independent of TypeSafe. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/ruling.md).
- [RYOTIDE](https://github.com/csabag/ryotide) - Local Jev-style typed decisions from one LLM forward pass (MLX/PyTorch), measured on JevBench. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/ryotide.md).
- [s1 (s1-rs)](https://github.com/AbdelStark/s1-rs) - Rust derive layer for TypeSafe System One Choice/Score/Noul over optional `typesafe-rs` (distinct from typesafe-api). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/s1-rs.md).
- [S1-Core](https://github.com/acbcore-labs/s1-core) - Zero-dependency Python harness for System 1 decision models like Jev: choice/score/noul specs, fail-closed calibration gates, wave-parallel fan-out and a replayable audit ledger. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/s1-core.md).
- [s1-tui](https://github.com/tnaftali/s1-tui) - Terminal UI for System One typed decisions (noul/choice/score) over laya (local MLX) and TypeSafe Jev backends. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/s1-tui.md).
- [s1doom (System1Doom)](https://github.com/PanikParty/System1Doom) - Harness where text-only System One models (TypeSafe Jev or open-weight Laya) play DOOM through ViZDoom: code computes geometry, the model makes the judgment. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/system1doom.md).
- [s1flow (choose, ship and fix decision models such as Jev on your own labelled data, driven from your coding agent)](https://github.com/gotalab/s1flow) - CLI and agent skill for picking a decision model by measuring it: describe the decision in plain words, let your coding agent collect labelled cases, compare models such as Jev, D1, Clef and OpenAI Decisions on accuracy and coverage at a confidence threshold, and save the winner as a policy file your app loads with a small client. Includes a benchmark app with reports across 13 to 21 models and an optional CI check. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/s1flow.md).
- [s1kit (test, calibrate and route System One decision models like Jev on your own data)](https://github.com/Eliot5566/s1kit) - Python CLI and library for anyone choosing between decision models that speak `/v1/systemone` (Jev, Laya, kev, llama.cpp, Ollama): `s1kit test` runs one labelled suite against every model side by side, `s1kit calibrate` picks a per-question confidence cutoff that reaches your target precision, and `s1kit.Router` asks the cheapest model first and escalates only when it is unsure. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/s1kit.md).
- [safe-to-merge (GitHub Action and CLI where Jev judges whether a pull request is safe to merge)](https://github.com/Hiccup-za/safe-to-merge) - GitHub Action and Bun CLI that answers one question about a pull request: can it merge right now? Hard facts such as conflicts, failing checks and approvals are enforced in code; judgment calls such as risk, test adequacy, breaking changes, blocking comments and flaky CI go to Jev. Jev can raise concerns but never turn a hard-rule block into a pass. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/safe-to-merge.md).
- [SafeDecide](https://github.com/yotambraun/safedecide) - Certified automate/escalate thresholds for System One decision models (Jev, Clef, d3, Laya, …): pick the lowest confidence line that keeps automated error rate under a bound on labeled data, plus a 140M on-device model and adapters for `/v1/systemone`. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/safedecide.md).
- [sales-pipeline-revival-jev](https://github.com/developer943/sales-pipeline-revival-jev) - Open benchmark: TypeSafe Jev vs Gemini triage on 203 dead sales-deal email threads with published metrics (Salesgear companion). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/sales-pipeline-revival-jev.md).
- [samplepick (describe a sound in words and Jev ranks the samples in your own library)](https://github.com/tenplus1000/samplepick) - For music producers: type something like "a warm piano loop around 80 bpm" and Jev ranks the samples in your library from their file names, folder tags, pack, key and tempo (plus an optional short sound description from local listening analysis). You listen, tick the keepers, and they are copied into a folder ready for your DAW. Audio never leaves your machine and files are only copied, never moved. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/samplepick.md).
- [sbt-decision-filter (sbt plugin that asks Jev which test classes a change could affect and skips the rest)](https://github.com/krrrr38/sbt-decision-filter) - sbt plugin that asks a decision API (TypeSafe Jev or OpenAI Decisions) whether each test class could be affected by the current change and leaves out the ones that cannot via `Tests.Filter`. When in doubt (no key, API error, changed `build.sbt`) every test runs. Scoring questions follow mizchi/jev-test-filter. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/sbt-decision-filter.md).
- [scala-jev-sdk](https://github.com/ticofab/scala-jev-sdk) - Unofficial Scala 3.3 LTS System One client (sttp 4, no bundled effect system; Maven Central `scala-jev-sdk_3` 0.1.0, Apache-2.0). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/scala-jev-sdk.md).
- [Scientific Decision Evaluation](https://github.com/bydeng01/scientific-decision-eval) - Code and data for evaluating Jev and other models on scientific Choice decisions where deterministic code computes downstream counts, relations and claim labels, reporting semantic, downstream and final-label correctness separately. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/scientific-decision-eval.md).
- [Scope (code context selector)](https://github.com/tjeastmond/scope) - CLI that selects the smallest useful code context for a software task: static analysis shortlists up to 30 candidate chunks, TypeSafe Jev judges the relevance of each through `@typesafe-ai/sdk`, and TypeScript adds supporting declarations to return traceable chunks. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/tjeastmond-scope.md).
- [Screen Intelligence (desk)](https://github.com/zacfink/screen-intelligence) - macOS command-line "hands and eyes" for Claude Code: Claude writes a plan and Jev does the looking and clicking, using the accessibility tree first and a two-pass Box search on pixels second; 47 of 48 buttons found in about 0.6 s each, and it stops before send, submit, delete or pay. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/screen-intelligence-desk.md).
- [Scruple](https://github.com/NAlexPear/scruple) - Semantic-aware linter for enforcing team taste: an OXC parser selects candidate code, rules decide what to ask, and a decision provider — TypeSafe Jev by default (pinned to `jev-1.13.0`), or Cloudflare Clef, local Decider, self-hosted Kev or OpenAI Decisions — classifies each one into diagnostics that fail CI on errors. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/scruple.md).
- [Search Gateway](https://github.com/alvarovillalbaa/search-gateway) - Self-hostable gateway and SDK for 13 AI search providers (Exa, Perplexity, Tavily, Brave and more) with Jev-first auto routing that picks the provider and search breadth, falling back to OpenAI Decisions and then rules. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/search-gateway.md).
- [searxng-jev-mcp](https://github.com/arashbehmand/searxng-jev-mcp) - MCP server that gives coding agents one search tool over your own SearXNG: it cleans the query, picks vertical engines, merges duplicates, ranks with BM25F and can rerank results with a Jev-compatible decision model. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/searxng-jev-mcp.md).
- [second-brain (knowledge refresh skill)](https://github.com/strickland-inc/second-brain) - Claude Code agent skill that finds stale pages in a wiki, memory or rules folder, asks Jev whether each was superseded or duplicates another, moves outdated pages to `_archive/` and rebuilds the index Claude reads first. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/second-brain-refresh.md).
- [Sedum](https://github.com/sedum-dev/sedum) - Pre-alpha browser-testing framework: write Playwright tests in TypeScript with plain-English `ai(...)` steps, where TypeSafe Jev (default) resolves which element a sentence means and judges whether a claim holds on the page. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/sedum.md).
- [SelfJev](https://github.com/Jwuthri/SelfJev) - Open decisions model with a Jev-shaped typed API (yes/no, choice, score, multi) from forward-pass probabilities — Qwen3.5-4B + LoRA, one GPU; independent of hosted Jev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jwuthri-selfjev.md).
- [semantic-assert](https://github.com/mondaychen/semantic-assert) - Assert plain-English claims about captured UI/text state with TypeSafe Jev (Playwright helpers; thresholds in code). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/semantic-assert.md).
- [semantic-test-matcher](https://github.com/JustasMonkev/semantic-test-matcher) - TypeScript CLI (`rbt`) that ranks which test files to re-run for a changed source file: it profiles paths, code structure and diffs, then TypeSafe Jev judges each candidate test in batched requests, with a local answer cache, score blending, an OpenAI Decisions ranker option and a heuristics fallback. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/semantic-test-matcher.md).
- [Semble + Jev (semble-jev)](https://github.com/fatelei/semble-jev) - Local Semble retrieval + TypeSafe Jev relevance filter returning original source locations for agents (`sj`). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/semble-jev.md).
- [SemDecide](https://github.com/sharziki/semdecide) - Typed semantic decisions for Unix pipelines and CI with TypeSafe Jev predicates, routes, scores, and filters. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/semdecide.md).
- [semgate](https://github.com/m-mizutani/semgate) - Go net/http middlewares that ask TypeSafe Jev typed questions about each request, then allow, block, or route. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/semgate.md).
- [SemIf](https://github.com/TheoLeeCJ/SemIf) - Explores typed option scoring and shared-state reuse with local open models; independent of official Jev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/semif.md).
- [Sensibility](https://github.com/rajnandan1/sensibility) - Claude Code plugin that lets Claude ask Jev quick typed questions (does this diff match the ticket, is this command safe, did I finish) and turn the answers into an act, confirm or escalate verdict, with reusable question sets and optional risk and finish gates. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/sensibility.md).
- [sensored](https://github.com/atomicpages/sensored) - Streaming-first TypeScript PII redaction library (155 regex detectors, optional NER, Node/Bun/Deno/browser/edge) with opt-in Jev semantic confirmation (`semantic: { provider: "jev" }`) that asks TypeSafe Jev to confirm detected candidates before redacting. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/sensored.md).
- [Session Board](https://github.com/kolotovalexander/session-board) - Local macOS panel for Codex that turns a conversation into cards of open questions, tasks and answers, with opt-in TypeSafe Jev analysis (TypeSafe API or OpenRouter's Jev endpoint) to match messages to cards; listens only on localhost and documents what is stored and sent. Docs are in Russian. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/session-board.md).
- [sgrep](https://github.com/Lagnajit09/sgrep) - Semantic grep for codebases: chunk files and ask TypeSafe Jev which chunks match a plain-English query (includes offline mock). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/sgrep.md).
- [Shopify Jev Flow](https://github.com/gbesse/shopify-jev-flow) - Shopify Flow action that returns a typed Jev decision (finite route, calibrated confidence, explicit `reviewRequired`) instead of prose; the first example routes return requests to auto-approve, manual review or reject. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/shopify-jev-flow.md).
- [sieve](https://github.com/derwells/sieve) - Local MCP server that enumerates candidates and scores them with TypeSafe Jev (`jev_grep` / `jev_rank` / `jev_search`). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/sieve.md).
- [sift-light](https://github.com/lightsifter/sift-light) - Evidence-first search tool for coding agents (Pi, OMP, Claude Code/Codex via MCP) with exact, concept and hybrid modes and an optional Jev semantic judge that classifies retained concept candidates to improve ordering. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/sift-light.md).
- [similarity-ts-jev](https://github.com/kongyo2/similarity-ts-jev) - Runs similarity-ts and fallow on TypeScript, then keeps only the pairs TypeSafe Jev judges worth merging, each with the change to make (copy, derive, or extract), plus tools to calibrate the cutoff. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/similarity-ts-jev.md).
- [SiteEye](https://github.com/seominpapa/siteeye) - Construction-site near-miss prediction prototype: YOLO vision plus TypeSafe Jev 1.14 noul questions over detected statements, served as a local web app. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/siteeye.md).
- [Skill Authoring Lab](https://github.com/sebastienblanc/skill-authoring-lab) - Short loop for authoring a Quarkus MCP skill: edit, run one JUnit test, read a Jev-backed score. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/skill-authoring-lab.md).
- [Skill Dash](https://github.com/48Nauts-Operator/skill-dash) - Local dashboard that judges Claude Code/Codex skills with TypeSafe Jev (usefulness, redundancy, clarity, action) plus transcript evidence. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/skill-dash.md).
- [Skill Router (LangChain)](https://github.com/deyna256/langchain-skill-router) - Drop-in replacement for the deepagents `SkillsMiddleware` that picks which `SKILL.md` skills to load each turn with a pluggable judge; the included `JevJudge` adapter uses TypeSafe Jev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/langchain-skill-router.md).
- [skill-scanner](https://github.com/FrancoisChastel/skill-scanner) - Offline static analysis for Agent Skills (prompt-injection, exfil, hidden text) before install; optional TypeSafe Jev second opinion. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/skill-scanner.md).
- [skill-tester (eval harness for agent skills with headless agent runs, Jev judge packs and blind human review)](https://github.com/milesroxas/skill-tester) - Eval harness for agent skills: it generates outputs with headless coding agents (Claude Code, Cursor's agent), grades them with code checks and Jev judge packs, keeps runs and reports, and offers blind human review in a local web app. Jev is the judge for every pack. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/skill-tester.md).
- [Skillbox](https://github.com/kitze/skillbox) - Self-hosts a versioned agent skill library with optional Jev recommendations over authorized skills and explicit search fallback. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/skillbox.md).
- [skillmine](https://github.com/flaviomartil/skillmine) - Mines coding-agent sessions (Claude Code, Codex CLI, Kimi CLI, OpenCode, Antigravity) for reusable knowledge: local embeddings cluster near-duplicate windows, TypeSafe Jev (or Laya / Haiku) gates whether a cluster taught something reusable, and a planner model writes skills or runbooks with provenance and one-command undo. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/skillmine.md).
- [SkillRanker](https://github.com/Dicklesworthstone/skillranker) - Ranks agent skills for the next step from live session context using TypeSafe Jev, with offline demos and a Claude Code hook; MIT plus an OpenAI/Anthropic license rider. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/skillranker.md).
- [skimmer](https://github.com/dorkitude/skimmer) - Go CLI that splits pages, files or lists into chunks and asks Jev one yes/no relevance question per chunk, keeping only what helps with your goal to save agent tokens. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/skimmer.md).
- [skycmd (tiny System One decision models trained from scratch in Rust on a Mac)](https://github.com/maxgfr/skycmd) - Independent research repo that trains tiny System One-style decision models (s1 to s6, the smallest about 445k parameters and 251 KB ternary) from scratch in Rust with MLX on Apple silicon, answers `/v1/systemone`-shaped requests from GGUF files and plays four games live in the browser through WebAssembly. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/skycmd.md).
- [Skylos](https://github.com/duriantaco/skylos) - Local-first PR scanner for dead code, security bugs, and secrets with an optional Jev-only or Jev-plus-LLM review mode for dead-code findings (pinned `jev-1.13.0`). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/skylos.md).
- [SlidePilot](https://github.com/harshil1712/slidepilot) - Experimental Slidev addon that uses presenter voice, Cloudflare STT, and TypeSafe Jev to auto-advance when policy agrees. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/slidepilot.md).
- [SlingSearch (multi-provider web and scholarly search for agents, ranked by Jev by default)](https://github.com/sileod/slingsearch) - Ask a question and get ranked, source-backed excerpts with URLs, identifiers and provider provenance, with no generated answer. Several search providers run in parallel, results are deduplicated and fused, then ranked once against the question with Jev Latest as the default ranker (D1, Decider, Voyage, ensembles or plain fusion are alternatives). A relevance cutoff drops sources that would not help, and JSON output reports costs and timings. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/slingsearch.md).
- [slophound](https://github.com/JosXa/slophound) - Deterministic linter for prose written by language models (regex, grammar and statistics rules in TOML) where Jev resolves ambiguous findings in context — e.g. clearing a three-noun cluster that is an established term, or a semicolon splice that reads fine — and runs automatically once a TypeSafe key is configured. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/slophound.md).
- [SlopSearX](https://github.com/magnus919/SlopSearX) - Stateless, AI-agent-first SearXNG-compatible meta search engine (50 engines, MCP server) with optional TypeSafe Jev specialist-engine routing and bounded result reranking when `TYPESAFE_API_KEY` is set; deterministic routing/RRF order otherwise. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/slopsearx.md).
- [SmartMoney-Cub](https://github.com/myc0576/SmartMoney-Cub) - Read-only trading journal and review harness with optional TypeSafe Jev typed judgments and a frozen finance-jev offline benchmark (no orders). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/smartmoney-cub.md).
- [snake-ai (Snake learning lab)](https://github.com/markmysler/snake-ai) - Hands-on lab and nine-lesson course where neural networks learn Snake with a genetic algorithm or DQN, plus an untrained third player where Jev picks each move from a text description of what the snake senses. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/snake-ai-lab.md).
- [snap-vs-jev-swe](https://github.com/kprzybys/snap-vs-jev-swe) - Reproducible SWE benchmark of SubQ Snap, TypeSafe Jev and open decision models (file localization, long context, option order). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/snap-vs-jev-swe.md).
- [Snapif](https://github.com/snapif/snapif) - Rust crate and CLI that scores one agent tool call against a shipped policy and returns Auto, Review or Escalate, using TypeSafe Jev (`SNAPIF_BACKEND=typesafe`) or any compatible endpoint, with a shadow mode before enforcement. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/snapif.md).
- [Sneakoscope Codex (macOS control center and bounded workflows for Codex, with optional Jev decisions)](https://github.com/mandarange/sneakoscope-codex) - Local tool for the Codex CLI and desktop app (`sks`): a macOS menu-bar control center for connections, subagent models, MCP servers, image generation and diagnostics, bounded agent workflows, a checkable repository index and evidence checks. Optional Jev decisions, with cloud consent, choose subagent models and can pick parallel work when a task has independent parts. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/sneakoscope-codex.md).
- [Sniff Test](https://github.com/DanRWilloughby/snifftest) - Prose linter with free local countable rules and optional TypeSafe Jev judgment rules after confirmation. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/snifftest.md).
- [Social Risk · Qwen3-VL + Jev](https://github.com/TianJinWeiBoss520/social-risk-qwen-jev) - Concluded research project (Chinese, with English README) on multimodal risk moderation for Chinese social posts (LT-EDI Chinese misogyny memes): Qwen3-VL with a language-side LoRA extracts structured evidence JSON, TypeSafe Jev makes the policy decision, and a frozen 170-item test leaderboard compares 13 methods — where a TF-IDF + logistic-regression baseline ranked first — with pure-Jev vs multimodal+Jev ablations and CPU regression tests. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/social-risk-qwen-jev.md).
- [Soenneker.TypesafeAi.OpenApiClient (generated .NET client for the TypeSafe API)](https://github.com/soenneker/soenneker.typesafeai.openapiclient) - .NET client generated from TypeSafe's OpenAPI document, published on NuGet, covering model listing and `POST /v1/systemone`. Part of the maintainer's large family of generated API clients. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/soenneker-typesafeai-openapiclient.md).
- [soft-lint](https://github.com/c9r-dev/plugins) - Claude Code plugin and npm CLI that asks a System One classifier — TypeSafe Jev by default, or Jev / Clef on Cloudflare Workers AI — plain-English yes/no rules about the lines a diff adds (comments that restate code, a getter that writes, swallowed errors) and reports each 'yes' over the rule's cutoff; an incomplete check exits 2, never a silent pass. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/soft-lint.md).
- [sokudan](https://github.com/hiroki-abe-58/sokudan) - Japanese System One decision model (Jev-style): typed answers and probabilities in one forward pass, no text generation; bench_ja/bench_en and Laya bias repro. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/hiroki-abe-58-sokudan.md).
- [SOLO](https://github.com/0814wdwd/solo_jev) - Layout optimizer that reorders table rows and fields so per-row decision-model judgements reuse the prefix cache. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/solo-jev.md).
- [sommelier](https://github.com/wide3751/sommelier) - Claude Code plugin (Chinese and English READMEs) that has Jev judge reasoning depth per request and picks model and effort, upgrading freely but downgrading cautiously for cache reasons. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/sommelier.md).
- [sony-pdf-repository-tools (repair, OCR and Jev relevance filtering for a 95,000-document Sony manual dump)](https://github.com/ArclightAutomata/sony-pdf-repository-tools) - Scripts behind the EveryCRT wiki's use of the Sony PDF Repository: they repair PDFs truncated at 1 MiB, index and OCR the dump, and use Jev as a first-pass yes/no filter for CRT and video-gear manuals before a local LLM labels each kept document; includes a 400-document hand-labelled evaluation. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/sony-pdf-repository-tools.md).
- [SoulsBench](https://github.com/AWoLnik/SoulsBench) - Dark Souls III realtime combat benchmark for decision models: TypeSafe Jev, OpenAI Decisions, Perplexity's decider and local Kev / Laya each fight Iudex Gundyr through the same harness, state, 16 actions and 200 ms budget on the live game; the author's 50-seed results table reports wins, damage, latency and cost per fight. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/soulsbench.md).
- [Spam → Lead Gen Pipeline](https://github.com/automatewithuday/spam-leadgen-pipeline) - Mines your Gmail spam folder for ICP-fit senders: Jev classifies and scores each email, then the pipeline audits the sender's email infrastructure and drafts (never sends) a reverse pitch. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/spam-leadgen-pipeline.md).
- [SpamGuard (tb-spam-guard)](https://github.com/Helicon1968/tb-spam-guard) - Thunderbird add-on that flags phishing mail impersonating Japanese organizations with header rules, asking TypeSafe Jev for a second opinion only on borderline messages (Japanese UI).
